A

The rules and how they grew规则及其演变

What the controls forbid and who they bind, the 2022→2026 timeline, the TPP / density / bandwidth thresholds, the Foreign Direct Product Rule, "reason to know", the shelved Diffusion rule, is-informed letters, license exceptions and model-weight controls.

管制禁止什么、约束谁，2022→2026 年的时间线，TPP／密度／带宽门槛，外国直接产品规则，"理应知道"，被搁置的扩散规则，告知函，许可例外，以及模型权重管制。

A1 What the controls forbid, and who they bind — only US companies, or mine too?管制禁止什么、约束谁——只约束美国公司，还是也约束我？ basic基础

They forbid selling advanced AI chips (and the gear and know-how to make them) to China, Hong Kong and Macau without a US license. They bind far more than US companies: any chip that is US-origin, or made abroad with enough US technology, carries US rules with it — so a Singapore or Hong Kong reseller is bound too.

它们禁止在未获美国许可证的情况下，向中国内地、香港和澳门出售先进 AI 芯片（以及制造这些芯片的设备与技术）。其约束范围远不止美国公司：任何美国原产的芯片，或在境外以足够多美国技术制造的芯片，都随身带着美国规则——因此新加坡或香港的转售商同样受约束。

The rules are the US Export Administration Regulations (EAR), run by the Commerce Department's Bureau of Industry and Security (BIS). A chip at or above the October 2023 performance line (export classification ECCN 3A090 / 4A090) needs a license to go to China, Hong Kong or Macau, and to any company headquartered in China or Macau wherever that company sits. US rules follow the item, not the company's nationality: they attach to US-origin chips anywhere in the world and, through the Foreign Direct Product Rule, to foreign-made chips built with US tools or designs. A non-US firm that exports, re-exports, or "causes or aids" a violation can be penalized under the EAR even without touching US soil.

相关规则是由美国商务部工业与安全局（BIS）执行的《出口管理条例》（EAR）。达到或超过 2023 年 10 月性能线的芯片（出口管制分类编码 ECCN 3A090 / 4A090），出口至中国内地、香港或澳门，以及出口给任何总部位于中国或澳门的公司（无论该公司身处何地），都需要许可证。美国规则跟着物项走，而非公司国籍：它附着于全球任何地方的美国原产芯片，并通过外国直接产品规则，附着于使用美国工具或设计制造的外国芯片。非美国企业若出口、再出口或"促成或协助"违规，即便从未踏足美国，也可依 EAR 受罚。

Penalties: criminal, up to $1,000,000 and 20 years per violation; civil, the greater of $374,474 per violation or twice the transaction value, plus loss of export privileges.

处罚：刑事方面，每项违规最高 100 万美元罚款、20 年监禁；民事方面，取每项违规 374,474 美元或交易额两倍中的较高者，并可吊销出口权。

Sources来源3
  • 88 FR 73458 · Oct 2023 rule2023 年 10 月规则 · 25 Oct 2023
  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026
  • 50 U.S.C. 4819 · penalties处罚条款
A2 Why does the US care, and will it loosen or tighten?美国为何在意，管制会放松还是收紧？ basic基础

The US wants to keep China from training and running the most advanced AI (which has military and intelligence uses) on cutting-edge American chips. Direction over the next two years is mixed: a late-2025 trade truce loosened a little (H200 allowed case-by-case), but Blackwell stays banned and Congress is pushing tighter rules.

美国希望阻止中国利用最先进的美国芯片训练和运行最先进的 AI（具有军事和情报用途）。未来两年的走向喜忧参半：2025 年末的贸易休战略有放松（H200 获准逐案审查），但 Blackwell 仍遭禁止，国会正在推动更严格的规则。

The stated goal is national security: the controls target chips that would give China an edge in frontier AI with military, surveillance or weapons applications, and the equipment to make such chips domestically. The trend from 2022 to early 2025 was steady tightening. Since mid-2025 it has split: the Trump administration allowed H20 (Aug 2025) and then H200-class chips (Jan 2026) under conditions and a revenue cut, as part of a US–China truce; but it kept the most capable Blackwell chips (B200/B300/B30A) banned, and Congress has bills that would tighten cloud access and mandate chip location-tracking. Net: the floor has been raised modestly for mid-tier chips, the ceiling stays shut, and the political risk is toward re-tightening if the truce frays.

官方目标是国家安全：管制针对的是那些可能让中国在具有军事、监控或武器用途的前沿 AI 上取得优势的芯片，以及在本土制造此类芯片的设备。2022 年至 2025 年初的趋势是持续收紧。2025 年年中以来走向分化：特朗普政府作为美中休战的一部分，在附加条件和收入分成下允许了 H20（2025 年 8 月），继而允许 H200 级芯片（2026 年 1 月）；但仍禁止能力最强的 Blackwell 芯片（B200/B300/B30A），且国会已有法案拟收紧云端访问并强制芯片定位追踪。总体而言：中端芯片的下限已略有抬高，上限仍然关闭，而一旦休战破裂，政治风险偏向重新收紧。

Sources来源3

A3 The main rule changes, 2022 → Oct 2026主要规则变化，2022 年 → 2026 年 10 月 detail细节

Six rounds that matter: the 2022 ban, the 2023 reset of the thresholds, the 2024 add-ons (HBM + tool-made-abroad rule), the 2025 Diffusion rule (issued then shelved), the April 2025 H20 letters, and the Jan 2026 H200 opening with a 25% tariff.

六个关键轮次：2022 年的禁令、2023 年门槛重置、2024 年的补充规则（HBM 管制 + 境外制造工具规则）、2025 年的扩散规则（发布后即搁置）、2025 年 4 月的 H20 告知函，以及 2026 年 1 月带 25% 关税的 H200 放行。

Timeline, oldest first:

时间线，由早至今：

Date日期 What it did作用
7 Oct 2022 (87 FR 62186)First real controls. Created ECCNs 3A090/4A090 and "supercomputer" end-use rules for China. Caught the A100 and H100.首批实质管制。设立 ECCN 3A090/4A090 及针对中国的"超级计算机"最终用途规则。A100、H100 被纳入。
17 Oct 2023 (88 FR 73458)Reset the thresholds still in force. Added the license requirement for companies headquartered in China/Macau anywhere (§744.23(a)(3)). Caught the A800/H800 workaround parts and the RTX 4090.重置了至今仍有效的门槛。新增对总部位于中国/澳门的公司（无论身处何地）的许可要求（§744.23(a)(3)）。纳入 A800/H800 规避版与 RTX 4090。
2 Dec 2024 (89 FR 96790)Added 3A090.c, the HBM control; 24 tool types; 140 Entity List additions; expanded the Foreign Direct Product Rule.新增 3A090.c，即 HBM 管制；24 类设备；实体清单新增 140 家；扩大外国直接产品规则。
13–16 Jan 2025The AI Diffusion Rule (90 FR 4544): a worldwide, three-tier license scheme plus a control on AI model weights (4E091). The "foundry rule" (90 FR 5298): due diligence for chipmakers/packagers.AI 扩散规则（90 FR 4544）：全球三级许可方案，并新增对 AI 模型权重的管制（4E091）。"代工厂规则"（90 FR 5298）：芯片制造商/封装商的尽职调查义务。
Apr 2025BIS "is-informed" letters to Nvidia and AMD: the H20 and AMD MI308 now need a license.BIS 向英伟达和 AMD 发出"告知函"：H20 与 AMD MI308 此后需要许可证。
Aug 2025H20/MI308 licenses begin, with an informal 15% revenue share to the US government.H20/MI308 许可开始发放，附带向美国政府非正式缴纳 15% 收入分成。
Sep–Nov 2025The Affiliates (50%) Rule (90 FR 47201) extends Entity List limits to 50%-owned affiliates; suspended 10 Nov 2025 for one year (90 FR 50857).关联方（50%）规则（90 FR 47201）将实体清单限制扩大至 50% 持股的关联企业；于 2025 年 11 月 10 日起暂停一年（90 FR 50857）。
13–15 Jan 2026The H200 opening (91 FR 1684): case-by-case review for chips under the H200 line, exported from the US, plus a 25% tariff (Proclamation 11002).H200 放行（91 FR 1684）：对低于 H200 线、自美国出口的芯片逐案审查，并加征 25% 关税（第 11002 号公告）。
13 Mar 2026A draft rule to replace the Diffusion Rule was withdrawn; no replacement published.一项拟取代扩散规则的草案被撤回；未发布替代规则。
31 May 2026BIS guidance confirming the Nov 2023 China-HQ license requirement still applies in every country.BIS 指引确认：2023 年 11 月的"中国总部"许可要求在任何国家仍然适用。
10 Jul 2026 (91 FR 43034)UAE moved to favorable Group A:5 (G42, Core42 and 8 US hyperscalers get license-free chips).阿联酋升入优惠的 A:5 组（G42、Core42 及 8 家美国云厂商可免许可获取芯片）。

Direction of travel: tighten (2022–early 2025), then a selective loosening of mid-tier chips under a trade truce (mid 2025–2026), with the top tier (Blackwell) still shut.

走向：先收紧（2022—2025 年初），再在贸易休战下对中端芯片选择性放松（2025 年中—2026 年），而顶级芯片（Blackwell）仍然关闭。

A4 The newest rule change as of October 2026, and when it took effect截至 2026 年 10 月最新的规则变化，及其生效时间 basic基础

The most recent rule is the H200 opening (effective 15 Jan 2026) with its 25% tariff. The most recent guidance is the 31 May 2026 BIS note confirming the China-HQ license rule, and the 10 Jul 2026 UAE upgrade. No new chip rule has been published since.

最新的规则是 H200 放行（2026 年 1 月 15 日生效）及其 25% 关税。最新的指引是 2026 年 5 月 31 日确认"中国总部"许可规则的 BIS 通知，以及 2026 年 7 月 10 日的阿联酋升级。此后未再发布新的芯片规则。

91 FR 1684 (eff. 15 Jan 2026) moved H200-class chips from presumption-of-denial to case-by-case review for US exports to China/Macau; Proclamation 11002 (eff. 15 Jan 2026) added the 25% Section 232 tariff. After that, the significant developments were guidance and administration, not new chip rules: the 31 May 2026 BIS guidance (China-HQ rule still in force) and the 10 Jul 2026 UAE A:5 upgrade. A draft replacement for the Diffusion Rule was withdrawn in March 2026. Pending on the calendar: the Affiliates Rule suspension expires on paper 10 Nov 2026 (no extension found in the Federal Register as of 27 Oct 2026), and China's draft AI-tech export controls close comments 15 Nov 2026.

91 FR 1684（2026 年 1 月 15 日生效）将 H200 级芯片对华/澳门的美国出口从推定拒绝改为逐案审查；第 11002 号公告（2026 年 1 月 15 日生效）加征 25% 的第 232 条关税。此后的重要进展是指引与行政执行，而非新的芯片规则：2026 年 5 月 31 日的 BIS 指引（"中国总部"规则仍然有效）以及 2026 年 7 月 10 日阿联酋升入 A:5 组。一项拟取代扩散规则的草案已于 2026 年 3 月撤回。日程上待定事项：关联方规则的暂停在书面上于 2026 年 11 月 10 日到期（截至 2026 年 10 月 27 日，《联邦公报》中未见延期），中国的 AI 技术出口管制草案于 2026 年 11 月 15 日结束征求意见。

A5 The performance thresholds (TPP, density, bandwidth), and how to tell if a chip is over the line性能门槛（TPP、密度、带宽），以及如何判断一款芯片是否越线 detail细节

A chip is controlled (ECCN 3A090) if its compute score (TPP) is high enough, or if it is a dense data-center chip in a mid band. A separate rule controls high-bandwidth memory. Above a second, higher line (the "H200 line") a chip is effectively banned; below it, it can be licensed case-by-case.

若一款芯片的算力分数（TPP）足够高，或它是处于中段区间的高密度数据中心芯片，则受管制（出口管制分类编码（ECCN）3A090）。另有一条单独规则管制高带宽内存。在第二条更高的线（即"H200 线"）之上，芯片实际上被禁止；在该线之下，可逐案审查获得许可证。

TPP (Total Processing Performance) = dense TOPS × bit length — the chip's peak operations per second times the number size it uses. The 2023 thresholds, still in force:

总处理性能（TPP）= 稠密 TOPS × 位长——即芯片每秒峰值运算次数乘以其所用的数字位宽。2023 年的门槛，至今仍然有效：

  • 3A090.a = TPP ≥ 4,800; or TPP ≥ 1,600 with performance density ≥ 5.92 (TPP per square mm).3A090.a = TPP ≥ 4,800；或 TPP ≥ 1,600 且性能密度 ≥ 5.92（每平方毫米的 TPP）。
  • 3A090.b = data-center chips with TPP 2,400–4,800 and density 1.6–5.92.3A090.b = TPP 为 2,400–4,800、密度为 1.6–5.92 的数据中心芯片。
  • 3A090.c (added Dec 2024) = HBM with memory-bandwidth density > 2 GB/s/mm².3A090.c（2024 年 12 月新增）= 内存带宽密度 > 2 GB/s/mm² 的 HBM。

How TPP and density interact: the first prong (TPP ≥ 4,800) catches any big chip outright. The density prongs are a second net — they catch a chip that keeps its total TPP down but packs a lot of compute into a small, efficient die (the kind you could cluster), and the .b band catches mid-size data-center parts specifically. So a maker can't simply shave peak TPP to escape; density and the data-center label can still pull it in.

TPP 与密度如何相互作用：第一项（TPP ≥ 4,800）直接纳入任何大芯片。密度各项是第二张网——它们纳入那些把总 TPP 压低、却在小而高效的裸片上塞入大量算力（即可成簇部署的那种）的芯片，而 .b 区间专门纳入中等规模的数据中心部件。因此厂商无法仅靠削减峰值 TPP 来规避；密度和"数据中心"标签仍可将其纳入。

The second, higher line (91 FR 1684, Jan 2026): a 3A090 chip with TPP < 21,000 AND total DRAM bandwidth < 6,500 GB/s (e.g., Nvidia H200, AMD MI325X) gets case-by-case review for US exports to China/Macau; anything above either number stays presumption of denial. Bandwidth settles most current chips: 8 TB/s parts (B200, B300, MI355X) fail; H200 (4.8 TB/s) and MI325X (6.0 TB/s) pass.

第二条更高的线（91 FR 1684，2026 年 1 月）：一款 3A090 芯片若 TPP < 21,000 且 DRAM 总带宽 < 6,500 GB/s（例如英伟达 H200、AMD MI325X），对华/澳门的美国出口可获逐案审查；任一数值超标则维持推定拒绝。带宽决定了当前大多数芯片：8 TB/s 的部件（B200、B300、MI355X）不通过；H200（4.8 TB/s）与 MI325X（6.0 TB/s）通过。

Chips that sit just under 3A090.a (TPP < 4,800) and so are uncontrolled by the formula: RTX 4090D (TPP ≈ 4,708), RTX 5090D (≈ 4,750), and the China SKUs RTX PRO 6000D / 5500 (designed below 4,800). The H20 is below 3A090 on specs (TPP ≈ 2,368) but is caught anyway by an is-informed letter.

刚好低于 3A090.a（TPP < 4,800）、因而按公式不受管制的芯片：RTX 4090D（TPP ≈ 4,708）、RTX 5090D（≈ 4,750），以及中国专供型号 RTX PRO 6000D / 5500（设计上低于 4,800）。H20 在规格上低于 3A090（TPP ≈ 2,368），但仍被一封告知函纳入。

How to check a chip: (1) compute TPP from its dense TOPS × bit length and compare to 4,800 / the density prongs; (2) if it's a data-center part, check the 2,400–4,800 band and the HBM rule; (3) check whether it (or its bandwidth/interconnect) is named in an is-informed letter; (4) get the maker's ECCN in writing before relying on "uncontrolled."

如何核查一款芯片：(1) 由其稠密 TOPS × 位长算出 TPP，与 4,800／密度各项比较；(2) 若为数据中心部件，核查 2,400–4,800 区间及 HBM 规则；(3) 核查它（或其带宽/互连）是否被某封告知函点名；(4) 在依赖"不受管制"之前，取得厂商书面出具的 ECCN。

A6 What does HBM memory have to do with the export rules?HBM 内存与出口规则有何关系？ basic基础

High-bandwidth memory (HBM) is the fast, stacked memory that lets an AI chip feed its cores. Since December 2024 the US controls HBM itself, because without it a Chinese-made chip can't reach frontier performance.

高带宽内存（HBM）是让 AI 芯片为其计算核心供数的高速堆叠式内存。自 2024 年 12 月起，美国对 HBM 本身实施管制，因为没有它，中国制造的芯片无法达到前沿性能。

The 2 Dec 2024 rule added ECCN 3A090.c, controlling HBM with memory-bandwidth density > 2 GB/s/mm² — i.e., essentially all modern HBM used in AI accelerators. The point is to choke a bottleneck: a GPU's usable AI speed is limited as much by how fast it can read memory as by its raw math, and China cannot yet make leading-edge HBM at scale. Note: NAC/ACA license exceptions explicitly do not cover 3A090.c, so HBM is tightly held.

2024 年 12 月 2 日的规则新增了出口管制分类编码（ECCN）3A090.c，管制内存带宽密度 > 2 GB/s/mm² 的 HBM——即几乎所有用于 AI 加速器的现代 HBM。其用意是扼住瓶颈：GPU 可用的 AI 速度既受制于其原始算力，也同样受制于读取内存的速度，而中国尚无法规模化制造最前沿的 HBM。注意：NAC/ACA 许可例外明确不涵盖 3A090.c，因此 HBM 被严格管控。

Sources来源3
  • 89 FR 96790 · 5 Dec 2024
  • computelaw.blog
  • 15 CFR 740.8 · NAC/ACA exclude 3A090.cNAC/ACA 不含 3A090.c
A7 Which EAR provisions control AI chips today, and where is the consolidated text?如今哪些 EAR 条款管制 AI 芯片，合并文本在哪里？ basic基础

The core classifications are ECCN 3A090 (the chips) and 4A090 (computers/boards containing them), plus related ".z" items. The destination license requirement sits in §742.6 / §744.23, with License Exceptions NAC and ACA in §740.8. The consolidated text is the eCFR, Title 15, Part 774 (the Commerce Control List) and Part 740/742/744.

核心分类是 出口管制分类编码（ECCN）3A090（芯片）与 4A090（含芯片的计算机/板卡），以及相关的 ".z" 项。目的地许可要求位于 §742.6 / §744.23，许可例外 NAC 与 ACA 位于 §740.8。合并文本见 eCFR 第 15 篇、第 774 部分（商业管制清单）及第 740/742/744 部分。

For a chip at the 3A090 line going to China/Macau, the operative license requirement is §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) (destination control for ".a" items) and §744.23(a)(3) (the end-user control for China/Macau-headquartered parties anywhere). The Foreign Direct Product reach is §734.9. License Exceptions NAC and ACA are §740.8. The H200 case-by-case policy and its nine conditions are in 91 FR 1684 (amending §742.6(b)(10) and Supp. 2 to Part 748, ¶(dd)). Current authoritative text: eCFR Title 15 (updated continuously). The Diffusion Rule's additions (model weights 4E091, Exceptions AIA/ACM/LPP) are on the books but not enforced.

对于处于 3A090 线、运往中国/澳门的芯片，适用的许可要求是 §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A)（针对 ".a" 项的目的地管制）与 §744.23(a)(3)（针对任何地方总部位于中国/澳门主体的最终用户管制）。外国直接产品规则的适用范围见 §734.9。许可例外 NAC 与 ACA 见 §740.8。H200 逐案审查政策及其九项条件见 91 FR 1684（修订 §742.6(b)(10) 及第 748 部分附录 2 第 (dd) 段）。当前权威文本：eCFR 第 15 篇（持续更新）。扩散规则的新增内容（模型权重 4E091、例外 AIA/ACM/LPP）虽在册，但未被执行。

Sources来源3
  • BIS guidance · names §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) and §744.23(a)(3)点名 §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) 与 §744.23(a)(3) · 31 May 2026

  • 91 FR 1684 · 15 Jan 2026
A8 A chip below the 3A090 line (H20-class, B30-class): fully EAR99, or still "subject to the EAR"?低于 3A090 线的芯片（H20 级、B30 级）：完全属于 EAR99，还是仍"受 EAR 管辖"？ basic基础

Still subject to the EAR. A below-threshold chip usually needs no license for a normal commercial buyer in China, but it is not free of all US rules: the Entity List, military/military-intelligence end-use, WMD and anti-diversion rules still apply, and the US can pull it under control overnight with an is-informed letter.

仍受《出口管理条例》（EAR）管辖。低于门槛的芯片对中国普通商业买家通常无需许可证，但并非不受任何美国规则约束：实体清单、军事／军事情报最终用途、大规模杀伤性武器及反转移规则仍然适用，而美国可凭一封告知函在一夜之间将其纳入管制。

"Below 3A090" and "not named in a letter" means no classification-based license requirement to China for an ordinary buyer. But the item remains "subject to the EAR," so: (1) you cannot sell to an Entity List party (and, if the Affiliates Rule returns 10 Nov 2026, to any company ≥50%-owned by one); (2) no military or military-intelligence end use; (3) no knowledge of diversion. And the H20 is the cautionary tale — it is below 3A090 on specs, yet the US made it license-required by letter in April 2025. So "uncontrolled" is a status the US can revoke without a rulemaking. Get the ECCN in writing from the maker before relying on it.

"低于 3A090"且"未被告知函点名"意味着对普通买家而言，对华出口没有基于分类的许可要求。但该物项仍"受 EAR 管辖"，因此：(1) 不得向实体清单主体出售（若关联方规则于 2026 年 11 月 10 日恢复，则不得向任何被其 ≥50% 持股的公司出售）；(2) 不得用于军事或军事情报最终用途；(3) 不得明知存在转移。H20 即是前车之鉴——它在规格上低于 3A090，美国却仍于 2025 年 4 月以告知函使其需要许可证。因此"不受管制"是一种美国无需经过规则制定即可撤销的状态。在依赖之前，应取得厂商书面出具的 ECCN。

Sources来源3
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • 88 FR 73458 · 25 Oct 2023
  • NVDA 10-Q · H20 letterH20 告知函 · 26 Aug 2026
A9 What is the status of the Jan 2025 AI Diffusion Rule?2025 年 1 月 AI 扩散规则的现状如何？ detail细节

It is on the books but not enforced, and operating as if rescinded. BIS announced non-enforcement on 13 May 2025; a formal rescission was never published, and the planned replacement rule was withdrawn in March 2026. Its three-tier worldwide scheme, its model-weights control, and its new license exceptions do not bind clients in practice.

它在册但未被执行，实际运作如同已被撤销。BIS 于 2025 年 5 月 13 日宣布不予执行；正式撤销从未发布，而计划中的替代规则已于 2026 年 3 月撤回。其全球三级方案、模型权重管制及新的许可例外，在实践中并不约束客户。

The Diffusion Rule (90 FR 4544, effective 13 Jan 2025, compliance 15 May 2025) would have imposed a worldwide, three-tier license framework, a control on AI model weights (ECCN 4E091), and new exceptions (AIA, ACM, LPP). On 13 May 2025 BIS directed staff not to enforce it and said it would rescind and replace it. Key wrinkle: the GAO ruled on 12 May 2026 that BIS's non-enforcement "Press Release" is itself a rule under the Congressional Review Act that BIS never properly submitted to Congress — a procedural irregularity that, per the law firms, does not change the practical status quo: the Diffusion Rule's new requirements are suspended, and parties fall back to the pre-Diffusion (Nov 2023) controls. For a Tier-2 country client (e.g., an ordinary Singapore or UAE buyer): the Diffusion Rule's caps do not bind you; what binds you is the Nov 2023 framework — a license for 3A090 to China/Macau and for China/Macau-HQ entities, plus end-user/diversion rules. The replacement rule drafted in 2026 (compute-scaled worldwide licenses) was withdrawn 13 Mar 2026; nothing has replaced it.

扩散规则（90 FR 4544，2025 年 1 月 13 日生效，2025 年 5 月 15 日合规）本应施加一套全球三级许可框架、对 AI 模型权重的管制（出口管制分类编码（ECCN）4E091）以及新的例外（AIA、ACM、LPP）。2025 年 5 月 13 日，BIS 指示工作人员不予执行，并表示将撤销并取而代之。关键曲折：GAO 于 2026 年 5 月 12 日裁定，BIS 的不予执行"新闻稿"本身就是《国会审查法》下的一项规则，而 BIS 从未按程序提交国会——据各律所称，这一程序瑕疵并不改变实际现状：扩散规则的新要求处于暂停状态，各方退回到扩散规则之前（2023 年 11 月）的管制。对第二级国家的客户（例如普通的新加坡或阿联酋买家）而言：扩散规则的上限并不约束你；约束你的是 2023 年 11 月的框架——对华/澳门以及对总部位于中国/澳门主体出口 3A090 需许可证，另加最终用户／转移规则。2026 年起草的替代规则（按算力分级的全球许可）已于 2026 年 3 月 13 日撤回；没有任何规则取而代之。

Sources来源5

A10 After rescission, what section actually carries the China/Macau license requirement for 3A090?撤销之后，究竟哪一条款承载对华/澳门 3A090 的许可要求？ expert专家

It rests on the pre-Diffusion controls: §744.23(a)(3) (end-user control for China/Macau-HQ parties) and §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) (the destination control for ".a" items). BIS confirmed this in writing on 31 May 2026. The governing citation is the Nov 2023 rule, 88 FR 73458, as clarified by the 31 May 2026 guidance.

它依托于扩散规则之前的管制：§744.23(a)(3)（针对总部位于中国/澳门主体的最终用户管制）与 §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A)（针对 ".a" 项的目的地管制）。BIS 已于 2026 年 5 月 31 日书面确认此点。管辖性引据是 2023 年 11 月的规则 88 FR 73458，并由 2026 年 5 月 31 日的指引予以澄清。

The Diffusion Rule had moved the requirement for the more sensitive ".a" items out of §744.23(a) and into a new worldwide §742.6 control. When BIS stopped enforcing the Diffusion Rule, that "effectively meant a return to the end-user control license requirement" of §744.23(a)(3) for China/Macau-HQ entities, while the ".b" items' requirement stayed in §744.23(a)(3)(i). The 31 May 2026 guidance states the license requirement "continues to apply under §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A)… to all destinations outside the United States for these advanced computing items when such items are for entities headquartered in… Country Group D:5 or Macau." So: no separate new rule — it is the Nov 2023 framework (88 FR 73458), confirmed by the May 2026 guidance; the H200 case-by-case review policy overlay is 91 FR 1684. An "is-informed" letter can still supplement this.

扩散规则曾将较敏感的 ".a" 项的要求从 §744.23(a) 移出，纳入一项新的全球性 §742.6 管制。当 BIS 停止执行扩散规则后，这"实际上意味着回到"针对总部位于中国/澳门主体的 §744.23(a)(3) 最终用户管制许可要求，而 ".b" 项的要求仍留在 §744.23(a)(3)(i)。2026 年 5 月 31 日的指引称，该许可要求"在这些先进计算物项系供总部位于……国家组 D:5 或澳门的主体时，继续依 §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A)……适用于美国境外的所有目的地"。因此：并无单独的新规则——它就是 2023 年 11 月的框架（88 FR 73458），并由 2026 年 5 月的指引确认；H200 逐案审查审查政策的叠加层是 91 FR 1684。一封"告知函"仍可对此加以补充。

Sources来源3
  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026
  • Baker McKenzie · 8 Jun 2026
  • 88 FR 73458 · 25 Oct 2023
A11 "Is-informed" letters — do they still get used, and can a letter you never saw bind you?"告知函"——它们仍在使用吗？一封你从未见过的信能约束你吗？ expert专家

Yes on both counts. BIS still uses individual letters to impose a license requirement on a specific part (the H20 and AMD MI308 were done this way in April 2025). And a letter sent to the US supplier can effectively bind the downstream chain, because once the item is license-required, everyone handling it is subject to that requirement.

两点皆是。BIS 仍以个别信函对特定部件施加许可要求（H20 与 AMD MI308 即于 2025 年 4 月如此处理）。而寄给美国供应商的一封信可实际约束下游链条，因为一旦该物项需要许可证，经手它的每一方都须遵守该要求。

In April 2025 BIS sent Nvidia and AMD letters requiring a license for the H20 "and any other circuits achieving the H20's memory bandwidth, interconnect bandwidth, or combination thereof," and for AMD's MI308, to China (incl. HK/Macau), D:5, and China/Macau-HQ companies. These letters were never published as a rule — they are known only through the companies' SEC filings. The mechanism (the §744.21/§744.23 "is-informed" style) lets BIS act fast and chip-specific. On the "letter you never received" point: the letter creates a license requirement on the item; under the EAR a downstream party that exports/re-exports a now-controlled item without a license violates the rule regardless of whether it personally received a copy — the obligation attaches to the goods, and a distributor who "knows or has reason to know" of the requirement is exposed. Practical upshot for a reseller: a chip you bought as "uncontrolled" can become license-required by a letter you'll only learn about from the maker or the press.

2025 年 4 月，BIS 向英伟达和 AMD 发出信函，要求对 H20"以及任何达到 H20 的内存带宽、互连带宽或两者组合的其他电路"，以及对 AMD 的 MI308，向中国（含香港/澳门）、D:5 及总部位于中国/澳门的公司出口时需要许可证。这些信函从未作为规则发布——只能通过两家公司的 SEC 备案得知。该机制（§744.21/§744.23 的"告知函"形式）让 BIS 能够迅速且针对特定芯片采取行动。关于"你从未收到的信"这一点：该信在物项上设立许可要求；依 EAR，下游一方若未经许可出口/再出口一项如今受管制的物项，无论其本人是否收到过副本都构成违规——义务附着于货物，而"明知或有理由知道"该要求的分销商即面临风险。对转售商的实际启示：你作为"不受管制"买入的芯片，可能因一封你只能从厂商或媒体得知的信而变为需要许可证。

Flag: the letters are not public; their exact text and scope are known only secondhand.提示：这些信函并不公开；其确切文本与范围只能从二手渠道得知。

Sources来源2
  • NVDA 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026
  • AMD 10-Q · 5 Aug 2026
A12 Which countries need a US license to buy advanced AI chips — the "tiers"?哪些国家购买先进 AI 芯片需要美国许可证——所谓"分级"？ detail细节

The widely reported three-tier system came from the AI Diffusion Rule, which is not enforced — so there are no live "tiers." What governs now is the older Country Group system: a license is needed for Country Groups D:1, D:4 and D:5 (D:5 = China, Russia, Iran and others; Macau too), and for any company headquartered in China/Macau anywhere. Most other countries — including a mid-size Asian hub like Singapore — need no license for ordinary buyers.

广为报道的三级体系来自 AI 扩散规则，而该规则未被执行——因此并不存在生效的"分级"。如今管辖的是更早的国家组体系：对 国家组 D:1、D:4 和 D:5（D:5 = 中国、俄罗斯、伊朗等；澳门亦在内）以及任何总部位于中国/澳门（无论身处何地）的公司，需要许可证。其余大多数国家——包括像新加坡这样的中型亚洲枢纽——对普通买家无需许可证。

The Diffusion Rule would have split the world into Tier 1 (close allies, few limits), Tier 2 (most countries, compute caps), Tier 3 (China and arms-embargoed states, denied). Because it is shelved, those tiers do not bind anyone. The operative map: 3A090 needs a license to D:1/D:4/D:5 and Macau, with D:5 (China/Macau) at presumption of denial (case-by-case only for H200-class from the US). A buyer in Singapore, Japan, Malaysia (Malaysia adds its own permit) or most of the world needs no US license for most chips — except a buyer headquartered in China/Macau, who needs one wherever it sits. The UAE was upgraded to favorable Group A:5 on 10 Jul 2026 (license-free for named parties). So "which tier is my country in" is better asked as "am I, or my customer, a China/Macau-HQ party, or shipping into D:5?" — if no, most chips flow license-free; if yes, a license (usually denied) is needed.

扩散规则本应将世界划分为第一级（亲密盟友，限制很少）、第二级（多数国家，设算力上限）、第三级（中国及受武器禁运国家，拒绝）。由于它被搁置，这些分级并不约束任何人。现行的适用图景：3A090 对 D:1/D:4/D:5 及澳门出口需许可证，其中 D:5（中国/澳门）为推定拒绝（仅 H200 级自美国出口可逐案审查）。新加坡、日本、马来西亚（马来西亚另设本国许可）或世界上大多数地方的买家，对多数芯片无需美国许可证——但总部位于中国/澳门的买家例外，无论身处何地都需要许可证。阿联酋已于 2026 年 7 月 10 日升入优惠的 A:5 组（对指定方免许可）。因此"我的国家属于哪一级"更应问成"我或我的客户是否为总部位于中国/澳门的主体，或是否运往 D:5？"——若否，多数芯片免许可流通；若是，则需要许可证（通常被拒）。

Sources来源4
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • 90 FR 4544 · tier scheme, now shelved分级方案，现已搁置 · 15 Jan 2025
  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026
  • 91 FR 43034 · UAE A:5阿联酋 A:5 · 14 Jul 2026
A13 Does the Foreign Direct Product Rule reach a Taiwan-made chip?外国直接产品规则是否触及台湾制造的芯片？ expert专家

Yes. A chip made in Taiwan with US chip-design software or US-origin tools is still "subject to the EAR" under the Foreign Direct Product (FDP) Rule, so your Singapore entity re-selling it into China needs the same US license as if it were US-made. For these advanced-computing items there is effectively no de minimis escape for China.

会。以美国芯片设计软件或美国原产工具制造的台湾芯片，依外国直接产品（FDP）规则仍"受 EAR 管辖"，因此你的新加坡实体将其转售入华，所需的美国许可证与它是美国制造时相同。对这些先进计算物项而言，对华实际上没有微量豁免可循。

The FDP Rule (15 CFR §734.9) pulls a foreign-made item under US control when it is the "direct product" of specified US technology/software, or made by a plant that is itself a direct product of US tools. TSMC (Taiwan) uses US EDA software and US-origin equipment, so its advanced AI chips are captured by the Advanced Computing FDP and, for designated parties, the supercomputer / Entity List variants (the "footnote" designations). BIS has applied this aggressively — most prominently alleging in 2025 that TSMC-made dies ended up in Huawei Ascend products, with reported penalty exposure in the $1bn+ range (press figure, not a final order). The ordinary de minimis test (a product with little US content escaping control) does not rescue these items for China: the advanced-computing/supercomputer FDP captures them regardless of the percentage of US content, so your Singapore resale carries the US license requirement.

FDP 规则（15 CFR §734.9）在外国制造的物项是特定美国技术/软件的"直接产品"，或由本身为美国工具直接产品的工厂制造时，将其纳入美国管制。台积电（台湾）使用美国 EDA 软件和美国原产设备，因此其先进 AI 芯片被 先进计算 FDP 捕获，并在涉及指定方时被 超级计算机／实体清单 变体（即"脚注"指定）捕获。BIS 对此适用得颇为强硬——最突出者为 2025 年指控台积电制造的裸片最终进入华为 Ascend 产品，据报处罚敞口达 10 亿美元以上（媒体数字，非最终裁定）。普通的 微量 测试（美国成分很少的产品可免于管制）并不能让这些物项对华获救：先进计算／超级计算机 FDP 无论美国成分占比多少都将其捕获，因此你的新加坡转售须承担美国许可要求。

Flag: the project's source files confirm the FDP reach and the TSMC-die allegation at a high level; the precise §734.9 footnote-1 vs footnote-5 mapping for a given Taiwan-packaged part, and the exact de minimis treatment per variant, are a lawyer-level detail not spelled out in those files — confirm against the eCFR §734.9 text and Supplement No. 1 to Part 734 for a specific transaction. The TSMC $1bn figure is a press report.提示：本项目的源文件在高层面确认了 FDP 的适用范围与台积电裸片的指控；但对某一台湾封装部件而言，§734.9 脚注 1 与脚注 5 的精确对应关系，以及每个变体的确切微量处理，属于律师层面的细节，这些文件未予阐明——就具体交易请对照 eCFR §734.9 文本及第 734 部分附录 1 核实。台积电 10 亿美元数字系媒体报道。

Sources来源3
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • 15 CFR 734.9
  • Project research files本站研究文件 press媒体
A14 "Knowledge or reason to know," and the red flags"明知或有理由知道"，以及那些危险信号 expert专家

You don't need to be told a buyer is diverting chips to China — if the facts around a deal should have tipped you off, the law treats you as knowing ("reason to know" / willful blindness). Classic red flags: a buyer with no AI use for that many GPUs, reluctance to name the end user, routing through a known transshipment hub, or prices and payment terms that don't fit a legitimate customer.

你无需被人告知买家正在把芯片转移到中国——若一笔交易周遭的事实本应让你警觉，法律即视你为明知（"有理由知道"／蓄意视而不见）。典型的危险信号：一个买家没有能用上这么多 GPU 的 AI 业务、不愿指明最终用户、经由已知的转运枢纽路由，或价格和付款条件不符合正当客户。

The EAR's "knowledge" standard covers actual knowledge and awareness of a high probability plus conscious avoidance of confirming it. BIS's May 2025 diversion guidance and the 2025–26 enforcement cases set out the red flags regulators use: a new or thinly-documented buyer ordering large volumes of controlled GPUs; a customer whose stated business can't use that compute; refusal to identify the end user or end use; shipment routed through Hong Kong, Singapore, Malaysia or the UAE with onward movement to China; mismatched addresses, freight-forwarder-only contacts, or payment from unrelated third parties; and requests to mislabel or under-declare goods. To defeat a "willful blindness" theory, a distributor's file should show screening against the Entity List and denied-persons lists, end-user/end-use certificates, KYC on ownership (the ultimate parent — is it China/Macau-HQ?), and records kept five years. Several 2025–26 prosecutions turned on exactly this evidence (freight documents, chat logs, false end-user statements).

EAR 的"知道"标准既涵盖实际知情，也涵盖意识到高度可能性却有意回避核实。BIS 2025 年 5 月的转移指引及 2025—2026 年的执法案件列出了监管者使用的危险信号：一个新的或资料单薄的买家订购大量受管制 GPU；客户所称业务用不上那么多算力；拒绝指明最终用户或最终用途；货物经香港、新加坡、马来西亚或阿联酋路由并继续转运至中国；地址不一致、仅有货代联系人，或由无关第三方付款；以及要求错误标注或低报货物。要击破"蓄意视而不见"的指控，分销商的档案应显示：对照实体清单与被拒绝人员清单的筛查、最终用户／最终用途证明、对所有权的 KYC（最终母公司——是否总部位于中国/澳门？），以及保存 五年 的记录。2025—2026 年的多起检控正是取决于此类证据（货运单据、聊天记录、虚假的最终用户声明）。

Sources来源3
  • BIS diversion guidance · 13 May 2025
  • Finnegan · five-year records, KYC五年记录、KYC · 21 Sep 2026
  • Project research files本站研究文件
A15 The legal status of model-weights export controls模型权重出口管制的法律现状 expert专家

There is a control on AI model weights on the books — ECCN 4E091, added by the Jan 2025 Diffusion Rule — but because that rule is not enforced, the weights control does not currently bind exporters. No separate, live rule controls model weights today.

在册有一项对 AI 模型权重的管制——出口管制分类编码（ECCN）4E091，由 2025 年 1 月的扩散规则新增——但由于该规则未被执行，这项权重管制目前并不约束出口商。如今没有任何单独的、生效的规则管制模型权重。

The Diffusion Rule added 4E091, a control on the weights of the most advanced closed-weight models, plus red-flag guidance on using US chips to train controlled Chinese models. When BIS announced non-enforcement (13 May 2025), the GAO's 2026 analysis spells out that this "effectively sets aside" the Diffusion Rule's additions — including the new model-weights authorization and reporting duties, which "no longer function in practice." So: the statutory hook exists, but the operative control is suspended. The related risk that survives is the May 2025 BIS warning that letting US chips train or run Chinese AI models can raise end-use concerns — a diversion/end-use matter, not a standalone weights license. If a replacement rule is ever published, a weights control could be revived (the withdrawn March 2026 draft would have scaled licenses to compute).

扩散规则新增了 4E091，对最先进闭源权重模型的权重实施管制，并附带关于使用美国芯片训练受管制中国模型的危险信号指引。当 BIS 宣布不予执行时（2025 年 5 月 13 日），GAO 的 2026 年分析阐明，这"实际上搁置"了扩散规则的新增内容——包括新的模型权重授权与报告义务，它们"在实践中不再运作"。因此：法定依据存在，但适用的管制处于暂停。仍然存续的相关风险是 2025 年 5 月 BIS 的警告：让美国芯片训练或运行中国 AI 模型可能引发最终用途方面的担忧——这属于转移／最终用途问题，而非单独的权重许可证。若最终发布替代规则，权重管制可能复活（2026 年 3 月撤回的草案本会按算力分级许可）。

Sources来源3
  • 90 FR 4544 · 4E0914E091 · 15 Jan 2025
  • GAO B-337935 · 12 May 2026
  • BIS · training warning训练警告 · 13 May 2025
A16 License Exceptions NAC, ACA, LPP — what they cover, notification, and Macau/Hong Kong许可例外 NAC、ACA、LPP——涵盖范围、通知要求，以及澳门/香港 detail细节

Two live exceptions for advanced-computing chips: NAC (for D:5/Macau and China/Macau-HQ parties, but not the top data-center chips, and it needs a 25-day advance notice to BIS) and ACA (for the rest of the world and in-country transfers, no notice). A third, LPP, exists only in the shelved Diffusion Rule, so it is not usable today. Hong Kong is treated as China/Macau for this purpose.

先进计算芯片有两项生效的例外：NAC（面向 D:5/澳门及总部位于中国/澳门的主体，但不含顶级数据中心芯片，且需提前 25 天通知 BIS）与 ACA（面向世界其余地区及境内转移，无需通知）。第三项 LPP 仅存在于被搁置的扩散规则中，因此如今不可使用。就此而言，香港被视同中国/澳门。

Both NAC and ACA live in 15 CFR §740.8:

NAC 与 ACA 均规定于 15 CFR §740.8：

  • NAC (Notified Advanced Computing). Authorizes export/re-export of 3A090 (except 3A090.c HBM), 4A090 and related ".z" items to Macau, Country Group D:5, and entities headquartered (or with an ultimate parent) in Macau/D:5 anywhere — but not items designed/marketed for a data center that meet 3A090.a (i.e., the top data-center GPUs are excluded). Requires a written purchase order and a prior notification to BIS at least 25 calendar days before shipment, via SNAP-R; BIS can convert it to a license requirement within those 25 days. In practice NAC covers lower-tier and ".z"-type items, not the H100/H200-class data-center parts.NAC（通知式先进计算，Notified Advanced Computing）。授权将 3A090（3A090.c HBM 除外）、4A090 及相关 ".z" 项出口/再出口至 澳门、国家组 D:5，以及总部设于（或最终母公司位于）澳门/D:5 任何地方的主体——但不含为数据中心设计/营销且达到 3A090.a 的物项（即顶级数据中心 GPU 被排除）。需在发货前经 SNAP-R 提交书面采购订单并至少提前 25 个日历日向 BIS 通知；BIS 可在该 25 天内将其转为许可要求。实践中，NAC 覆盖较低级别与 ".z" 类物项，而非 H100/H200 级数据中心部件。
  • ACA (Advanced Computing Authorized). Split out from NAC in April 2024. Same item scope, but for everywhere except Macau/D:5/their HQ parties (plus it allows in-country transfers within Macau/D:5). No notification required.ACA（先进计算授权，Advanced Computing Authorized）。于 2024 年 4 月从 NAC 中拆分。物项范围相同，但面向 除澳门/D:5/其总部主体以外的所有地方（并允许在 澳门/D:5 境内转移）。无需通知。
  • LPP (Low Processing Performance), §740.29. Created by the Jan 2025 Diffusion Rule: up to 26,900,000 TPP per calendar year per ultimate consignee, direct to consignees in destinations outside D:5/Macau (not for China/Macau-HQ parties, not through distributors). Because it is part of the non-enforced Diffusion Rule, LPP (and the Diffusion Rule's AIA/ACM exceptions) cannot be relied on today.LPP（低处理性能，Low Processing Performance），§740.29。由 2025 年 1 月的扩散规则设立：每个最终收货人每日历年最多 26,900,000 TPP，直接发往 D:5/澳门以外目的地的收货人（不适用于总部位于中国/澳门的主体，不得经由分销商）。由于它属于未被执行的扩散规则，LPP（以及扩散规则的 AIA/ACM 例外）今日不可依赖。

Macau and Hong Kong: Macau is named throughout §740.8 alongside D:5; Hong Kong is treated as part of China for US export controls (the 10-Qs say "China (including Hong Kong and Macau)"), so these exceptions apply to HK on the same China/D:5 footing. The top data-center chips and HBM are excluded from NAC/ACA, so for those the realistic path is a license, not an exception.

澳门与香港：§740.8 通篇将澳门与 D:5 并列列明；就美国出口管制而言，香港被视为中国的一部分（各 10-Q 称"中国（含香港和澳门）"），因此这些例外对香港按与中国/D:5 相同的地位适用。顶级数据中心芯片与 HBM 被排除在 NAC/ACA 之外，故对这些物项而言，现实路径是许可证而非例外。

Flag: NAC/ACA/LPP text was not in the project's legal files; it was confirmed from the eCFR and the Jan 2025 Federal Register for this answer.提示：NAC/ACA/LPP 文本不在本项目的法律文件中；本答案据 eCFR 与 2025 年 1 月《联邦公报》予以确认。

  • CBP CSMS · C67/C68 and the NAC/ACA splitC67/C68 与 NAC/ACA 拆分 · 2024
  • Baker McKenzie · NAC/ACA splitNAC/ACA 拆分 · 22 Apr 2024
  • 90 FR 4544 · LPP in §740.29§740.29 中的 LPP · 15 Jan 2025

B

Which chips, where哪些芯片，去哪里

Chip by chip — allowed, licensed or banned for China today, with ECCN; the H20 and the China-only parts; vendor self-classification vs BIS confirmation; servers and boards vs loose chips; used and refurbished; Hong Kong and Macau.

逐款芯片——当前对华属允许、须许可还是被禁，附 ECCN；H20 及中国特供版；厂商自行归类 vs BIS 确认；整机与板卡 vs 散装芯片；二手与翻新；香港与澳门。

B1 Which Nvidia chips can be sold to China today, and which cannot?如今哪些英伟达芯片可售往中国，哪些不可？ basic基础

The newest/most powerful chips (Blackwell: B200, B300, B30A) are banned. Mid-tier chips (H200, and AMD's MI325X) can be licensed case-by-case but only shipped from the US with a 25% tariff, and Beijing has throttled imports. H20 / MI308 need a license (granted since Aug 2025). Below-threshold cards (RTX PRO 5500 / 6000D, RTX 4090D, 5090D) need no US license. So yes, Nvidia can sell some chips to China in Oct 2026 — just not its best ones, and not freely.

最新/最强的芯片（Blackwell：B200、B300、B30A）遭禁止。中端芯片（H200 及 AMD 的 MI325X）可逐案审查获得许可，但只能自美国发货并缴 25% 关税，且北京已对进口加以节流。H20 / MI308 需要许可证（自 2025 年 8 月起已获批）。低于门槛的显卡（RTX PRO 5500 / 6000D、RTX 4090D、5090D）无需美国许可证。因此，2026 年 10 月英伟达确实可以向中国出售部分芯片——只是并非其最好的芯片，也并非自由销售。

Four buckets as of 31 Oct 2026:

截至 2026 年 10 月 31 日，分为四类：

Bucket类别 Chips芯片 China status对华状态
Banned (presumption of denial)禁止（推定拒绝）B300/GB300, B200/GB200, B30A (blocked), RTX 5090B300/GB300、B200/GB200、B30A（已封锁）、RTX 5090No path; Blackwell survived the Sep 2026 summit controls无路径；Blackwell 在 2026 年 9 月峰会的管制中存续
Licensable case-by-case (US exports only, 25% tariff)可逐案审查许可（仅限美国出口，25% 关税）H200, H100/H800, A100/A800, L40S/L40/L4 (H200 the only one with licenses granted)H200、H100/H800、A100/A800、L40S/L40/L4（仅 H200 已获批许可）Licenses granted Feb 2026 for "small amounts"; Beijing capped imports2026 年 2 月为"小批量"发放许可；北京对进口设限
Licensed by is-informed letter凭告知函许可H20, AMD MI308H20、AMD MI308Licenses from Aug 2025; but Beijing discouraged H20自 2025 年 8 月起发放许可；但北京不鼓励 H20
No US license needed (below threshold)无需美国许可证（低于门槛）RTX PRO 5500, RTX PRO 6000D, RTX 4090D, RTX 5090D/5090D V2RTX PRO 5500、RTX PRO 6000D、RTX 4090D、RTX 5090D/5090D V2Supply is the limit, plus China-side blocks on some SKUs瓶颈在供应，另有中方对部分型号的封锁

Is Nvidia "still allowed to sell at all" in Oct 2026? Yes — via the licensed H20/H200 route and the below-threshold cards — but it is tiny relative to the past: H200 shipments were "a fraction of the allowed shipments" (<1% of data-center revenue), and the big money is in the below-threshold RTX PRO 5500, which drew >3M Chinese orders.

英伟达在 2026 年 10 月"究竟还能不能卖"？能——通过已获许可的 H20/H200 渠道及低于门槛的显卡——但相对过去微乎其微：H200 出货量只是"获准出货量的一小部分"（占数据中心收入 <1%），而真正赚钱的是低于门槛的 RTX PRO 5500，它吸引了 >300 万份中国订单。

B2 What is the H20, and why was it made for China?H20 是什么，为何专为中国而造？ basic基础

The H20 is a cut-down Nvidia data-center GPU built specifically to stay under the October 2023 export thresholds so it could be sold to China without tripping the ban. It is much weaker than an H100 at raw math but keeps big, fast memory, which suits AI inference (serving models).

H20 是一款阉割版的英伟达数据中心 GPU，专为保持在 2023 年 10 月出口门槛之下而设计，以便在不触发禁令的情况下售往中国。它在原始算力上远弱于 H100，却保留了大而快的内存，适合 AI 推理（为模型提供服务）。

Nvidia designed the H20 after the Oct 2023 rules to fit below 3A090 on the compute formula: TPP ≈ 2,368 (vs ≈ 15,832 for H100/H200), with FP8 ≈ 296 TFLOPS dense. But it keeps 96 GB HBM3 and 4.0 TB/s bandwidth and NVLink 900 GB/s — so per-chip it is ~15× weaker than a B200 on compute but only ~2× on memory bandwidth. That memory-heavy profile makes it decent for inference and for clustering. Its weakness was the point: comply with the letter of the threshold. The US closed that gap in April 2025 with an is-informed letter making the H20 license-required anyway (it named "the H20's memory bandwidth, interconnect bandwidth, or combination thereof"). Then Beijing discouraged H20 purchases over "backdoor" claims, so Nvidia ended up "unable to sell our H20 inventory."

英伟达在 2023 年 10 月规则之后设计 H20，使其在算力公式上低于 3A090：TPP ≈ 2,368（相比 H100/H200 的 ≈ 15,832），FP8 稠密 ≈ 296 TFLOPS。但它保留了 96 GB HBM3 与 4.0 TB/s 带宽及 NVLink 900 GB/s——因此单芯片在算力上比 B200 弱约 15 倍，而在内存带宽上仅弱约 2 倍。这种偏重内存的特性使其适于推理与成簇部署。它的弱正是用意所在：恪守门槛的字面规定。美国于 2025 年 4 月以一封告知函填补了这一缺口，使 H20 无论如何都需要许可证（信中点名"H20 的内存带宽、互连带宽或两者组合"）。随后北京以"后门"为由不鼓励购买 H20，英伟达最终"无法售出我们的 H20 库存"。

Sources来源4
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • SemiAnalysis · H20 below both 3A090 testsH20 低于 3A090 两项测试
  • NVDA 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026
  • AP · CAC "backdoor" summonsCAC "后门" 约谈 · 31 Jul 2025
B3 The H20 saga: allowed, banned, revived — and on what terms?H20 风波：获准、禁止、复活——以什么条件？ detail细节

The H20 was sellable (2024) → license-required by US letter (April 2025) → effectively blocked → re-allowed with licenses from Aug 2025, under an informal 15% revenue share to the US government → then undercut by Beijing's own discouragement, so little actually sold. None of the US moves was published as a formal rule.

H20 曾可销售（2024 年）→ 因美国信函而需要许可证（2025 年 4 月）→ 实际上被封锁 → 自 2025 年 8 月 起凭许可重新获准，附带向美国政府非正式缴纳 15% 收入分成 → 随后被北京自身的劝阻所削弱，实际销量甚微。美国的各项举措均未作为正式规则发布。

Sequence: designed in 2024 to be uncontrolled; April 2025 — BIS is-informed letter requires a license for H20 (and AMD MI308) to China/D:5/Macau-HQ; a period where no sales could proceed; Aug 2025 — licenses begin, paired with an informal arrangement under which Nvidia (and AMD on MI308) would pay 15% of China sales to the US government. That 15% share "was never published as a rule, and its legality is contested" — and neither Nvidia's nor AMD's 10-Q confirms a 15% payment was actually made; in practice it was overtaken by the separate 25% import tariff of Jan 2026. On the China side, the CAC's July 2025 "backdoor" summons and import gating meant Nvidia "was unable to sell our H20 inventory." So the H20 is legally sellable under license today but commercially stuck.

过程：2024 年设计为不受管制；2025 年 4 月——BIS 告知函要求 H20（及 AMD MI308）向中国/D:5/总部位于澳门主体出口需许可证；随后一段时间无法进行任何销售；2025 年 8 月——许可开始发放，并附带一项非正式安排，英伟达（及 AMD 就 MI308）将向美国政府缴纳中国销售额的 15%。这 15% 分成"从未作为规则发布，其合法性存在争议"——而英伟达与 AMD 的 10-Q 均未确认实际支付过 15%；实践中它被 2026 年 1 月单独的 25% 进口关税 所取代。在中国一侧，CAC 2025 年 7 月的"后门"约谈与进口把关，使英伟达"无法售出我们的 H20 库存"。因此，H20 如今在法律上可凭许可销售，但在商业上陷入停滞。

Flags: the April 2025 letters and the H20 license conditions are not public; whether the 15% share was ever paid is not confirmed.提示：2025 年 4 月的信函与 H20 许可条件不公开；15% 分成是否曾被支付未获证实。

Sources来源4
  • NVDA 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026
  • AMD 10-Q · 5 Aug 2026
  • builtin.com · 15% share15% 分成
  • Project research files本站研究文件
B4 H200 licensing status for China, and the conditionsH200 对华许可现状及其条件 detail细节

The H200 can be licensed case-by-case since 15 Jan 2026, but only as a shipment from the US to a vetted China/Macau end user, after an independent US lab test, with a 25% import tariff and a cap tying China volume to US volume. Nvidia got licenses in Feb 2026, but Beijing let in only a fraction.

自 2026 年 1 月 15 日起，H200 可逐案审查获得许可，但仅限于作为自美国发往经审查的中国/澳门最终用户的货物，须经美国独立实验室测试，附带 25% 进口关税，并有一项将对华数量与对美数量挂钩的上限。英伟达于 2026 年 2 月获得许可，但北京只放行了一小部分。

Under 91 FR 1684 a chip under the H200 line (TPP < 21,000 and DRAM bandwidth < 6,500 GB/s; H200 is TPP ≈ 15,800, 4.8 TB/s) moves from denial to case-by-case for US-origin exports to end users in China/Macau. The applicant must certify nine conditions, the load-bearing ones being: (1) full specs and the maker's US shipment numbers; (2) enough US supply (no delay to US orders, no foundry capacity pulled from US customers); (3) total TPP to China/Macau ≤ 50% of the same product's TPP shipped to US end users; (4) no military/MI/WMD/Entity List end user, including via remote access; (5) consignee KYC filed with BIS; (9) every shipment tested by an independent US lab before export. Because conditions 1–3 need the maker's own US sales/foundry data, in practice the license applicant is Nvidia (or AMD), not a reseller; a third party's role is as the named Chinese end user/consignee. Cost: the 25% Section 232 tariff on entry to the US for testing (Nvidia says it cannot pass this on). Reality: Beijing capped approvals — ByteDance and Tencent got ~10k H200 each by Aug 2026 against 75k licensed, and Nvidia shipped "a fraction," <1% of data-center revenue. (No H100 licenses have been reported, though H100 is below the line "in principle.")

依 91 FR 1684，低于 H200 线的芯片（TPP < 21,000 且 DRAM 带宽 < 6,500 GB/s；H200 为 TPP ≈ 15,800、4.8 TB/s），就美国原产、运往中国/澳门最终用户的出口，从拒绝转为逐案审查。申请人须证明九项条件，其中关键者为：(1) 完整规格及厂商对美出货数量；(2) 对美供应充足（不延误对美订单，不从美国客户抽调代工产能）；(3) 对华/澳门的总 TPP ≤ 同一产品对美最终用户出货 TPP 的 50%；(4) 无军事/军事情报/大规模杀伤性武器/实体清单最终用户，包括经由远程访问；(5) 向 BIS 提交收货人 KYC；(9) 出口前每批货物均由美国独立实验室测试。由于条件 1–3 需要厂商自身的对美销售/代工数据，实践中许可申请人是英伟达（或 AMD），而非转售商；第三方的角色是被指明的中国最终用户/收货人。成本：进入美国接受测试时的 25% 第 232 条关税（英伟达称无法转嫁）。实情：北京对批准设限——截至 2026 年 8 月，字节跳动和腾讯各获约 1 万片 H200，而获批许可为 7.5 万片，英伟达只出货"一小部分"，占数据中心收入 <1%。（尚无 H100 许可的报道，尽管 H100"原则上"低于该线。）

Flag: the specific H200 license conditions granted to Nvidia are not public beyond the rule's certifications.提示：授予英伟达的具体 H200 许可条件，除规则所列证明外并不公开。

Sources来源4
  • 91 FR 1684 · §(dd)§(dd) · 15 Jan 2026
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • NVDA 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026
  • Asia Times · 29 Sep 2026

B6 Nvidia's China-specific parts: specs vs thresholds, and is the classification BIS-confirmed or self-declared?英伟达的中国专供部件：规格对比门槛，分类是 BIS 确认还是自行申报？ detail细节

For the H20 and the China SKUs (RTX PRO 6000D, RTX PRO 5500, and the never-shipped B30A/B40 class), the "compliant" status is essentially the vendor's self-classification against the published thresholds — BIS has not confirmed them in a public writing. The H20 was in fact overridden by BIS with a license letter despite its sub-threshold specs.

对于 H20 及中国专供型号（RTX PRO 6000D、RTX PRO 5500，以及从未出货的 B30A/B40 级），"合规"状态本质上是厂商对照公开门槛的自行分类——BIS 从未以公开书面形式予以确认。事实上，尽管 H20 规格低于门槛，BIS 仍以一封许可信函将其推翻。

Specs against the 3A090 line: H20 TPP ≈ 2,368 (below 4,800) — yet license-required by the April 2025 letter, so sub-threshold specs did not keep it free. RTX PRO 6000D / RTX PRO 5500 are designed "just below the export control red line" (TPP < 4,800; non-data-center workstation parts are outside the 3A090.b data-center band), with 84 GB GDDR7 and 1,398 GB/s — but no official US classification has been found; they are sold as uncontrolled on the maker's reading. B30A (one B300 die, ~144 GB HBM3e, ~4 TB/s) sits above the H200 line and was blocked by a White House decision (reported Nov 2025) — never approved, so no classification question arises. The pattern: makers self-classify to the thresholds, BIS does not publish per-chip confirmations, and BIS reserves the right to override with an is-informed letter (as with H20). Prudent practice is to get the ECCN in writing from the maker and watch for new letters.

对照 3A090 线的规格：H20 TPP ≈ 2,368（低于 4,800）——却因 2025 年 4 月的信函而需要许可证，可见低于门槛的规格并未使其获得豁免。RTX PRO 6000D / RTX PRO 5500 被设计为"刚好低于出口管制红线"（TPP < 4,800；非数据中心的工作站部件不在 3A090.b 数据中心区间内），配 84 GB GDDR7 与 1,398 GB/s——但未发现官方美国分类；它们按厂商的理解作为不受管制品销售。B30A（一颗 B300 裸片、约 144 GB HBM3e、约 4 TB/s）高于 H200 线，并被白宫决定封锁（据报于 2025 年 11 月）——从未批准，故不涉及分类问题。模式是：厂商对照门槛自行分类，BIS 不发布逐芯片确认，并保留以告知函推翻的权利（一如 H20）。审慎做法是取得厂商书面出具的 ECCN，并留意新的信函。

Flags: no public BIS classification for RTX PRO 5500 or 6000D; B40 specs not independently found.提示：RTX PRO 5500 或 6000D 无公开的 BIS 分类；B40 规格未经独立查证。

B7 Servers, boards and cloud systems vs loose chips — and does used / refurbished change anything?服务器、板卡与云系统相较散装芯片——二手/翻新是否有别？ expert专家

No material difference. A board, server or rack containing a controlled chip is itself controlled (ECCN 4A090 mirrors 3A090), so a server full of H100s is treated like the loose chips. Used or refurbished controlled chips are still controlled — age doesn't declassify a GPU — and reselling a controlled chip that's already inside China is itself presumption-of-denial.

没有实质区别。含受管制芯片的板卡、服务器或机架本身即受管制（出口管制分类编码（ECCN）4A090 对应 3A090），因此一台装满 H100 的服务器与散装芯片同等对待。二手或翻新的受管制芯片仍受管制——GPU 不会因年份而解除管制——而转售已在中国境内的受管制芯片本身即属推定拒绝。

The EAR controls the 4A090 "computer" and the components alike; the big smuggling cases were about servers (Supermicro/Dell boxes), not loose dies, and they were charged just the same. So a data-center system, DGX/HGX board, or NVL rack carrying 3A090 chips needs the same license as the chips. Cloud systems (the physical kit) are controlled goods; renting compute over them is a different question (currently not treated as an export of the chips). Used/refurbished: the classification follows the chip's specs, not its condition or price; a used H100 is still 3A090.a. And because in-country transfers inside China are presumption of denial, you cannot lawfully buy a controlled chip already sitting in China and resell it there without a license. The only "condition" nuance that matters is BIS's allowance that bona fide data-center operators may keep using/servicing/disposing of chips they already hold — but that is not a license to buy or trade more.

EAR 对 4A090"计算机"与其组件一视同仁；重大走私案件涉及的是服务器（超微/戴尔整机），而非散装裸片，起诉也一视同仁。因此，承载 3A090 芯片的数据中心系统、DGX/HGX 板卡或 NVL 机架，所需许可证与芯片相同。云系统（实体设备）属受管制货物；在其上出租算力则是另一回事（目前不被视为芯片的出口）。二手/翻新：分类取决于芯片规格，而非其状况或价格；一块二手 H100 仍是 3A090.a。而由于中国境内转移属推定拒绝，你不得在未经许可的情况下合法买入已在中国的受管制芯片并就地转售。唯一相关的"状况"细节是 BIS 的许可：善意的数据中心运营商可继续使用/维护/处置其已持有的芯片——但这并非再行买卖的许可。

Sources来源5
  • 91 FR 1684 · in-country transfers = denial境内转移=拒绝 · 15 Jan 2026
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • DOJ Operation Gatekeeper · servers charged服务器被起诉
  • The Register · Supermicro servers超微服务器 · 20 Mar 2026
  • BIS guidance · existing kit已有设备 · 31 May 2026
B8 Are Hong Kong and Macau treated the same as mainland China?香港和澳门是否与中国内地同等对待？ expert专家

Yes. For US export controls, Hong Kong and Macau are treated as part of China (both are in, or tied to, Country Group D:5). Every China restriction — the license requirement, presumption of denial, the is-informed letters — applies to HK and Macau the same way. There is no chip you can send to Hong Kong that you couldn't send to the mainland.

是。就美国出口管制而言，香港和澳门被视为中国的一部分（二者均属或挂钩国家组 D:5）。每一项对华限制——许可要求、推定拒绝、告知函——对香港和澳门同样适用。没有哪款芯片是你能发往香港却不能发往内地的。

Nvidia and AMD describe every China restriction as "China (including Hong Kong and Macau)." Macau is named explicitly throughout the rules (e.g., §744.23, §740.8, 91 FR 1684's "China or Macau"); Hong Kong has been treated as China for export-control purposes since 2020. So: same ECCNs, same license requirement, same denial posture, same case-by-case H200 treatment, same NAC/ACA exclusion of top data-center chips. HK is also a named transit point in smuggling cases (Operation Gatekeeper shipped "to the PRC and Hong Kong"), and Nvidia notes its own supply is "warehoused in and distributed from Hong Kong." Separately, Hong Kong has its own strategic-commodities regime (Cap. 60) that follows the multilateral lists, not US ECCNs — but that is an additional local layer, not a loophole: a US-origin or FDP chip still carries the US rules into HK. Macau is not a meaningful commercial chip destination but is covered identically in the text.

英伟达和 AMD 将每一项对华限制表述为"中国（含香港和澳门）"。规则通篇明确点名澳门（例如 §744.23、§740.8、91 FR 1684 的"中国或澳门"）；就出口管制而言，香港自 2020 年起即被视为中国。因此：相同的 ECCN、相同的许可要求、相同的拒绝姿态、相同的 H200 逐案审查处理、相同的 NAC/ACA 对顶级数据中心芯片的排除。香港在走私案件中也是被点名的中转点（Operation Gatekeeper 的货物发往"中华人民共和国和香港"），英伟达亦指出其自身供应"在香港仓储并从香港分销"。另外，香港有其自身的战略物品制度（第 60 章），遵循多边清单而非美国 ECCN——但这是附加的本地层级，而非漏洞：美国原产或 FDP 芯片进入香港时仍带着美国规则。澳门并非有意义的商业芯片目的地，但在文本中被同等涵盖。

Sources来源5

C

China's own side中国这一侧

Beijing's ban / "guidance" / customs hold / CAC security review and which agency carries it; "buy domestic" rules for state data centers; China's Export Control Law, Unreliable Entity List and Anti-Foreign-Sanctions Law; rare-earth and gallium counter-controls.

北京的禁令／"窗口指导"／海关扣留／网信办安全审查及其主管机构；国资数据中心的"用国产"规定；中国的《出口管制法》、不可靠实体清单与《反外国制裁法》；稀土与镓的反制管制。

C1 Is the Chinese government telling its companies to stop buying Nvidia, and does that kill the demand I am hoping to serve?中国政府是否在要求本国企业停止购买英伟达芯片？这会不会扼杀我希望服务的那部分需求？ basic基础

Beijing discourages Nvidia purchases and gates them case by case, but it has not banned them, and it lets a few in where Chinese chips cannot yet do the job. Demand is far from dead: orders for Nvidia parts still run into the millions of units; what is dead is the idea of selling freely.

北京劝阻采购英伟达芯片并逐案设限，但并未禁止，且在国产芯片尚无法胜任之处仍放行少量。需求远未消失：英伟达产品的订单仍以数百万颗计；真正消失的，是自由销售的空间。

  • There is no published ban. The levers are informal: a CAC summons over the H20 (31 Jul 2025), a CAC instruction to ByteDance, Alibaba and others to stop testing and ordering the RTX Pro 6000D (mid-Sep 2025), a customs crackdown (Oct 2025), a "domestic chips only" rule for new state-funded data centers (Nov 2025), a customs hold on the H200 (Jan 2026), then capped, conditional approvals (ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent allowed >400,000 H200 in Jan 2026; approvals reportedly capped under 200,000 by Jul 2026; ~10,000 each delivered to ByteDance and Tencent by Aug 2026 against 75,000 each licensed by the US).没有公开的禁令。手段都是非正式的：国家网信办（CAC）就 H20 约谈英伟达（2025 年 7 月 31 日）、CAC 指示字节跳动、阿里巴巴等停止测试与订购 RTX Pro 6000D（2025 年 9 月中）、海关整顿（2025 年 10 月）、新建国家资助数据中心"仅用国产芯片"的规定（2025 年 11 月）、海关扣下 H200（2026 年 1 月），其后转为设限的、附条件的批准（2026 年 1 月批准字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯合计 >400,000 颗 H200；据报到 2026 年 7 月批准额被压至 200,000 颗以下；到 2026 年 8 月字节跳动与腾讯各约 10,000 颗到货，而美方各许可 75,000 颗）。
  • Nvidia's own filing says H200 "sales were restricted by the PRC government" and it "was unable to sell our H20 inventory"; it needs a product that "meets the approval of both the USG and the Chinese government".英伟达自己的申报文件称，H200"销售受到中国政府限制"，且公司"无法销售其 H20 库存"；它需要一款"同时获得美国政府与中国政府批准"的产品。
  • Demand signal: Chinese firms ordered >2 million H200 (Jan 2026) and ByteDance, Alibaba and Tencent >3 million RTX PRO 5500 (>$35bn, Oct 2026); the industry ministry MIIT asked for RTX PRO 5500 purchase plans and signalled approval (late Sep 2026).需求信号：中国企业订购 >200 万颗 H200（2026 年 1 月）；字节跳动、阿里巴巴与腾讯订购 >300 万颗 RTX PRO 5500（逾 350 亿美元，2026 年 10 月）；工信部（MIIT）索要 RTX PRO 5500 采购计划并释放批准信号（2026 年 9 月下旬）。
  • Read-through from the market desk: Beijing mirrors Washington's tools (caps, end-use conditions, case-by-case) so Nvidia is admitted for frontier training and blocked elsewhere. The Bloomberg Intelligence gap estimate is ~1.6M units short in 2026 (demand ~4.2M vs supply ~2.6M).市场研究台的解读：北京在镜像华盛顿的工具（限额、最终用途条件、逐案审查），因而英伟达获准用于前沿训练、在其他场景被挡。彭博行业研究（Bloomberg Intelligence）估算 2026 年供需缺口约 160 万颗（需求约 420 万 vs 供给约 260 万）。

C2 What has Beijing formally told Chinese companies about buying Nvidia chips: a ban, "guidance", a customs hold, or a CAC-driven security review, and which instrument carries it?北京就购买英伟达芯片对中国企业正式下达了什么：禁令、"指导"、海关扣留，还是由网信办主导的安全审查？又由哪种法律文书承载？ basic基础

All four have happened, and none is a published legal instrument. The only text a client can cite is the CAC's 31 July 2025 notice that it summoned Nvidia under the Cybersecurity Law, Data Security Law and PIPL. Everything else is oral "window guidance" reported by the press, plus Nvidia's SEC disclosures of its effect.

四者都发生过，且没有一项是公开的法律文书。客户唯一能援引的文本，是网信办 2025 年 7 月 31 日依《网络安全法》《数据安全法》《个人信息保护法》约谈英伟达的通告。其余都是媒体报道的口头"窗口指导"，外加英伟达在美国证监会文件中对其影响的披露。

  • Legal character: the CAC notice is a 约谈 (regulatory interview), not an order to buyers. The rest is administrative guidance with no public text, enforceable in practice through customs clearance, procurement lists and the NDRC's import approvals.法律性质：网信办的通告是一次约谈（监管谈话），而非对买方的命令。其余是没有公开文本的行政指导，实践中通过海关放行、采购清单与发改委（NDRC）的进口审批来执行。
  • The citable documents are Nvidia's 10-Q ("sales were restricted by the PRC government") and the CAC notice.可援引的文件是英伟达的 10-Q（"销售受到中国政府限制"）与网信办通告。

C3 Which Chinese agency issued the 2025 instructions to stop ordering H20 / RTX Pro 6000D, and is there a written document my client can cite, or only oral window guidance?是哪个中国机构发出了 2025 年停止订购 H20 / RTX Pro 6000D 的指示？我的客户能援引书面文件，还是只有口头窗口指导？ detail细节

The CAC, by oral instruction. No written order to buyers has surfaced; the only written item is the CAC's notice that it summoned Nvidia on 31 July 2025.

是网信办，以口头指示下达。没有出现针对买方的书面命令；唯一的书面材料，是网信办关于 2025 年 7 月 31 日约谈英伟达的通告。

  • H20: after the 31 Jul 2025 summons, the FT reported in Aug 2025 that regulators "including the CAC" warned firms against buying the H20 and asked them to justify purchases over domestic products. Reuters reported Tencent and ByteDance among the firms discouraged.H20：2025 年 7 月 31 日约谈之后，《金融时报》于 2025 年 8 月报道，"包括网信办在内"的监管机构警告企业勿购 H20，并要求其说明为何不选国产。路透报道，被劝阻的企业包括腾讯与字节跳动。
  • RTX Pro 6000D: the FT (17 Sep 2025, three people) reports the CAC told ByteDance, Alibaba and others "this week" to end testing and orders; the firms then told server suppliers to stop. Regulators had first summoned Huawei, Cambricon, Alibaba and Baidu to compare their chips with Nvidia's China parts and concluded domestic chips were comparable.RTX Pro 6000D：《金融时报》（2025 年 9 月 17 日，三名知情人）报道，网信办"本周"告知字节跳动、阿里巴巴等停止测试与订购；随后企业要求服务器供应商停止。监管机构此前先约谈了华为、寒武纪、阿里巴巴与百度，将其芯片与英伟达的中国版比对，得出国产芯片性能相当的结论。
  • Nothing was published by CAC, MIIT, NDRC, MOFCOM or GACC. A client cannot cite a document; it can cite Nvidia's 10-Q, the CAC notice, and the press reports. The practical enforcement is downstream: customs clearance, the state procurement list (Huawei and Cambricon are the only vendors on the approved AI hardware list), and NDRC import approvals.网信办、工信部、发改委、商务部与海关总署均未发布任何文本。客户无法援引一份文件；可援引英伟达 10-Q、网信办通告以及媒体报道。实际执行在下游：海关放行、政府采购清单（华为与寒武纪是获批 AI 硬件清单上仅有的供应商），以及发改委进口审批。

Gap: whether any firm received a written notice privately is unknown.存疑：是否有企业私下收到书面通知，尚不可知。

C4 Has Beijing banned or discouraged companies from buying Nvidia chips?北京是禁止还是劝阻企业购买英伟达芯片？ basic基础

Discouraged, gated, and in some cases told to stop, but not banned by any published rule. The one hard rule is for new state-funded data centers, which must use domestic chips.

是劝阻、设限，某些情形下要求停止，但没有任何公开规则加以禁止。唯一的硬性规定针对新建的国家资助数据中心——它们必须使用国产芯片。

See the table in C2. The direction since July 2025: H20 discouraged (Jul–Aug 2025), RTX Pro 6000D orders stopped (Sep 2025), customs crackdown (Oct 2025), state data centers domestic-only (Nov 2025), H200 held at customs (Jan 2026) then allowed in capped batches (Jan–Aug 2026), RTX PRO 5500 purchase plans invited (Sep 2026). Nvidia's China AI accelerator share is estimated at 66% (2024), 40% (2025), ~8% projected for 2026 (Bernstein), though IDC's unit count still shows Nvidia at 34% of H1 2026 shipments because it includes lower-end parts.

见 C2 的表格。2025 年 7 月以来的走向：H20 被劝阻（2025 年 7—8 月）、RTX Pro 6000D 订单被叫停（2025 年 9 月）、海关整顿（2025 年 10 月）、国家数据中心仅用国产（2025 年 11 月）、H200 被海关扣留（2026 年 1 月）后分批设限放行（2026 年 1—8 月）、RTX PRO 5500 采购计划被征询（2026 年 9 月）。英伟达在中国 AI 加速器的份额估计为 66%（2024）、40%（2025）、2026 年预计约 8%（伯恩斯坦），不过 IDC 的出货量统计仍显示英伟达占 2026 年上半年出货的 34%，因其纳入了低端产品。

C5 What did Chinese regulators tell companies about H20 and similar purchases, and which firms were named?中国监管机构就 H20 及类似采购对企业说了什么？点名了哪些企业？ detail细节

Regulators told the big platforms to justify H20 buys, then to stop ordering the RTX Pro 6000D, then let three of them buy capped numbers of H200. Firms named in the reporting: ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent, Baidu, DeepSeek.

监管机构先要求大平台说明购买 H20 的理由，继而叫停 RTX Pro 6000D 订单，再允许其中三家购入设限数量的 H200。报道点名的企业：字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯、百度、DeepSeek。

  • H20 (Aug 2025): firms asked why they buy H20 over domestic chips; Tencent and ByteDance named as discouraged (Reuters). H20 "backdoor" concern was the stated reason (CAC).H20（2025 年 8 月）：企业被问及为何购 H20 而非国产芯片；腾讯与字节跳动被点名劝阻（路透）。所述理由是 H20 的"后门"担忧（网信办）。
  • RTX Pro 6000D (Sep 2025): CAC told ByteDance and Alibaba, among others, to end tests and orders; several firms had planned tens of thousands of units (FT).RTX Pro 6000D（2025 年 9 月）：网信办告知字节跳动、阿里巴巴等停止测试与订购；数家企业原计划采购数万颗（《金融时报》）。
  • H200 (Jan 2026): Beijing told Alibaba and other top firms to prepare H200 orders, reportedly tied to buying set amounts of domestic chips (Bloomberg, 23 Jan 2026); approvals for ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent for >400,000 total with conditions (Reuters, 28 Jan 2026). By Jul 2026 a plan to allow Alibaba, ByteDance and DeepSeek a limited number (Reuters, 8 Jul 2026). Reported use conditions: H200 for training, inference steered to Huawei; a possible fixed ratio of Chinese chips per H200 purchase (unconfirmed, aggregator).H200（2026 年 1 月）：北京要求阿里巴巴等头部企业准备 H200 订单，据报与购买一定数量国产芯片挂钩（彭博，2026 年 1 月 23 日）；附条件批准字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯合计 >400,000 颗（路透，2026 年 1 月 28 日）。到 2026 年 7 月，有计划允许阿里巴巴、字节跳动与 DeepSeek 购入有限数量（路透，2026 年 7 月 8 日）。据报的使用条件：H200 用于训练，推理引导至华为；每购一批 H200 可能搭配固定比例的国产芯片（未经证实，二手来源）。
  • RTX PRO 5500 (Sep–Oct 2026): MIIT asked big buyers for purchase plans; ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent ordered >3M (Reuters).RTX PRO 5500（2026 年 9—10 月）：工信部向大买家索要采购计划；字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯订购 >300 万颗（路透）。

C6 Why has Beijing discouraged Chinese companies from buying Nvidia chips?北京为何劝阻中国企业购买英伟达芯片？ basic基础

Three reasons, in Beijing's own order of telling: security (fear of tracking and remote kill switches in US chips), self-reliance (build a market for Huawei, Cambricon and the rest), and bargaining (chips are a card in the trade talks).

三条理由，按北京自己的陈述顺序：安全（担心美国芯片内置追踪与远程停用开关）、自主（为华为、寒武纪等培育市场），以及筹码（芯片是贸易谈判中的一张牌）。

  • Security: the CAC's 31 Jul 2025 notice cites US lawmakers' calls for "tracking and positioning" in exported chips and claims the technology for tracking and remote shutdown is mature. The Chip Security Act (H.R. 3447) would mandate location verification; it cleared House Foreign Affairs 42–0 on 26 Mar 2026.安全：网信办 2025 年 7 月 31 日的通告援引美国议员对出口芯片内置"追踪与定位"的呼吁，并称追踪与远程停用的技术已经成熟。《芯片安全法》（H.R. 3447）将强制要求位置核验；该法案于 2026 年 3 月 26 日在众议院外交事务委员会以 42–0 通过。
  • Self-reliance: the FT reports regulators concluded domestic chips are "comparable to or exceeding" Nvidia's China parts, and "the top-level consensus now is there's going to be enough domestic supply." State-funded data centers must use domestic chips (Nov 2025). Huawei and Cambricon are the only vendors on the approved procurement list. Domestic chips were ~41% of 2025 shipments (IDC); TrendForce projects nearly 90% of the "high-end" market in 2026 (definitions differ).自主：《金融时报》报道，监管机构认定国产芯片"相当于或优于"英伟达的中国版，且"高层的共识是国产供给将足够"。国家资助数据中心须用国产芯片（2025 年 11 月）。华为与寒武纪是获批采购清单上仅有的供应商。国产芯片约占 2025 年出货的 41%（IDC）；TrendForce 预计 2026 年将占"高端"市场近 90%（口径不同）。
  • Bargaining: SAMR's Nvidia finding (15 Sep 2025) and MOFCOM's anti-discrimination probe (13 Sep 2025) were timed with the Madrid trade talks; analysts quoted by Reuters read them as leverage.筹码：市场监管总局对英伟达的认定（2025 年 9 月 15 日）与商务部的反歧视调查（2025 年 9 月 13 日）与马德里贸易谈判同期；路透援引的分析人士将其解读为筹码。
  • Cost of the policy: Chinese chips trail (Ascend 910C ~60% of H100 on inference; CANN vs CUDA gap), and HBM is the binding limit, so Beijing admits Nvidia for frontier training while blocking it elsewhere.政策代价：国产芯片仍落后（Ascend 910C 推理性能约为 H100 的 60%；CANN 与 CUDA 存在差距），且 HBM 是硬约束，故北京放行英伟达用于前沿训练、在其他场景加以阻挡。

C7 What has Beijing done in return: customs checks, antitrust probes on Nvidia, rare-earth or gallium export limits, guidance to state firms?作为回应，北京做了什么：海关检查、对英伟达的反垄断调查、稀土或镓的出口限制、对国有企业的指导？ detail细节

All of them. The antitrust probe found a preliminary breach but no penalty yet; the rare-earth and gallium controls are the heavy weapon, and the newest round is paused until November 2026 as part of the Trump–Xi truce.

全都做了。反垄断调查作出初步违规认定，但尚无处罚；稀土与镓管制是重武器，而最新一轮作为特朗普—习近平休战的一部分，暂停至 2026 年 11 月。

C8 The SAMR antitrust probe into Nvidia (Mellanox conditions) and the CAC "backdoor" inquiry into H20: what is their legal basis, what remedies are on the table, and has any decision been published?市场监管总局对英伟达的反垄断调查（Mellanox 条件）与网信办对 H20 的"后门"调查：法律依据是什么？可能的救济有哪些？是否已有决定公布？ expert专家

SAMR acts under the Anti-Monopoly Law and its own 2020 conditional-approval decision on Nvidia–Mellanox; it published a one-line preliminary finding of breach on 15 Sep 2025 and nothing since. The CAC acts under the Cybersecurity Law, Data Security Law and PIPL; it published only the fact of the summons. No penalty, remedy or final decision has been published in either case.

市场监管总局依据《反垄断法》及其 2020 年对英伟达—Mellanox 的附条件批准决定行事；于 2025 年 9 月 15 日发布一句话的初步违规认定，此后再无动静。网信办依据《网络安全法》《数据安全法》《个人信息保护法》行事；仅公布了约谈这一事实。两案均未公布任何处罚、救济或最终决定。

  • SAMR: investigation opened 9 Dec 2024; preliminary finding 15 Sep 2025 that Nvidia violated the AML and the 2020 decision (公告 on conditional approval of the Mellanox acquisition). The 2020 conditions were behavioural: no tying of GPUs with Mellanox networking, no unreasonable terms, continued supply of GPU accelerators and Mellanox gear to China on fair, reasonable and non-discriminatory terms, interoperability, protection of third-party competitively sensitive information; stated to run six years from effectiveness, with an application-for-release process. SAMR has not said which condition was breached.市场监管总局：2024 年 12 月 9 日立案；2025 年 9 月 15 日初步认定英伟达违反《反垄断法》及 2020 年决定（关于附条件批准收购 Mellanox 的公告）。2020 年的条件属行为性：不得将 GPU 与 Mellanox 网络设备搭售、不得设不合理条款、以公平合理且无歧视的条件持续向中国供应 GPU 加速器与 Mellanox 设备、保证互操作性、保护第三方具竞争敏感性的信息；声明自生效起六年有效，并设有申请解除程序。市场监管总局未说明被违反的是哪一条。
  • Remedies available under the AML: fines of 1–10% of the previous year's sales (China revenue ~$17bn in FY2025 by billing location, $25bn by customer headquarters), orders to stop or modify conduct, renewed compliance obligations; structural remedies are possible in theory but none has been proposed publicly. Analysts (Omdia) floated a requirement to sell China products without Mellanox technology.《反垄断法》下可用的救济：处以上一年度销售额 1–10% 的罚款（2025 财年按开票地计中国营收约 $17bn，按客户总部计约 $25bn）、责令停止或改正行为、重申合规义务；结构性救济理论上可行，但未见公开提出。分析机构（Omdia）提出过一种可能：要求其销售不含 Mellanox 技术的中国产品。
  • CAC: the 31 Jul 2025 notice is a 约谈 requiring Nvidia to explain H20 "vulnerability/backdoor" risks and submit evidence. No finding or sanction published. The practical effect was the informal discouragement of H20 and 6000D purchases.网信办：2025 年 7 月 31 日的通告是一次约谈，要求英伟达说明 H20 的"漏洞/后门"风险并提交证据。未公布任何认定或处罚。实际效果是非正式地劝阻了 H20 与 6000D 的采购。

Gap: our searches through Oct 2026 found no final SAMR decision and no CAC follow-up document; a Sep 2026 secondary summary says none existed by 18 Aug 2026.存疑：截至 2026 年 10 月的检索未发现市场监管总局的最终决定，也未见网信办的后续文件；一份 2026 年 9 月的二手综述称截至 2026 年 8 月 18 日仍无。

  • Xinhua · 2020 conditions2020 年条件 · 15 Sep 2025 state media官媒
  • Reuters via Calcalist · fine range, condition list罚款区间、条件清单 · 15 Sep 2025 press媒体
  • CNA/Reuters · supply-to-China condition, Omdia remedy view对华供应条件、Omdia 救济观点 · 15 Sep 2025 press媒体
  • CAC notice · 31 Jul 2025 official官方
  • Status check · weak较弱 · 7 Sep 2026 secondary二手

C9 What do Chinese anti-dumping or antitrust probes into Nvidia mean for my purchasing contracts?中国对英伟达的反倾销或反垄断调查，对我的采购合同意味着什么？ expert专家

The antitrust case could end in a fine or in rules on how Nvidia bundles GPUs with its networking gear, which may change what configurations you can buy; the anti-dumping probe does not touch Nvidia products. The bigger contract risk is the import gate, not the probes.

反垄断案可能以罚款告终，或对英伟达如何将 GPU 与其网络设备搭售作出规定，从而改变你能买到的配置；反倾销调查不涉及英伟达产品。更大的合同风险在于进口闸口，而非这些调查。

  • The anti-dumping case (13 Sep 2025) covers US-origin commodity interface ICs and gate-driver ICs (Texas Instruments, onsemi class), not GPUs. Nothing to change in a GPU contract.反倾销案（2025 年 9 月 13 日）针对美国原产的通用接口集成电路与栅极驱动集成电路（德州仪器、onsemi 之类），不涉及 GPU。GPU 合同无需改动。
  • The anti-discrimination probe (MOFCOM Ann. 50) targets US government measures; its outcome would be "corresponding measures" against the US, which could in principle include import restrictions. No ruling found.反歧视调查（商务部第 50 号公告）针对美国政府措施；其结果将是对美国的"相应措施"，原则上可能包括进口限制。未见裁定。
  • SAMR: the 2020 conditions required Nvidia to keep supplying GPUs and Mellanox networking to China on FRAND terms and not to tie them. Possible outcomes that touch a buyer: an order to offer GPUs and InfiniBand/NVLink-adjacent networking separately, a China-specific configuration, or supply commitments. A fine would not change contracts directly.市场监管总局：2020 年条件要求英伟达以 FRAND（公平、合理、无歧视）条件持续向中国供应 GPU 与 Mellanox 网络设备，且不得搭售。可能触及买方的结果：责令将 GPU 与 InfiniBand/NVLink 相关网络设备分开提供、设中国专属配置，或作出供应承诺。罚款不会直接改变合同。
  • Practical contract terms worth having (our reading, not legal advice): delivery conditional on both US licence and PRC import approval (NDRC/customs); price and quantity adjustment if approvals are capped; a clause for regulator-ordered configuration changes; an exit if the product is pulled (as the RTX Pro 6000D was discontinued after the CAC stop order).值得写入的实务合同条款（我们的解读，非法律意见）：交付以同时取得美国许可证与中国进口批准（发改委/海关）为条件；若批准受限则调整价格与数量；设监管机构要求变更配置的条款；若产品被撤出则设退出机制（如 RTX Pro 6000D 在网信办停单后即停产）。
Sources来源5
  • MOFCOM spokesperson · 19 Sep 2025 official官方
  • AP · products and companies affected by the anti-dumping probe反倾销调查涉及的产品与企业 · 14 Sep 2025 press媒体
  • MOFCOM Ann. 50 of 2025 official官方

C10 If I help a Chinese customer buy a US chip lawfully, could I break a Chinese rule or a "buy domestic" instruction instead?如果我合法地帮助中国客户购买美国芯片，会不会反而违反某项中国规则或"买国产"指示？ expert专家

Yes, in two ways: the shipment can be stopped at customs or by the NDRC gate even with a valid US licence, and if your customer is a state-funded data center the purchase itself breaks Beijing's domestic-only instruction. The exposure falls mostly on the Chinese buyer; yours is commercial (stranded stock, unpaid invoices) unless you also trip the Anti-Foreign Sanctions Law by refusing Chinese customers to comply with US rules.

会，有两种方式：即便持有有效的美国许可证，货物仍可能在海关或发改委闸口被拦下；若你的客户是国家资助数据中心，采购本身便违反北京的仅用国产指示。风险主要落在中国买方身上；你的风险是商业性的（滞销库存、未付货款）——除非你为遵守美国规则而拒绝中国客户，从而触犯《反外国制裁法》。

  • Import gate: Nvidia's H200 licences covered 75,000 each for ByteDance and Tencent; ~10,000 each got in by Aug 2026. H20 inventory could not be sold at all. Customs told agents H200 was "not permitted" in Jan 2026. None of this is a rule you can read; it is applied at the border and through NDRC approvals.进口闸口：英伟达的 H200 许可覆盖字节跳动与腾讯各 75,000 颗；到 2026 年 8 月各约 10,000 颗入境。H20 库存则完全无法销售。海关在 2026 年 1 月告知代理，H200"不予放行"。这些都不是可供查阅的规则；它在边境并经发改委审批中执行。
  • Buy-domestic: new state-funded data centers must use domestic AI chips; projects under 30% complete must remove foreign chips or cancel purchases (Nov 2025). Shanghai's guideline asked for >50% domestic in city computing centers by 2025. Selling a US chip into such a project means the buyer breaches its instruction and the contract may be unwound.买国产：新建国家资助数据中心须用国产 AI 芯片；完工不足 30% 的项目须拆除外国芯片或取消采购（2025 年 11 月）。上海的指引要求 2025 年前城市算力中心国产占比 >50%。向此类项目出售美国芯片，意味着买方违反其指示，合同可能被解除。
  • Anti-Foreign Sanctions Law (2021; implementing provisions 23 Mar 2025) and the Blocking Rules (2021): a company that refuses Chinese customers because of a foreign measure can face claims and countermeasures. The Unreliable Entity List can bar a foreign firm from China trade. So a distributor that screens out Chinese parties to satisfy BIS has a theoretical Chinese-law exposure; no case against a chip distributor has been found.《反外国制裁法》（2021 年；实施规定 2025 年 3 月 23 日）与《阻断办法》（2021 年）：企业若因为某项外国措施而拒绝中国客户，可能面临索赔与反制。不可靠实体清单可禁止外国企业在华贸易。因此，为满足 BIS 而筛除中国方的分销商，在中国法下存在理论上的风险；未发现针对芯片分销商的案例。
  • The 16 Oct 2026 draft export controls on AI technology run the other way (technology leaving China) and would not catch an import of US chips.2026 年 10 月 16 日的 AI 技术出口管制草案方向相反（技术流出中国），不会触及美国芯片的进口。
  • Nvidia's own framing: it needs a product that passes "both the USG and the Chinese government".英伟达自己的表述：它需要一款能通过"美国政府与中国政府双方"的产品。
Sources来源7
  • Reuters · 5 Nov 2025 press媒体
  • SCMP · Shanghai >50% guideline上海 >50% 指引 · 17 Aug 2025 press媒体
  • Asia Times · 29 Sep 2026 press媒体
  • Nvidia 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026 official官方

C11 Does China's Export Control Law (2020), the Unreliable Entity List or the Anti-Foreign Sanctions Law create a conflict-of-laws exposure for a Chinese subsidiary that complies with a US "is-informed" letter?中国《出口管制法》（2020）、不可靠实体清单或《反外国制裁法》，是否会让遵守美国"告知函"的中国子公司面临法律冲突风险？ expert专家

The AFSL and the Blocking Rules do; the Export Control Law does not (it governs exports from China, not compliance with US rules). A PRC subsidiary that cuts off a Chinese customer to honour a US letter can be sued in China and can be reported under the Blocking Rules, though no such case against a chip distributor has been found.

《反外国制裁法》与《阻断办法》会；《出口管制法》不会（它管的是从中国出口，而非对美国规则的遵守）。为履行美国告知函而切断某中国客户的中国子公司，可能在中国被起诉，并可依《阻断办法》被举报——尽管未发现针对芯片分销商的此类案例。

  • Export Control Law (1 Dec 2020), Dual-Use Items Regulations (1 Dec 2024), 2026 catalogue (1 Jan 2026): control outbound items and technology. Relevant only if the subsidiary re-exports chips, compute or know-how out of China, or sends rare-earth-bearing items abroad (MOFCOM licence). Art. 48-type retaliation clauses exist but have not been used against a chip distributor that we found.《出口管制法》（2020 年 12 月 1 日）、《两用物项出口管制条例》（2024 年 12 月 1 日）、2026 年管制清单（2026 年 1 月 1 日）：管制出境的物项与技术。只有在子公司将芯片、算力或技术诀窍再出口出中国，或将含稀土的物项运往境外（需商务部许可证）时才相关。第 48 条式的反制条款存在，但据我们所见未曾用于芯片分销商。
  • Anti-Foreign Sanctions Law (10 Jun 2021) and Implementing Provisions (23 Mar 2025): art. 12 bars organisations and individuals from implementing or assisting foreign "discriminatory restrictive measures"; a Chinese party harmed may sue for an injunction and damages in a PRC court. Complying with a BIS is-informed letter by refusing a Chinese customer is the paradigm case. Enforcement to date has been against foreign defence firms and officials, not distributors.《反外国制裁法》（2021 年 6 月 10 日）与实施规定（2025 年 3 月 23 日）：第 12 条禁止组织和个人执行或协助外国的"歧视性限制措施"；受损的中国方可在中国法院提起禁令与损害赔偿之诉。为遵守 BIS 告知函而拒绝中国客户，正是典型情形。迄今的执法针对外国国防企业与官员，而非分销商。
  • MOFCOM Blocking Rules (Jan 2021): Chinese persons must report foreign measures that bar normal trade within 30 days; MOFCOM can issue a prohibition order; parties that comply with the blocked foreign measure can be sued. No prohibition order has ever been issued (as far as found).商务部《阻断办法》（2021 年 1 月）：中国主体须在 30 日内报告阻碍正常贸易的外国措施；商务部可发布禁令；遵守被阻断外国措施的一方可被起诉。据查从未发布过禁令。
  • Unreliable Entity List (19 Sep 2020 provisions): MOFCOM can bar a foreign entity from China trade and investment for "discriminatory measures" against Chinese parties. Additions in 2025 targeted US defence and some technology firms; no chip distributor found.不可靠实体清单（2020 年 9 月 19 日规定）：商务部可因针对中国方的"歧视性措施"而禁止外国实体在华贸易与投资。2025 年的新增针对美国国防与部分科技企业；未见芯片分销商。
  • Practical posture seen in filings: Nvidia and AMD disclose the US letters and the PRC restrictions side by side and do not report any AFSL claim. The exposure is real on paper and so far latent.申报文件中可见的实务姿态：英伟达与 AMD 将美国告知函与中国的限制并列披露，且未报告任何《反外国制裁法》索赔。该风险在纸面上真实存在，但至今潜伏未发。

Gap: no PRC court decision applying the AFSL to export-control compliance by a chip seller was found.存疑：未发现有中国法院判决将《反外国制裁法》适用于芯片卖方的出口管制合规行为。

  • Conventus Law · counter-sanctions overview反制措施概览 law firm律所

C12 What do China's own export controls on gallium, germanium, antimony and rare earths (2023–2025, and the Oct 2025 expansion) require of a Hong Kong or Singapore trader re-exporting those materials, and what licence from MOFCOM?中国自身对镓、锗、锑与稀土的出口管制（2023—2025，以及 2025 年 10 月的扩展），对再出口这些材料的香港或新加坡贸易商有何要求？需要商务部的何种许可证？ detail细节

For material leaving mainland China, the Chinese exporter needs a MOFCOM dual-use export licence and must declare the true final destination; the HK or Singapore trader needs nothing from MOFCOM today, but must not on-ship to a US military user. The 9 Oct 2025 rule that would have made the overseas trader itself apply to MOFCOM (if the goods contain ≥0.1% Chinese rare earths) is suspended until 10 Nov 2026.

对于离开中国内地的材料，中国出口商须取得商务部两用物项出口许可证，并申报真实最终目的地；香港或新加坡贸易商今日无需向商务部申请任何许可，但不得转运给美国军事用户。2025 年 10 月 9 日本应要求境外贸易商自行向商务部申请（若货物含 ≥0.1% 中国稀土）的规则，已暂停至 2026 年 11 月 10 日。

Ann. 62: technology and "substantial assistance" by Chinese persons abroad第 61 号公告：境外"指定出口经营者"在从任何非中国法域出口以下物项前须取得商务部许可证——含 ≥0.1% 中国原产受控稀土、或以中国稀土技术制造的境外物项；≤14 nm 逻辑 / ≥256 层存储器的研发及军用 AI 逐案审查；原则上拒绝军事用户及列名主体（含 50% 持股子公司）；境外经营者须向下一接收方传递《合规声明》。第 62 号公告：中国人在境外提供的技术与"实质性协助"Suspended 7 Nov 2025 → 10 Nov 2026 (Ann. 70 of 2025)自 2025 年 11 月 7 日暂停 → 2026 年 11 月 10 日（2025 年第 70 号公告）
  • Note on 境外: MOFCOM's term covers Hong Kong, Macau and Taiwan, so a HK trader is an "overseas" operator for Ann. 61 purposes.关于"境外"：商务部该术语涵盖香港、澳门与台湾，故香港贸易商在第 61 号公告意义上属"境外"经营者。
  • Licence mechanics for the Chinese exporter: dual-use export licence via MOFCOM's system (ecomp.mofcom.gov.cn), documents per art. 16 of the Dual-Use Regulations, Chinese-language version controlling. Under Ann. 61 (when live) the foreign operator applies directly or via an agent.中国出口商的许可办理：通过商务部系统（ecomp.mofcom.gov.cn）申领两用物项出口许可证，按《两用物项出口管制条例》第 16 条备齐文件，以中文版本为准。在第 61 号公告（生效时）下，境外经营者可直接或经代理人申请。
  • The 1 Nov 2025 US fact sheet says China will issue general licences for rare earths, gallium, germanium, antimony and graphite for US end users; China had not confirmed this as of mid-Nov 2025 (gap: no later confirmation found).2025 年 11 月 1 日美方情况说明称，中国将就稀土、镓、锗、锑与石墨为美国最终用户发放一般许可；截至 2025 年 11 月中，中国未予确认（存疑：未发现更晚的确认）。

C13 Could China's own export controls on rare earths or gallium be used against US chip supply, and what is the current state?中国自身对稀土或镓的出口管制，能否用于打击美国芯片供应？目前状况如何？ expert专家

They already were: the Dec 2024 ban on gallium, germanium and antimony to the US and the Oct 2025 rare-earth package were direct replies to US chip rules. Both are suspended until late November 2026 under the Trump–Xi truce; the April 2025 rare-earth licensing and all earlier licence requirements still apply.

已经用过了：2024 年 12 月对美禁运镓、锗、锑，以及 2025 年 10 月的稀土一揽子，都是对美国芯片规则的直接回应。两者在特朗普—习近平休战下暂停至 2026 年 11 月下旬；2025 年 4 月的稀土许可要求及更早的全部许可要求仍然适用。

  • Why it bites chips: gallium (GaN, GaAs), germanium (optics, SiGe), antimony, rare-earth magnets and sputtering targets feed fabs, lithography tools and data-center hardware. Ann. 61 singled out ≤14 nm logic and ≥256-layer memory production and "AI with potential military uses" for case-by-case review.为何击中芯片：镓（GaN、GaAs）、锗（光学、SiGe）、锑、稀土磁体与溅射靶材，供给晶圆厂、光刻设备与数据中心硬件。第 61 号公告特别点名 ≤14 nm 逻辑与 ≥256 层存储器生产，以及"具潜在军事用途的 AI"，列入逐案审查。
  • Sequence: US SME rule (7 Oct 2022) → Ga/Ge licences (Jul 2023); US rule (17 Oct 2023) → graphite (20 Oct 2023); US rule (2 Dec 2024) → US ban (3 Dec 2024); US tariffs (Apr 2025) → 7 rare earths (4 Apr 2025); US Affiliates Rule (29 Sep 2025) → Oct 9 package with its own 50%-affiliate clause.顺序：美国半导体制造设备规则（2022 年 10 月 7 日）→ 镓/锗许可（2023 年 7 月）；美国规则（2023 年 10 月 17 日）→ 石墨（2023 年 10 月 20 日）；美国规则（2024 年 12 月 2 日）→ 对美禁运（2024 年 12 月 3 日）；美国关税（2025 年 4 月）→ 7 种稀土（2025 年 4 月 4 日）；美国关联方规则（2025 年 9 月 29 日）→ 10 月 9 日一揽子，自带 50% 关联方条款。
  • Current state: Ann. 70 (7 Nov 2025) suspends the Oct 9 package to 10 Nov 2026; Ann. 72 (9 Nov 2025) suspends the US ban to 27 Nov 2026; the US suspended its Affiliates Rule to 9 Nov 2026. Treasury said in Sep 2026 the truce is extended to 10 Jan 2027, but no MOFCOM or Federal Register notice extending the suspensions had appeared by 27 Oct 2026 (gap). China's new draft AI-technology export controls (16 Oct 2026) and Taiwan's planned chip controls show the truce fraying.目前状况：第 70 号公告（2025 年 11 月 7 日）将 10 月 9 日一揽子暂停至 2026 年 11 月 10 日；第 72 号公告（2025 年 11 月 9 日）将对美禁运暂停至 2026 年 11 月 27 日；美国将其关联方规则暂停至 2026 年 11 月 9 日。美国财政部在 2026 年 9 月称休战延长至 2027 年 1 月 10 日，但截至 2026 年 10 月 27 日，未见商务部或《联邦公报》延长暂停的通知（存疑）。中国新的 AI 技术出口管制草案（2026 年 10 月 16 日）与台湾拟议的芯片管制，显示休战正在松动。
  • Did they bite? Customs data show de facto bans to the US and the Netherlands from Aug 2023; US users saw shortages and routed via third countries (Reuters).是否奏效？海关数据显示自 2023 年 8 月起对美国与荷兰实际上形成禁运；美国用户出现短缺，并经第三国迂回采购（路透）。

C14 How do China's rare earth and gallium export limits bargain against US chip controls in current talks?在当前谈判中，中国的稀土与镓出口限制如何与美国的芯片管制讨价还价？ expert专家

They are the main card. At Busan (30 Oct 2025) China paused its Oct 9 rare-earth package for a year in exchange for the US pausing its Affiliates Rule for a year; both pauses lapse around 10 Nov 2026, which is why that date matters more than any chip rule.

它们是主牌。在釜山（2025 年 10 月 30 日），中国将其 10 月 9 日稀土一揽子暂停一年，以换取美国将关联方规则暂停一年；两项暂停都在 2026 年 11 月 10 日前后到期，这正是该日期比任何芯片规则都重要的原因。

  • The trade: MOFCOM's 31 Oct 2025 release pairs the two suspensions explicitly. The US fact sheet (1 Nov 2025) adds Chinese general licences for rare earths, gallium, germanium, antimony and graphite for US end users; China did not confirm the general licences.交易：商务部 2025 年 10 月 31 日的通稿将两项暂停明确对应。美方情况说明（2025 年 11 月 1 日）补充称，中国将就稀土、镓、锗、锑与石墨为美国最终用户发放一般许可；中国未确认这些一般许可。
  • What China kept: the April 2025 licensing of seven heavy rare earths and magnets, and all 2023–Feb 2025 licence requirements. So the leverage stays loaded; only the escalation is paused.中国保留了什么：2025 年 4 月对 7 种重稀土及磁体的许可，以及 2023 年至 2025 年 2 月的全部许可要求。因此筹码仍然上膛，暂停的只是升级。
  • What the US kept: every chip rule. The H200 case-by-case policy (15 Jan 2026) came after Busan as a US concession on chips; Blackwell stays denied and "survived" the Sep 2026 summit.美国保留了什么：全部芯片规则。H200 逐案政策（2026 年 1 月 15 日）在釜山之后出台，是美方在芯片上的让步；Blackwell 仍被拒，并在 2026 年 9 月的峰会中"存续"。
  • Next dates: 9–10 Nov 2026 (both suspensions end on paper), 27 Nov 2026 (US gallium ban suspension ends), 10 Jan 2027 (announced truce end). Sep 2026 reports of an extension to 10 Jan 2027 lack a formal notice as of 27 Oct 2026.接下来的日期：2026 年 11 月 9—10 日（两项暂停在纸面上到期）、2026 年 11 月 27 日（美国镓禁运暂停到期）、2027 年 1 月 10 日（宣布的休战终点）。2026 年 9 月关于延长至 2027 年 1 月 10 日的报道，截至 2026 年 10 月 27 日仍无正式通知。
  • Signals of fraying: China's draft AI-tech export controls (16 Oct 2026), SAMR's Nvidia case still open, MOFCOM's anti-discrimination probe.松动的信号：中国的 AI 技术出口管制草案（2026 年 10 月 16 日）、市场监管总局对英伟达的案件仍未结、商务部的反歧视调查。
