C15 What do Chinese state policies say about domestic chip use in new state-funded data centers?中国的国家政策对新建国家资助数据中心使用国产芯片有何规定？ detail细节

Since November 2025, new state-funded data centers must use only Chinese AI chips, and projects less than 30% built must pull foreign chips or cancel the orders. Before that, Shanghai and other hubs asked for more than half domestic.

自 2025 年 11 月起，新建国家资助数据中心必须只用国产 AI 芯片，完工不足 30% 的项目须拆除外国芯片或取消订单。在此之前，上海等枢纽要求国产过半。

  • 2024 (Shanghai): guideline for >50% domestic chips in city intelligent computing centers by 2025.2024 年（上海）：指引要求 2025 年前城市智能算力中心国产芯片占比 >50%。
  • ~Aug 2025: public computing hubs asked to source >50% of chips domestically.约 2025 年 8 月：公共算力枢纽被要求从国内采购 >50% 的芯片。
  • Nov 2025 (Reuters, unnamed sources): all new state-funded data center projects must use domestic AI chips; projects <30% complete must remove installed foreign chips or cancel planned purchases; projects further along reviewed case by case. No published text.2025 年 11 月（路透，匿名消息源）：所有新建国家资助数据中心项目须用国产 AI 芯片；完工 <30% 的项目须拆除已装外国芯片或取消拟议采购；进度更靠后的项目逐案审查。无公开文本。
  • Procurement: Huawei and Cambricon are the only vendors on the government-approved AI hardware list.采购：华为与寒武纪是政府核准 AI 硬件清单上仅有的供应商。
  • Effect in numbers: a Bloomberg Intelligence survey puts the domestic share of Chinese firms' compute budgets at ~30% now and 46% in the next 12 months; TrendForce projects domestic chips at nearly 90% of the high-end market in 2026 (definitions vary).数字上的影响：彭博行业研究的一项调查显示，中国企业算力预算中的国产占比目前约 30%，未来 12 个月将达 46%；TrendForce 预计 2026 年国产芯片将占高端市场近 90%（口径不一）。

Gap: no ministry document; the 30% threshold and the "state-funded" scope rest on Reuters.存疑：无部委文件；30% 门槛与"国家资助"范围均依据路透。

C16 What rules does Beijing set on domestic chip share in state-funded data centers?北京对国家资助数据中心的国产芯片占比设定了什么规则？ expert专家

The rule is 100% domestic for new state-funded projects (Nov 2025), with a retrofit duty for projects under 30% built; earlier city-level targets were >50%. There is no published national text, so "rule" here means an unpublished directive applied through funding and procurement.

规则是新建国家资助项目 100% 国产（2025 年 11 月），完工不足 30% 的项目负有改造义务；更早的市级目标为 >50%。没有公开的全国性文本，故此处的"规则"指通过资金与采购执行的未公开指令。

Same facts as C15. Added for the analyst: the policy is enforced through (a) the state procurement list (Huawei, Cambricon), (b) NDRC approval of any foreign-chip purchase, and (c) the reported condition that H200 approvals come with domestic-chip purchase quotas (Bloomberg, Jan 2026; bundling ratio unconfirmed). Private hyperscalers are not covered by the state-funded rule but are the ones being steered by the CAC and NDRC. Watch for: a written MIIT/NDRC measure, and whether the 30% threshold is applied to projects already past it.

事实同 C15。为分析师补充：该政策通过以下方式执行——（a）国家采购清单（华为、寒武纪）、（b）发改委对任何外国芯片采购的审批、（c）据报 H200 批准附带国产芯片采购配额的条件（彭博，2026 年 1 月；捆绑比例未经证实）。民营云厂商不在国家资助规则范围内，却正是被网信办与发改委引导的对象。关注：是否出台成文的工信部/发改委措施，以及 30% 门槛是否适用于已超过该进度的项目。

C17 How much has China's national chip investment fund spent in 2025 and 2026, and on what?中国国家芯片投资基金在 2025 与 2026 年花了多少钱？投向何处？ detail细节

Big Fund III (RMB 344bn, registered May 2024) committed about RMB 220bn to three sub-funds in early 2025 and only started placing money in companies from September 2025: equipment, materials, advanced packaging and AI chips, in deals of RMB 100m to a few hundred million each. A total deployed figure has not been published.

大基金三期（注册资本 3,440 亿元人民币，2024 年 5 月注册）于 2025 年初向三只子基金承诺约 2,200 亿元，直到 2025 年 9 月才开始投向企业：设备、材料、先进封装与 AI 芯片，单笔从 1 亿元到数亿元不等。累计投放总额尚未公布。

  • Structure: registered capital RMB 344bn (the six big state banks among the LPs). Jan 2025: first commitments, RMB 160bn to two sub-funds (Huaxin Dingxin RMB 93.09bn, Guotou Jixin RMB 71.07bn), plus the National AI Industry Investment Fund (RMB 60.06bn, set up 17 Jan 2025, 13-year life). Bloomberg (Jun 2025): the fund had raised only part of the RMB 344bn and would target chokepoints (lithography, EDA) with longer holds.结构：注册资本 3,440 亿元（六大国有银行在有限合伙人之列）。2025 年 1 月：首批承诺，向两只子基金投入 1,600 亿元（Huaxin Dingxin 930.9 亿元、Guotou Jixin 710.7 亿元），外加国家人工智能产业投资基金（600.6 亿元，2025 年 1 月 17 日设立，存续 13 年）。彭博（2025 年 6 月）：该基金仅募得 3,440 亿元中的一部分，将以更长的持有期瞄准卡脖子环节（光刻、EDA）。
  • Disclosed company deals: Sep 2025 Tuojing Jianke (3D bonding/HBM stacking equipment), ≤RMB 450m for ~12.7%; Oct 2025 Nantong Crystal (photomask substrates), RMB 100m for 25%; Dec 2025 AKM Meadville (IC substrates); 2026 Anhui Juhe Microelectronics (chiplet packaging, 18%), Tiansui Xinyuan (RMB 300m, 31.58%), Changfei Quartz; AI chips via the AI fund: YunSilicon (interconnect), TsingMicro (compute-in-memory), Beijing Aijieko Xin (AI accelerators), Shanghai Oriental Chip Computing. May–Jun 2026: reports the Big Fund is in talks to lead DeepSeek's first round at ~$45bn valuation (unconfirmed).已披露的企业投资：2025 年 9 月 Tuojing Jianke（3D 键合/HBM 堆叠设备），≤4.5 亿元取得约 12.7%；2025 年 10 月 Nantong Crystal（光掩模基板），1 亿元取得 25%；2025 年 12 月 AKM Meadville（IC 载板）；2026 年 Anhui Juhe Microelectronics（芯粒封装，18%）、Tiansui Xinyuan（3 亿元，31.58%）、Changfei Quartz；经 AI 基金投向 AI 芯片：YunSilicon（互连）、TsingMicro（存算一体）、Beijing Aijieko Xin（AI 加速器）、Shanghai Oriental Chip Computing。2026 年 5—6 月：有报道称大基金正洽谈领投 DeepSeek 首轮融资，估值约 $45bn（未经证实）。
  • Meanwhile Big Fund I and II are selling down (SMIC, NSIG, Biwin and others) in 2025–26; proceeds go to their own LPs, not to Fund III.与此同时，大基金一期与二期在 2025—26 年减持（中芯国际、NSIG、Biwin 等）；所得归其各自有限合伙人，而非三期。
  • Cumulative committed across all three phases: >RMB 680bn.三期累计承诺：>6,800 亿元。

Gap: no official total for 2025 or 2026 disbursements; the amounts above are from company filings and press.存疑：2025 或 2026 年的投放无官方总额；上述金额来自公司公告与媒体。

D

The hubs: Hong Kong, Singapore, UAE, Malaysia中转枢纽：香港、新加坡、阿联酋、马来西亚

Each hub's own licensing regime and how strict enforcement is; the UAE / Saudi deals (G42, Humain) and their US security conditions; Chinese-owned data centers abroad renting compute; Malaysia's 2025 permit; headquarters / ownership rules.

各枢纽自身的许可制度及执法力度；阿联酋／沙特的交易（G42、Humain）及其美方安全条件；境外中资数据中心出租算力；马来西亚 2025 年许可；总部／所有权规则。

D1 Why are Hong Kong, Singapore and the UAE mentioned so often in chip news?为何香港、新加坡与阿联酋在芯片新闻里被频繁提及？ basic基础

They are where chips change hands on paper or in transit. Hong Kong is legally China under US rules but is the region's warehouse; Singapore is where Nvidia bills a fifth of its revenue while shipping almost nothing there; the UAE is the one place outside the West that the US lets buy top chips in bulk, on condition that China gets no access.

它们是芯片在纸面上或转运中易手之地。按美国规则，香港法律上属中国，却是该地区的仓库；新加坡是英伟达开票计入五分之一营收、而几乎不向其发货之地；阿联酋则是西方之外唯一获美国允许大批购买顶级芯片之处，条件是中国不得获得访问权。

  • Hong Kong: Nvidia's supply chain is "warehoused in and distributed from Hong Kong"; HK handled more than half of China's $239bn chip imports in Jan–May 2026; US indictments name HK as a route (Operation Gatekeeper, the SANDKYAN relabelling case).香港：英伟达供应链"在香港仓储并由香港分发"；2026 年 1—5 月，香港处理了中国 2,390 亿美元芯片进口的一半以上；美国起诉书点名香港为通道（Gatekeeper 行动、SANDKYAN 换标案）。
  • Singapore: 18% of Nvidia FY2025 revenue by billing location, under 2% by shipment; 22% in Q2 FY2026 with Nvidia saying over 99% of controlled data-center revenue billed there was for US-based customers. The Feb 2025 fraud case (~$390M of servers) and the Megaspeed probe keep it in the news.新加坡：按开票地计占英伟达 2025 财年营收的 18%，按发货计不足 2%；2026 财年第二季度占 22%，英伟达称在该地开票的受控数据中心营收中逾 99% 面向美国客户。2025 年 2 月的欺诈案（约 3.9 亿美元服务器）与 Megaspeed 调查使其持续见诸报端。
  • UAE: May 2025 US–UAE AI Acceleration Partnership and the 5 GW campus; Nov 2025 approval of up to 35,000 GB300-equivalents each for G42 and Saudi Humain; Jul 2026 move to Country Group A:5 with license-free chips for G42, Core42 and eight US hyperscalers.阿联酋：2025 年 5 月美国—阿联酋 AI 加速伙伴关系与 5 GW 园区；2025 年 11 月批准 G42 与沙特 Humain 各最多 35,000 颗 GB300 等效芯片；2026 年 7 月升入国家组 A:5，G42、Core42 及八家美国云厂商可免许可获取芯片。
  • Malaysia and Thailand show up in the smuggling cases as the next hop; Malaysia's imports under HS 847150 ("processing units") went from $1.73bn (2023) to $13.80bn (2025).马来西亚与泰国在走私案中作为下一跳出现；马来西亚 HS 847150（"处理器"）项下进口从 17.3 亿美元（2023）增至 138.0 亿美元（2025）。

  • Commerce · 15 May 2025 official官方
  • 91 FR 43034 · 14 Jul 2026 official官方

D2 Can a Hong Kong or Singapore company legally buy Nvidia chips and resell them, or are these hubs now treated like China?香港或新加坡公司能否合法购买英伟达芯片并转售，还是这些枢纽如今被当作中国对待？ basic基础

Hong Kong is China for US purposes: a controlled chip needs a US licence to go there at all. Singapore is not: a Singapore company can buy controlled chips without a licence and resell them to buyers outside China, but not to China, Hong Kong, Macau or any China-headquartered company, and not to anyone it has reason to think will divert.

就美国规则而言，香港即中国：受控芯片运往香港本身就需要美国许可证。新加坡则不是：新加坡公司无需许可即可购买受控芯片，并转售给中国以外的买家，但不得售予中国内地、香港、澳门或任何总部位于中国的公司，也不得售予其有理由认为会转移货物的任何人。

  • Hong Kong: treated as part of China since 2020; Nvidia and AMD write "China (including Hong Kong and Macau)". 3A090 chips need a licence; reexport from HK into the mainland faces presumption of denial. HK's own licensing (Cap. 60) is separate and follows the multilateral lists, not 3A090 (see D4).香港：自 2020 年起被视为中国的一部分；英伟达与 AMD 写作"中国（含香港与澳门）"。3A090 芯片需许可证；从香港再出口至内地适用推定拒绝。香港自身的许可制度（第 60 章）另成一套，遵循多边清单，而非 3A090（见 D4）。
  • Singapore: not in Country Groups D:1/D:4/D:5, and the worldwide licence of the Diffusion Rule is not enforced. Exceptions: (a) buyers headquartered in China/Macau or with a parent there need a licence wherever they sit (BIS guidance 31 May 2026), with presumption of denial for new sales; (b) knowledge of, or red flags for, diversion (BIS 13 May 2025 guidance). Singapore law adds permits for strategic goods and true declarations for every transshipment; misdeclaring the end user is prosecuted as fraud.新加坡：不在国家组 D:1/D:4/D:5 之列，扩散规则的全球许可要求亦未执行。例外：（a）总部位于中国/澳门、或母公司在此的买家，无论身处何地均需许可证（BIS 2026 年 5 月 31 日指引），新销售适用推定拒绝；（b）明知转移、或存在转移的危险信号（BIS 2025 年 5 月 13 日指引）。新加坡法律另要求战略物项许可，以及每次转运的真实申报；谎报最终用户将以欺诈罪起诉。
  • Resale into China from either hub is "not lawful" under the EAR: reexports from third countries into China are presumption of denial (91 FR 1684), and the cases (Supermicro co-founder ~$2.5bn, Earthmade >$300M, Gatekeeper $160M) all ran through these hubs.从任一枢纽转售进入中国，依 EAR 均"不合法"：自第三国再出口至中国适用推定拒绝（91 FR 1684），而相关案件（Supermicro 联合创始人约 25 亿美元、Earthmade >$300M、Gatekeeper 1.6 亿美元）都经由这些枢纽。
  • Below-threshold parts (RTX PRO 5500, 4090D, 5090D) can be traded to China from either hub without a US licence, subject to end-user screening and Beijing's import gate.低于门槛的产品（RTX PRO 5500、4090D、5090D）可从任一枢纽无需美国许可销往中国，但须经最终用户筛查与北京的进口闸口。
Sources来源6
  • Nvidia 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026 official官方
  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026 official官方
  • BIS press release · 13 May 2025 official官方
  • 91 FR 1684 · 15 Jan 2026 official官方
  • MTI Singapore · 18 Feb 2025 official官方
  • Project research files本站研究文件
D3 Can a company in Singapore buy Nvidia chips and ship them to China?新加坡的公司能购买英伟达芯片并运往中国吗？ basic基础

Buy, yes. Ship controlled chips to China, no: that needs a US licence that is presumed denied, and in Singapore it is prosecuted as fraud or a false declaration. Only chips under the US thresholds can go.

购买，可以。将受控芯片运往中国，不行：这需要一张被推定拒绝的美国许可证，在新加坡还会以欺诈或虚假申报起诉。只有低于美国门槛的芯片可以运。

The Singapore buyer needs no US licence for its own purchase unless it is China-headquartered. Reexporting a 3A090 chip (H100, H200, B200 and the rest) to China, Hong Kong or Macau needs a BIS licence; 91 FR 1684 keeps reexports from third countries under presumption of denial even for H200-class parts (the case-by-case policy applies only to exports from the US). In Singapore, the Feb 2025 case charged three men (later four people and four companies) with fraud and money laundering for lying to Dell and Super Micro about the end users of ~$390M of servers that went to Malaysia; a S$38M house was seized. Megaspeed (Singapore; ~$2bn of Nvidia chips through its Malaysian and Indonesian units) is under Singapore and US investigation. Under-threshold parts such as the RTX PRO 5500 can be shipped, subject to Beijing's gate and Singapore's declaration rules.

新加坡买家为自身采购无需美国许可证，除非其总部在中国。将 3A090 芯片（H100、H200、B200 等）再出口至中国内地、香港或澳门需要 BIS 许可证；91 FR 1684 使自第三国的再出口即便对 H200 级产品也维持推定拒绝（逐案政策仅适用于自美国出口）。在新加坡，2025 年 2 月的案件以欺诈与洗钱起诉三名男子（后扩至四人及四家公司），因其就运往马来西亚的约 3.9 亿美元服务器的最终用户向戴尔与超微谎报；一栋价值 3,800 万新元的住宅被查封。Megaspeed（新加坡；约 20 亿美元英伟达芯片经其马来西亚与印尼实体）正受新加坡与美国调查。RTX PRO 5500 等低于门槛的产品可以发运，但须经北京的闸口与新加坡的申报规则。

D4 Does Hong Kong have its own strategic-commodities control regime that catches AI chips, and does the HK Trade and Industry Department actually require an import/export licence for a server with H100s?香港是否有自己的战略物品管制制度能管到 AI 芯片？香港工业贸易署是否真的要求一台装有 H100 的服务器办理进出口许可证？ basic基础

Hong Kong has a strict licensing regime (Cap. 60 and Cap. 60G), but its control list follows the Wassenaar and other multilateral lists, which do not include the US-only 3A090 entry. So an H100 server needs a TID licence only if it is caught by another list entry, typically the encryption entry 5A002 or the high-performance computer entry, which depends on the server's own classification. Confirm with TID's Classification Section before shipping.

香港有一套严格的许可制度（第 60 章与第 60G 章），但其管制清单遵循瓦森纳等多边清单，不含美国独有的 3A090 条目。因此，一台 H100 服务器只有在被另一条目涵盖时才需工贸署许可证——通常是加密条目 5A002 或高性能计算机条目，取决于服务器自身的归类。发运前请向工贸署归类科确认。

  • Law: Import and Export Ordinance (Cap. 60) s. 6A and the Import and Export (Strategic Commodities) Regulations (Cap. 60G). A licence from the Director-General of Trade and Industry is needed for import, export, re-export and transshipment of every shipment of a listed item; transit is exempt except Schedule 2 items. Schedule 1 was last updated 30 May 2025 to track the multilateral regimes.法律：《进出口条例》（第 60 章）第 6A 条与《进出口（战略物品）规例》（第 60G 章）。列明物品的每一批进口、出口、再出口与转运，均需工业贸易署署长的许可证；过境除附表 2 物品外获豁免。附表 1 最近一次更新为 2025 年 5 月 30 日，以跟随多边机制。
  • Not on the list: 3A090/4A090 are unilateral US ECCNs. Singapore's and Malaysia's regimes say the same of their own lists (Malaysia had to invent "3U090" as an unlisted category). TID publishes "Commonly Not-Controlled Items" lists for ICs and computers.不在清单上：3A090/4A090 是美国单边的 ECCN。新加坡与马来西亚的制度对各自清单亦如是说（马来西亚不得不发明"3U090"作为未列明类别）。工贸署发布针对集成电路与计算机的"通常不受管制物品"清单。
  • What can still catch a GPU server: 5A002 (information security) if the server's crypto is not mass-market decontrolled; 4A003 high-performance computing if above the APP threshold; Customs has prosecuted exports of 5A002 ICs and 3A001 ICs to the mainland without a licence (fines HK$30,000–602,100; forfeiture).仍可能管到 GPU 服务器的条目：5A002（信息安全），若服务器的加密未按大众市场解控；4A003 高性能计算，若超过 APP 门槛；海关曾就未经许可向内地出口 5A002 集成电路与 3A001 集成电路提起公诉（罚款 30,000–602,100 港元；没收）。
  • Penalty: summary conviction HK$500,000 and 2 years; indictment unlimited fine and 7 years, plus forfeiture and loss of licensing facilities.处罚：简易程序定罪罚款 50 万港元、监禁 2 年；公诉程序罚款无上限、监禁 7 年，另加没收与丧失许可便利。
  • Stance: HK "does not implement, nor do we have the legal authority to take action on, unilateral sanctions imposed by other jurisdictions." US rules still bind US-origin items and EAR-subject parties in HK.立场：香港"不实施、也无法律权力就其他法域施加的单边制裁采取行动"。美国规则仍约束香港境内的美国原产物项与受 EAR 管辖的主体。

Gap: no TID classification ruling for Nvidia servers found; whether a given H100/H200 server is 5A002-controlled in HK is a product-specific question.存疑：未发现工贸署对英伟达服务器的归类裁定；某台 H100/H200 服务器在香港是否受 5A002 管制，属产品层面的具体问题。

D5 What licences or end-user checks does Singapore now require for AI chip shipments, and how strict is enforcement there?新加坡如今对 AI 芯片货运要求哪些许可或最终用户核查？当地执法有多严？ detail细节

Singapore has not added AI chips to its own control list and will not enforce US rules for the US. What it does enforce, hard, is honesty: permits and true declarations for every export and transshipment, and fraud charges when a company lies to its supplier about the end user. Expect investigations to be triggered by US tip-offs and to move fast on assets.

新加坡未将 AI 芯片列入自己的管制清单，也不会替美国执行美国规则。它严厉执行的是诚信：每一次出口与转运都要许可与真实申报，企业若就最终用户向供应商撒谎则以欺诈罪起诉。预期调查会由美方线报触发，并迅速查封资产。

  • Singapore's own requirements: Strategic Goods (Control) Act (SGCA) permits for export, transshipment, transit and brokering of listed strategic goods (Wassenaar-based list, Strategic Goods (Control) Order); a WMD catch-all; Regulation of Imports and Exports Regulations reg. 3(1) and Customs Act s. 128 for permits and true declarations on every shipment. Penalties: SGCA first conviction up to S$100,000 or 3× goods value and/or 2 years; false declaration up to S$10,000 and/or 12 months.新加坡自身的要求：《战略物资（管制）法》（SGCA）对列明战略物资的出口、转运、过境与中介要求许可（基于瓦森纳的清单，《战略物资（管制）令》）；一条大规模杀伤性武器（WMD）兜底条款；《进出口管理规例》第 3(1) 条与《海关法》第 128 条要求每批货物的许可与真实申报。处罚：SGCA 首次定罪最高 10 万新元或货值 3 倍，和/或 2 年；虚假申报最高 1 万新元，和/或 12 个月。
  • No AI-chip-specific rule has been introduced (Feb 2025 parliamentary answer; Apr 2025 Customs advisory). The government "will not enforce foreign export regimes" but "will not countenance evasion, deception, false declarations, or even misaccounting," and Customs "works closely" with US counterparts.未出台针对 AI 芯片的专门规则（2025 年 2 月议会答复；2025 年 4 月海关公告）。政府"不会执行外国出口制度"，但"不会容忍规避、欺骗、虚假申报，乃至错误记账"，海关与美方对口单位"密切合作"。
  • The Customs advisory (Apr 2025) asks firms for an internal compliance programme, KYC and end-user screening with red-flag checks (unusual routes, end-use inconsistencies), and legal advice on foreign regimes. These are expectations, not legal duties.海关公告（2025 年 4 月）要求企业建立内部合规计划、KYC 与最终用户筛查并进行危险信号核查（异常路线、最终用途不一致），并就外国制度征询法律意见。这些是期望，而非法律义务。
  • Enforcement record: Feb 2025 fraud charges over ~$390M of Dell/Super Micro servers misdeclared and sent to Malaysia (part of a 22-party probe); Jul 2026 expansion to four companies, a money-laundering charge, a S$38M house seized; Megaspeed under investigation since 2025. No conviction yet.执法记录：2025 年 2 月就约 3.9 亿美元戴尔/超微服务器被谎报并运往马来西亚提起欺诈指控（22 方调查的一部分）；2026 年 7 月扩至四家公司，新增一项洗钱指控，查封一栋价值 3,800 万新元的住宅；Megaspeed 自 2025 年起受调查。尚无定罪。
  • US side: the 31 May 2026 BIS guidance means a Singapore data center owned by a China-headquartered group needs a US licence for new chips (presumption of denial) but may keep using what it has.美国一侧：2026 年 5 月 31 日的 BIS 指引意味着，由总部在中国的集团持有的新加坡数据中心，购买新芯片需美国许可证（推定拒绝），但可继续使用已有设备。

D6 Singapore: what charges were brought in the Feb–Mar 2025 server-fraud case (the Supermicro/Dell servers routed to Malaysia), under which Singapore statute, and what were the sentences or outcomes?新加坡：2025 年 2—3 月的服务器欺诈案（超微/戴尔服务器转运至马来西亚）提起了哪些指控？依据哪部新加坡法律？判决或结果如何？ detail细节

Fraud and money-laundering charges under the Penal Code and the anti-money-laundering law, plus a Computer Misuse Act count, not the Strategic Goods (Control) Act. Four people and four companies are charged; one has pleaded not guilty; no verdict as of October 2026.

依《刑法典》与反洗钱法提起欺诈与洗钱指控，另加一项《滥用电脑法》指控，而非《战略物资（管制）法》。四人及四家公司被起诉；一人作无罪答辩；截至 2026 年 10 月无判决。

  • 27–28 Feb 2025: three men charged (Aaron Woon Guo Jie, Alan Wei Zhaolun of Aperia Group, and Chinese national Li Ming) with fraud for false representations to Dell and Super Micro about the end users of servers; bail granted 13 Mar 2025. Minister Shanmugam: the servers "may contain Nvidia chips," were sent to Malaysia, final destination unknown; part of a probe of 22 individuals and companies. Transactions ~US$390M.2025 年 2 月 27—28 日：三名男子被控欺诈（Aaron Woon Guo Jie、Aperia Group 的 Alan Wei Zhaolun，以及中国籍的 Li Ming），因就服务器最终用户向戴尔与超微作虚假陈述；2025 年 3 月 13 日获准保释。尚穆根部长称，这些服务器"可能含英伟达芯片"，被运往马来西亚，最终目的地不明；属对 22 名个人与公司调查的一部分。交易额约 3.9 亿美元。
  • Jul 2026: a fourth person (Jenny Lim) and four companies charged; a money-laundering charge added; a S$38M (US$42M) bungalow seized and accounts frozen. Wei pleaded not guilty to 11 charges on 6 Jul 2026.2026 年 7 月：第四人（Jenny Lim）与四家公司被起诉；新增一项洗钱指控；查封一栋价值 3,800 万新元（4,200 万美元）的洋房并冻结账户。Wei 于 2026 年 7 月 6 日就 11 项指控作无罪答辩。
  • Statutes: Penal Code cheating/fraud (s. 420 class), Corruption, Drug Trafficking and Other Serious Crimes (Confiscation of Benefits) Act for laundering, Computer Misuse Act (Li). No SGCA charge because the chips are not on Singapore's list (see D7).所依法律：《刑法典》欺骗/欺诈（第 420 条一类）、《贪污、贩毒及其他严重罪行（没收得益）法》用于洗钱、《滥用电脑法》（Li）。因芯片不在新加坡清单上，故无 SGCA 指控（见 D7）。
  • Outcome: none yet; trial dates not found (gap). Expect, as in the US cases, that seizure precedes any verdict.结果：尚无；未查得开庭日期（存疑）。预期如美国各案一样，查封先于任何判决。

  • Reuters · 3 Mar 2025 press媒体

  • Digitimes · 2 Jul 2026 press媒体

D7 Does Singapore's Strategic Goods (Control) Act catch 3A090-class chips by its own list, or only via catch-all (end-use) provisions, and what is the reporting duty of a Singapore freight forwarder?新加坡《战略物资（管制）法》是以其自身清单管到 3A090 级芯片，还是仅通过兜底（最终用途）条款？新加坡货运代理的报告义务是什么？ expert专家

Not by its list: Singapore's list tracks the multilateral regimes, and the government has said in Parliament that US chip controls are unilateral and outside them. The catch-all reaches only WMD end uses. A freight forwarder's duties are the ordinary ones: a permit and a true declaration for every transshipment, and no handling of a shipment it knows or has reason to believe is for a WMD end use.

不是以其清单：新加坡的清单跟随多边机制，政府已在议会表示美国芯片管制属单边、不在其中。兜底条款仅及于 WMD 最终用途。货运代理的义务是常规义务：每次转运都要许可与真实申报，且不得经手其明知或有理由相信用于 WMD 最终用途的货物。

  • List: the Strategic Goods (Control) Order sets the technical specifications; it is aligned with Wassenaar, the Australia Group, MTCR and UN sanctions (MTI, 18 Feb 2025). The US controls on advanced ICs "are unilateral and fall outside of those multilateral regimes" (same source). No 3A090-equivalent entry has been added through Oct 2026 (gap: the latest SGCO amendment was not read in full).清单：《战略物资（管制）令》设定技术规格；与瓦森纳、澳大利亚集团、MTCR 及联合国制裁对齐（新加坡贸工部，2025 年 2 月 18 日）。美国对先进集成电路的管制"属单边，落在这些多边机制之外"（同一来源）。截至 2026 年 10 月未新增 3A090 等效条目（存疑：最新一版 SGCO 修订未通读）。
  • Catch-all: SGCA s. 5 covers goods not on the list where the person knows or has reasonable grounds to believe they are for a WMD end use; there is no "military end use" or "advanced computing" catch-all, unlike Malaysia's Directive 1/2025, which used its own WMD catch-all to reach AI chips.兜底：SGCA 第 5 条涵盖清单外物品，条件是当事人明知或有合理理由相信其用于 WMD 最终用途；不存在"军事最终用途"或"先进计算"的兜底，这与马来西亚第 1/2025 号指令不同——后者用自己的 WMD 兜底条款管到了 AI 芯片。
  • Forwarder duties: (a) permit and declaration for every import, export, transshipment (Regulation of Imports and Exports Regulations reg. 3(1); Customs Act s. 128 for false declarations); (b) SGCA brokering and transshipment permits for listed goods; (c) the Apr 2025 Customs advisory expects an internal compliance programme, KYC and red-flag screening, and notes that handling a shipment with false declarations can itself be an offence. There is no statutory duty to report suspected US-rule diversion, but the 2025 prosecutions show false end-user statements to suppliers are charged as fraud.代理的义务：（a）每次进口、出口、转运都要许可与申报（《进出口管理规例》第 3(1) 条；《海关法》第 128 条管虚假申报）；（b）列明物资的 SGCA 中介与转运许可；（c）2025 年 4 月的海关公告期望企业建立内部合规计划、KYC 与危险信号筛查，并指出经手带虚假申报的货物本身即可能构成犯罪。没有报告疑似违反美国规则之转移的法定义务，但 2025 年的公诉表明，向供应商作虚假最终用户陈述会以欺诈罪起诉。
  • US overlay: a forwarder that handles a reexport to China of a 3A090 item with knowledge is exposed under EAR 764.2(e) regardless of Singapore law.美国叠加层：明知而经手将 3A090 物项再出口至中国的代理，无论新加坡法律如何，均依 EAR 764.2(e) 承担风险。

  • Singapore Customs, strategic goods overview official官方

  • SGCA s. 5 · official text mirror官方文本镜像 official官方

  • MITI Malaysia Directive 1/2025 · for contrast作对照 official官方
D8 Malaysia's 2025 permit requirement for US-origin AI chips: which ministry, what threshold, how it interacts with transshipment through Port Klang and Johor data centers?马来西亚 2025 年对美国原产 AI 芯片的许可要求：哪个部门？什么门槛？如何与经巴生港转运及柔佛数据中心相互作用？ detail细节

MITI's Strategic Trade Secretariat, by Directive 1/2025 of 14 July 2025 under the Strategic Trade Act 2010 s. 12 catch-all: anyone exporting, transshipping or transiting US-origin advanced AI chips must notify 30 days ahead and obtain a Strategic Trade Permit, with the US re-export licence and the maker's classification attached. The thresholds copy 3A090 (TPP ≥ 4,800, or ≥ 1,600 with density ≥ 5.92; the data-center band 2,400–4,800). It applies to every port, Port Klang and Johor included, but not to imports for use in Malaysia.

由国际贸易及工业部（MITI）战略贸易秘书处，依 2025 年 7 月 14 日第 1/2025 号指令、《2010 年战略贸易法》第 12 条兜底条款：任何人出口、转运或过境美国原产的先进 AI 芯片，须提前 30 天通报并取得战略贸易许可证，并附美国再出口许可证与制造商的归类。门槛照抄 3A090（TPP ≥ 4,800，或 ≥ 1,600 且密度 ≥ 5.92；数据中心区间 2,400–4,800）。它适用于所有口岸，含巴生港与柔佛，但不适用于在马来西亚境内使用的进口。

  • Instrument: MITI press statement and Directive of the Strategic Trade Controller No. 1/2025, both 14 Jul 2025, "effective immediately until further notice." Pending review of adding the chips to the Strategic Items List.文书：MITI 新闻声明与战略贸易管制官第 1/2025 号指令，均为 2025 年 7 月 14 日，"即时生效，另行通知前有效"。是否将这些芯片列入战略物项清单尚在审议。
  • Legal basis: STA 2010 s. 12 lets the Controller require notice of "unlisted items" that the person knows or has reason to believe will be used for a restricted (WMD) activity; the Directive declares the AI-chip categories (3U090, 4U090, 3A001.u, 4A003.u, 5A002.u and others) as such items. Application must carry the originating country's re-export licence and the manufacturer's export-control classification.法律依据：《2010 年战略贸易法》第 12 条允许管制官就当事人明知或有理由相信将用于受限（WMD）活动的"未列明物项"要求通报；该指令将 AI 芯片类别（3U090、4U090、3A001.u、4A003.u、5A002.u 等）宣布为此类物项。申请须附来源国的再出口许可证与制造商的出口管制归类。
  • Penalties (s. 12(4)(b)): with intent, up to 10 years or RM10m (body corporate RM20m); without intent, up to 5 years or RM5m (RM10m). False declaration also enforceable under other laws.处罚（第 12(4)(b) 条）：故意者，最高 10 年或 1,000 万林吉特（法人 2,000 万林吉特）；非故意者，最高 5 年或 500 万林吉特（1,000 万林吉特）。虚假申报亦可依其他法律追究。
  • Scope: export, transshipment and transit only. A Johor or Cyberjaya data center importing chips for its own use needs no Malaysian permit; moving them on does. The US HQ rule (31 May 2026) separately requires a BIS licence for new chips to any China-headquartered operator in Malaysia.范围：仅限出口、转运与过境。柔佛或赛城的数据中心为自用而进口芯片无需马来西亚许可；转运出去则需要。美国总部规则（2026 年 5 月 31 日）另行要求：向马来西亚任何总部位于中国的运营方供应新芯片须经 BIS 许可。
  • Context: Malaysia's imports under HS 847150 rose from US$1.73bn (2023) to US$13.80bn (2025); Epoch AI found a six-fold mismatch between Malaysian and Chinese customs records for shipments into China (Apr 2024–Jun 2025); the Earthmade indictment says >$176M came from two unnamed Malaysia-based shipping companies in 2024. Megaspeed's Malaysian unit Speedmatrix is under US investigation. No Malaysian prosecution found.背景：马来西亚 HS 847150 项下进口从 17.3 亿美元（2023）升至 138.0 亿美元（2025）；Epoch AI 发现 2024 年 4 月至 2025 年 6 月运入中国的货物在马来西亚与中国海关记录间存在六倍错配；Earthmade 起诉书称 2024 年有 >$176M 来自两家未具名的马来西亚航运公司。Megaspeed 的马来西亚实体 Speedmatrix 正受美国调查。未发现马来西亚的公诉。

Gap: whether the chips were added to the SIL after the review; no update found through Oct 2026.存疑：审议后芯片是否被列入战略物项清单（SIL）；截至 2026 年 10 月未见更新。

D9 Did Singapore or Malaysia change their own rules on AI chip transshipment, and when?新加坡或马来西亚是否修改了各自关于 AI 芯片转运的规则？何时？ expert专家

Malaysia did, on 14 July 2025 (permit and 30-day notice for US-origin AI chips). Singapore did not change its law; it issued a Customs advisory in April 2025 and relies on fraud and false-declaration charges. Taiwan (Oct 2026) and South Korea (1 Sep 2026) also added AI chips to their lists.

马来西亚修改了，时间为 2025 年 7 月 14 日（对美国原产 AI 芯片要求许可与 30 天通报）。新加坡未改法律；它在 2025 年 4 月发布海关公告，并依靠欺诈与虚假申报指控。台湾（2026 年 10 月）与韩国（2026 年 9 月 1 日）也将 AI 芯片列入各自清单。

  • Malaysia: Directive 1/2025 under STA 2010 s. 12 (see D8). Reported as partly a negotiating move during US tariff talks (Straits Times).马来西亚：《2010 年战略贸易法》第 12 条下的第 1/2025 号指令（见 D8）。据报部分是美国关税谈判期间的一步谈判动作（《海峡时报》）。
  • Singapore: 18 Feb 2025 parliamentary answers (no obligation to enforce unilateral controls; firms must not use Singapore to evade them); Apr 2025 Customs advisory asking for compliance programmes and KYC; enforcement through the Feb 2025 case and the Megaspeed probe. No SGCA list change for AI chips found.新加坡：2025 年 2 月 18 日议会答复（无义务执行单边管制；企业不得利用新加坡规避之）；2025 年 4 月海关公告要求建立合规计划与 KYC；通过 2025 年 2 月的案件与 Megaspeed 调查执法。未发现 SGCA 清单就 AI 芯片作出变更。
  • Hong Kong: Schedule 1 update effective 30 May 2025 tracking multilateral lists only.香港：附表 1 更新于 2025 年 5 月 30 日生效，仅跟随多边清单。
  • Neighbours: South Korea made AI chips strategic items needing a MOTIR licence from 1 Sep 2026; Taiwan said on 16 Oct 2026 it will add advanced AI chips and servers to its export-control list.周边：韩国自 2026 年 9 月 1 日起将 AI 芯片列为需 MOTIR 许可的战略物项；台湾于 2026 年 10 月 16 日表示将把先进 AI 芯片与服务器列入其出口管制清单。
  • US pressure: BIS announced in May 2025 that it would impose licence requirements for Malaysia and Thailand; no such rule appeared in the Federal Register through 27 Oct 2026.美国施压：BIS 于 2025 年 5 月宣布将对马来西亚与泰国施加许可要求；截至 2026 年 10 月 27 日，《联邦公报》未出现此类规则。

D10 How many chips were estimated to reach Chinese buyers through Singapore and Malaysia, and in what years?估计有多少芯片经新加坡与马来西亚流向中国买家？分别在哪些年份？ detail细节

Nobody has a count; the estimates run from tens of thousands to over a million chips a year. The best-cited figures: CNAS put 2024 smuggling at 10,000 to several hundred thousand chips (median ~140,000); Epoch AI put 2025 at 290,000 to 1.6 million; the FT reported $1bn of Nvidia chips reaching China in the three months after the April 2025 H20 ban. The named cases add up to roughly $3bn of servers over 2023–2025.

无人有确切计数；估计从每年数万到逾百万颗不等。最常被引用的数字：CNAS 将 2024 年走私量定为 1 万到数十万颗（中位数约 14 万）；Epoch AI 将 2025 年定为 29 万到 160 万；《金融时报》报道，2025 年 4 月 H20 禁令后的三个月内有 10 亿美元英伟达芯片流入中国。已具名的案件在 2023—2025 年合计约 30 亿美元服务器。

  • Billing vs shipping: Nvidia billed 18% of FY2025 revenue ($23.7bn) to Singapore but shipped under 2% there ($473M); Q2 FY2026 billing 22%, with Nvidia saying over 99% of controlled data-center revenue billed to Singapore was for US-based customers. Singapore's own check: physical deliveries under 1% of Nvidia revenue. The billing figure is therefore not a diversion estimate.开票地 vs 发货地：英伟达将 2025 财年营收的 18%（237 亿美元）开票计入新加坡，但在该地发货不足 2%（4.73 亿美元）；2026 财年第二季度开票占 22%，英伟达称开票计入新加坡的受控数据中心营收中逾 99% 面向美国客户。新加坡自己的核查：实物交付不足英伟达营收的 1%。因此开票数字并非转移量的估计。
  • Malaysia trade data: imports under HS 847150 US$1.73bn (2023), US$5.44bn (2024), US$13.80bn (2025); from Taiwan up ~45× to US$4.06bn. Epoch AI found a six-fold mismatch between Malaysian export and Chinese import records for Apr 2024–Jun 2025.马来西亚贸易数据：HS 847150 项下进口 17.3 亿美元（2023）、54.4 亿美元（2024）、138.0 亿美元（2025）；自台湾进口增长约 45 倍至 40.6 亿美元。Epoch AI 发现 2024 年 4 月至 2025 年 6 月马来西亚出口与中国进口记录间存在六倍错配。
  • Case totals (allegations unless stated): Supermicro co-founder, ~$2.5bn of servers to China via SE Asia incl. $510M in Apr–May 2025; Earthmade, >$300M via Malaysia/Singapore in 2023–24; Operation Gatekeeper, $160M+ of H100/H200 via HK (guilty plea); ALX Solutions, 21+ shipments via Singapore/Malaysia 2022–25; Singapore fraud case, ~$390M of servers to Malaysia; Megaspeed, ~$2bn of chips bought, allegedly accessed from China via Malaysia/Indonesia.各案金额（除注明外均为指控）：Supermicro 联合创始人，约 25 亿美元服务器经东南亚流入中国，含 2025 年 4—5 月的 5.1 亿美元；Earthmade，2023—24 年经马来西亚/新加坡 >$300M；Gatekeeper 行动，经香港 1.6 亿美元以上的 H100/H200（已认罪）；ALX Solutions，2022—25 年经新加坡/马来西亚 21 批以上；新加坡欺诈案，约 3.9 亿美元服务器运往马来西亚；Megaspeed，购入约 20 亿美元芯片，据称经马来西亚/印尼从中国访问。
  • Market signal: a grey-market B300 8-GPU server went from RMB 4M to >8M in six months (FT, Jun 2026), consistent with supply tightening after the May 2026 BIS guidance and the 2026 arrests.市场信号：灰色市场上一台 B300 八卡服务器在六个月内从 400 万元涨至 >800 万元（《金融时报》，2026 年 6 月），与 2026 年 5 月 BIS 指引及 2026 年的逮捕之后供给收紧相符。

Caveat: all unit estimates are modelled from trade gaps or revenue mismatches; none is an official count.须注意：所有颗数估计均由贸易缺口或营收错配建模得出；没有一项是官方计数。

D11 What are the UAE deals (G42, large data centres) and what conditions did the US attach to them?阿联酋的交易（G42、大型数据中心）是什么？美国附加了哪些条件？ detail细节

The US–UAE AI Acceleration Partnership (15 May 2025) lets the UAE build a 5 GW AI campus run by US hyperscalers and, after a year of haggling, buy top chips: 35,000 GB300-equivalents each for G42 and Saudi Humain (Nov 2025) and, from 10 Jul 2026, license-free chips for G42, Core42 and eight US firms. Conditions: KYC to keep Chinese parties out, no Chinese hardware or staff, physical security, reporting to BIS, and for G42 a deadline to become a US company by 6 Apr 2027.

美国—阿联酋 AI 加速伙伴关系（2025 年 5 月 15 日）允许阿联酋建设由美国云厂商运营的 5 GW AI 园区，并在一年的讨价还价后购买顶级芯片：G42 与沙特 Humain 各 35,000 颗 GB300 等效芯片（2025 年 11 月），以及自 2026 年 7 月 10 日起，G42、Core42 与八家美国公司可免许可获取芯片。条件：以 KYC 将中国方排除在外、不得使用中国硬件或人员、实体安保、向 BIS 报告，以及 G42 须在 2027 年 4 月 6 日前成为美国公司的期限。

  • May 2025: G42 to build a 10-square-mile, 5 GW campus in Abu Dhabi; capacity "reserved for US hyperscalers and approved cloud service providers"; the two governments to "enhance Know-Your-Customer protocols." Reported volume up to 500,000 Blackwell-class GPUs a year (100,000 to G42, 400,000 to US firms in the UAE), never confirmed as a licence.2025 年 5 月：G42 将在阿布扎比建设占地 10 平方英里的 5 GW 园区；容量"预留给美国云厂商与获批的云服务提供商"；两国政府将"强化 KYC 协议"。据报规模至每年至多 500,000 颗 Blackwell 级 GPU（10 万颗给 G42、40 万颗给在阿联酋的美国公司），但从未被确认为一项许可。
  • 19 Nov 2025: Commerce authorises up to 35,000 GB300-equivalents each for G42 and Humain, "conditioned on both companies meeting rigorous security and reporting requirements," with BIS monitoring; estimated ~$1bn each. Companies must declare planned uses, storage locations and security measures (Rest of World). G42 describes its "Regulated Technology Environment": Western-only hardware, air-gapped compute, FIPS 140-2 encryption, no Chinese nationals on site, telemetry for anomalous compute use. G42 had earlier divested from ByteDance and dropped Huawei; Humain pledged not to buy Huawei.2025 年 11 月 19 日：美国商务部批准 G42 与 Humain 各最多 35,000 颗 GB300 等效芯片，"以两家公司达到严格的安全与报告要求为条件"，由 BIS 监督；估计各约 10 亿美元。公司须申报计划用途、存储地点与安全措施（Rest of World）。G42 描述其"受监管技术环境"：仅用西方硬件、物理隔离算力、FIPS 140-2 加密、现场无中国籍人员、对异常算力使用进行遥测。G42 此前已从字节跳动撤资并弃用华为；Humain 承诺不购买华为。
  • 10 Jul 2026 (91 FR 43034): UAE moved to Country Group A:5. G42 and Core42 (as consignee or end user in the UAE) and Amazon, Apple, Google, Meta, Microsoft, OpenAI, Oracle and X.AI with subsidiaries may receive 3A090.a/.b items license-free; MGX gets favourable review; others apply for an advisory opinion (30 days). Part 744 end-user rules still apply. If G42 and Core42 do not become US companies by 6 Apr 2027, they must apply to keep the status. Exports to parties headquartered in China or Macau still need a licence.2026 年 7 月 10 日（91 FR 43034）：阿联酋升入国家组 A:5。G42 与 Core42（作为在阿联酋的收货人或最终用户）以及亚马逊、苹果、谷歌、Meta、微软、OpenAI、甲骨文与 X.AI 及其子公司，可免许可获取 3A090.a/.b 物项；MGX 获优惠审查；其余须申请咨询意见（30 天）。第 744 部分最终用户规则仍适用。若 G42 与 Core42 未在 2027 年 4 月 6 日前成为美国公司，须申请以保留该地位。对总部位于中国或澳门的主体出口仍需许可证。
  • Earlier precedent: Microsoft–G42 IGAA (2024) with KYC excluding Chinese entities, "vault-within-a-vault" isolation, a joint compliance committee, financial penalties and London arbitration.更早的先例：微软—G42 的 IGAA（2024），含排除中国实体的 KYC、"库中库"隔离、联合合规委员会、经济处罚与伦敦仲裁。

Gap: the licence conditions themselves are not public; the rule text does not address remote access by Chinese users (Covington).存疑：许可条件本身不公开；规则文本未涉及中国用户的远程访问（Covington）。

D12 For the UAE and Saudi Arabia: which specific licenses or authorizations exist (G42, Humain, the 2025 Stargate-UAE deals), what volumes they cover, and what the security conditions on the licensee look like?就阿联酋与沙特阿拉伯而言：存在哪些具体的许可或授权（G42、Humain、2025 年 Stargate-UAE 交易）？覆盖多少数量？对被许可方的安全条件是什么样的？ detail细节

Three instruments are public: (1) the Sep 2024 licence for Microsoft-operated Azure capacity in G42 facilities; (2) the 19 Nov 2025 authorisations of up to 35,000 GB300-equivalents each for G42 and Humain; (3) the 10 Jul 2026 rule putting G42, Core42 and eight US firms on a license-free list for the UAE (Supplement No. 8 to Part 740). The 500,000-chips-a-year Stargate UAE figure is a reported framework, not a published licence. Saudi Arabia has no equivalent rule; Humain's 18,000 GB300 first tranche with Nvidia had no disclosed licence until the Nov 2025 approval.

三项文书是公开的：（1）2024 年 9 月针对 G42 设施内由微软运营的 Azure 容量的许可；（2）2025 年 11 月 19 日对 G42 与 Humain 各最多 35,000 颗 GB300 等效芯片的授权；（3）2026 年 7 月 10 日将 G42、Core42 与八家美国公司列入阿联酋免许可清单的规则（第 740 部分附录 8）。每年 500,000 颗的 Stargate UAE 数字是据报的框架，而非公开的许可。沙特阿拉伯无对应规则；Humain 与英伟达的首批 18,000 颗 GB300 在 2025 年 11 月批准前未披露任何许可。

  • Saudi Arabia: Humain's Nov 2025 authorisation is the only public one; Humain is not a Data Center VEU and no IGAA-style agreement has been announced (CSET). Saudi Arabia stays in its prior country group (no A:5 move found).沙特阿拉伯：Humain 的 2025 年 11 月授权是唯一公开的；Humain 不是数据中心 VEU，且未宣布 IGAA 式协议（CSET）。沙特阿拉伯仍留在其原有国家组（未见升入 A:5）。

Gaps: the licence conditions are not public; "35,000 GB300-equivalents" is a TPP-based cap whose exact arithmetic is not published; no Federal Register notice for the Nov 2025 authorisations.存疑：许可条件不公开；"35,000 颗 GB300 等效"是以 TPP 为基础的上限，其确切算法未公布；2025 年 11 月的授权无《联邦公报》通知。

D13 What UAE and Saudi deals were approved for advanced chips, and what safeguards against diversion to China do they carry?阿联酋与沙特的哪些交易获批进口先进芯片？它们带有哪些防止转移至中国的保障措施？ detail细节

G42 (UAE) and Humain (Saudi) each got approval in November 2025 for up to 35,000 Blackwell GB300-equivalents; from July 2026 G42, Core42 and eight US hyperscalers can take chips into the UAE without a licence. Safeguards: Western-only hardware, no Chinese staff or investors in the chip business, KYC that excludes Chinese customers, physical and logical isolation, telemetry, reporting to BIS, and US audit rights.

G42（阿联酋）与 Humain（沙特）各于 2025 年 11 月获批最多 35,000 颗 Blackwell GB300 等效芯片；自 2026 年 7 月起，G42、Core42 与八家美国云厂商可免许可将芯片运入阿联酋。保障措施：仅用西方硬件、芯片业务中无中国员工或投资者、排除中国客户的 KYC、物理与逻辑隔离、遥测、向 BIS 报告，以及美国审计权。

See D11 and D12 for the instruments. Engineering-relevant conditions reported: compute physically separated from building systems (cameras, cooling); FIPS 140-2 encryption; monitoring of compute spikes that could indicate large training runs by unapproved users; logging and auditability with US inspection rights; a "vault-within-a-vault" for chips and model weights (Microsoft–G42). G42 removed Huawei equipment and divested its ByteDance stake before approval; Humain pledged not to buy Huawei. The rule itself is silent on remote access, so whether a Chinese user can rent compute in these facilities depends on each licensee's conditions and the KYC it promised (and Part 744 end-user bans always apply).

文书详见 D11 与 D12。据报与工程相关的条件：算力与楼宇系统（摄像头、冷却）物理分离；FIPS 140-2 加密；监测可能表明未获批用户进行大型训练的算力峰值；带美国检查权的日志与可审计性；芯片与模型权重的"库中库"（微软—G42）。G42 在获批前拆除华为设备并出售其字节跳动股份；Humain 承诺不购买华为。规则本身对远程访问未置一词，因此中国用户能否在这些设施租用算力，取决于各被许可方的条件及其承诺的 KYC（且第 744 部分最终用户禁令始终适用）。

Sources来源4
  • Semafor · 10 Oct 2025 press媒体
  • Rest of World · 21 Nov 2025 press媒体
  • CSET think tank智库
  • Covington · Jul 2026 law firm律所
D14 What conditions were attached to the UAE and Saudi Arabia chip deals?阿联酋与沙特阿拉伯的芯片交易附加了哪些条件？ detail细节

Security and reporting conditions enforced by BIS, a KYC regime that shuts Chinese parties out, no Chinese hardware or personnel, and for the UAE a structural condition: G42 and Core42 keep license-free status past 6 April 2027 only if they become US companies.

由 BIS 执行的安全与报告条件、将中国方排除在外的 KYC 制度、无中国硬件或人员，以及就阿联酋而言的一项结构性条件：G42 与 Core42 只有成为美国公司，才能在 2027 年 4 月 6 日之后保留免许可地位。

The published conditions are thin: "rigorous security and reporting requirements" (Nov 2025) and the Supplement No. 8 list with the April 2027 deadline (Jul 2026). The substantive terms are in unpublished licences and in the May 2025 partnership text. Reported content: KYC excluding Chinese entities; no Chinese-origin technology or staff; logging, auditability and US inspection rights; declared storage locations; divestment of Chinese holdings (G42 sold its ByteDance stake); and for Microsoft–G42, financial penalties and London arbitration. The US also kept a licence requirement for any China-headquartered party in the UAE and for anyone not on the list. G42 is reported to be exploring a US majority stake to meet the 2027 deadline. For investors: the UAE's status is revocable and conditional; the Saudi approvals are company-specific with no country-group upgrade.

已公开的条件很单薄："严格的安全与报告要求"（2025 年 11 月），以及附带 2027 年 4 月期限的附录 8 清单（2026 年 7 月）。实质性条款在未公开的许可证与 2025 年 5 月的伙伴关系文本中。据报内容：排除中国实体的 KYC；无中国原产技术或人员；日志、可审计性与美国检查权；申报的存储地点；剥离中国持股（G42 出售其字节跳动股份）；以及就微软—G42 而言的经济处罚与伦敦仲裁。美国还保留了对阿联酋境内任何总部在中国的主体、以及任何不在清单上者的许可要求。据报 G42 正探索引入美方控股以满足 2027 年期限。对投资者而言：阿联酋的地位可撤销且附条件；沙特的批准针对具体公司，无国家组升级。

  • See sources under D11–D12见 D11–D12 的来源
  • Startup Fortune · on G42's US-ownership plan关于 G42 引入美方控股计划 secondary二手
D15 UAE: what does the 2025 US–UAE AI framework require of the UAE side (KYC, no Chinese ownership, no remote access from China), and which UAE statute or ministerial decision implements it locally?阿联酋：2025 年美国—阿联酋 AI 框架对阿联酋一侧有何要求（KYC、无中国所有权、禁止来自中国的远程访问）？哪部阿联酋法律或部级决定在本地实施之？ detail细节

The framework commits the UAE to strict anti-diversion measures, enhanced KYC for access to compute, and reserving the campus for US hyperscalers and approved providers; the detailed obligations sit in company-level licence conditions and the April 2026 working-group process, not in a published UAE statute. No UAE law or cabinet decision specific to AI chips has been found; the general regime is Federal Decree-Law 43/2021 on non-proliferation goods, administered by the Executive Office for Control and Non-Proliferation (EOCN).

该框架使阿联酋承诺采取严格的防转移措施、对访问算力实施强化 KYC，并将园区预留给美国云厂商与获批提供商；详细义务在公司层面的许可条件与 2026 年 4 月的工作组进程中，而非公开的阿联酋法律中。未发现专门针对 AI 芯片的阿联酋法律或内阁决定；一般性制度是关于防扩散物品的 2021 年第 43 号联邦法令，由管制与防扩散执行办公室（EOCN）主管。

  • Framework text (Commerce, 15 May 2025): "The UAE is committed to safeguarding advanced AI technologies by implementing stringent measures to prevent diversion and ensure controlled access to technology. The UAE and US will work together to enhance Know-Your-Customer protocols to regulate access to the compute resources, which are reserved for US hyperscalers and approved cloud service providers."框架文本（商务部，2025 年 5 月 15 日）："阿联酋承诺通过实施严格措施来保护先进 AI 技术，以防止转移并确保对技术的受控访问。阿联酋与美国将共同强化 KYC 协议，以规范对算力资源的访问，这些资源预留给美国云厂商与获批的云服务提供商。"
  • Implementation mechanism: the US–UAE AI Acceleration Partnership Interagency Working Group (first meeting Apr 2026) covers "export controls, investment screening, and other technology protection measures"; next steps include G42's initiatives within its "Regulated Technology Environment." BIS keeps an Export Control Officer in the UAE (91 FR 43034 preamble).实施机制：美国—阿联酋 AI 加速伙伴关系跨部门工作组（首次会议 2026 年 4 月）涵盖"出口管制、投资审查及其他技术保护措施"；后续步骤包括 G42 在其"受监管技术环境"内的举措。BIS 在阿联酋常驻一名出口管制官（91 FR 43034 序言）。
  • UAE domestic law: Federal Decree-Law No. 43 of 2021 (replacing Law 13/2007) and Cabinet Decision 97/2024 require licences for export, re-export and transit of listed dual-use goods; the control list mirrors the multilateral regimes; EOCN is the permitting authority. No AI-chip listing or Chinese-access rule found. The EOCN's July 2026 statement welcomes the A:5 status without naming new domestic measures.阿联酋国内法：2021 年第 43 号联邦法令（取代 2007 年第 13 号法律）与 2024 年第 97 号内阁决定要求对列明两用物品的出口、再出口与过境办理许可；管制清单镜像多边机制；EOCN 是发证机关。未发现 AI 芯片的列入或中国访问规则。EOCN 2026 年 7 月的声明欢迎 A:5 地位，但未点名新的国内措施。
  • Remote access: the US rule does not address it (Covington); the framework's KYC commitment and licence conditions are the only levers. No UAE instrument on remote access found.远程访问：美国规则未涉及（Covington）；框架的 KYC 承诺与许可条件是仅有的抓手。未发现阿联酋关于远程访问的文书。

Gap: the partnership text itself is not published; UAE implementing instruments, if any, were not found.存疑：伙伴关系文本本身未公开；阿联酋的实施文书（若有）未被找到。

D16 How do UAE data centers that serve Chinese firms get licenses, and what checks apply?服务中国企业的阿联酋数据中心如何取得许可？适用哪些核查？ detail细节

They mostly cannot. A UAE data center gets its chips either license-free (if it is G42, Core42 or a listed US hyperscaler) or by individual BIS licence; either way the approval comes with KYC that excludes Chinese parties, and any party headquartered in China or Macau still needs its own licence, which is presumed denied. Serving Chinese customers remotely is not itself an export today, but it would breach the KYC the licensee promised.

大多不能。阿联酋数据中心获取芯片，要么免许可（若是 G42、Core42 或列名的美国云厂商），要么凭单独的 BIS 许可；无论哪种，批准都附带将中国方排除在外的 KYC，而任何总部位于中国或澳门的主体仍需自己的许可证，且被推定拒绝。远程服务中国客户今日本身不构成出口，但会违反被许可方承诺的 KYC。

Three layers of checks: (1) US export licence or Supplement No. 8 status for the chips, with Part 744 end-user bans (Entity List, military-intelligence, WMD) applying regardless; (2) the licensee's security and reporting conditions monitored by BIS (declared uses, storage, telemetry, inspections); (3) the May 2025 partnership's KYC commitment reserving compute for US hyperscalers and approved providers. For a China-HQ customer: the 31 May 2026 BIS guidance requires a licence for advanced computing items to such entities wherever located, and 91 FR 43034 repeats that the UAE rule does not lift it. BIS's May 2025 guidance also warns against letting US chips train or run Chinese AI models for military-intelligence or WMD ends. Pending: the Remote Access Security Act (passed House 369–22, 12 Jan 2026) would make cloud access by China-HQ parents an export control matter. Practical result: UAE compute is a route for the Gulf and the Global South, not for Chinese firms (legal desk's route G, "not a route to Chinese customers").

三层核查：（1）芯片的美国出口许可证或附录 8 地位，第 744 部分最终用户禁令（实体清单、军事情报、WMD）无论如何均适用；（2）由 BIS 监督的被许可方安全与报告条件（申报用途、存储、遥测、检查）；（3）2025 年 5 月伙伴关系的 KYC 承诺，将算力预留给美国云厂商与获批提供商。对总部在中国的客户而言：2026 年 5 月 31 日的 BIS 指引要求，向此类实体（无论位于何处）提供先进计算物项须经许可，91 FR 43034 重申阿联酋规则并不解除这一点。BIS 2025 年 5 月的指引还警告，不得让美国芯片为军事情报或 WMD 目的训练或运行中国 AI 模型。待定：《远程访问安全法》（众议院以 369–22 于 2026 年 1 月 12 日通过）将把总部在中国的母公司的云访问纳入出口管制。实际结果：阿联酋算力是面向海湾与全球南方的通道，而非面向中国企业（法律台的路线 G，"并非通往中国客户的路线"）。

  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026 official官方

D17 What are the license rules for Chinese-headquartered companies renting GPUs from data centers in Malaysia, Singapore or the UAE?总部位于中国的公司在马来西亚、新加坡或阿联酋的数据中心租用 GPU，适用什么许可规则？ detail细节

Renting compute is not an export of the chips under today's US practice, so a Chinese-headquartered company can rent GPUs abroad from a data center that is not Chinese-owned, as long as the operator bought its chips lawfully, is not serving an Entity List or military-intelligence user, and its own licence or approval does not forbid Chinese users. The operator cannot be your own overseas subsidiary with new chips, and Congress may close the route.

按今日的美国实践，租用算力不构成芯片的出口，因此总部位于中国的公司可以从非中资的数据中心在境外租用 GPU，只要运营方合法购入芯片、未服务实体清单或军事情报用户，且其自身的许可或批准不禁止中国用户。该运营方不能是你自己配置了新芯片的境外子公司，且国会可能关闭这条路线。

  • The rule: BIS treats IaaS/remote access as "generally not treated as an export of the chips themselves"; the Diffusion Rule's remote-access provisions are not enforced and the March 2026 replacement draft was withdrawn.规则：BIS 将 IaaS/远程访问视为"一般不作为芯片本身的出口对待"；扩散规则的远程访问条款未执行，2026 年 3 月的替代草案已撤回。
  • Operator conditions: (a) chips obtained lawfully, with no licence needed for a non-China-owned operator in Singapore or Japan; in Malaysia a MITI permit applies to moving chips out, not to using them; in the UAE only listed parties get chips license-free; (b) no service to Entity List parties or knowing support of military-intelligence or WMD end uses, including AI training for such parties headquartered in China or Macau (BIS May 2025 guidance); (c) if the operator holds an H200-route licence, it must have filed KYC procedures against unauthorised remote access and listed its IaaS remote users located in or headquartered in China and Macau.运营方条件：（a）合法取得芯片，非中资运营方在新加坡或日本无需许可；在马来西亚，MITI 许可适用于将芯片转运出去，而非使用；在阿联酋，只有列名方可免许可获取芯片；（b）不得服务实体清单主体，不得明知支持军事情报或 WMD 最终用途，包括为总部在中国或澳门的此类主体进行 AI 训练（BIS 2025 年 5 月指引）；（c）若运营方持有 H200 路线许可证，须已就防止未经授权的远程访问提交 KYC 程序，并列出位于或总部位于中国与澳门的 IaaS 远程用户。
  • Your own subsidiary abroad: the 31 May 2026 guidance requires a licence for new advanced chips to any entity headquartered in, or with an ultimate parent in, China or Macau, wherever it sits, with presumption of denial; existing fleets may keep running "until further notice."你自己的境外子公司：2026 年 5 月 31 日的指引要求，向任何总部位于、或最终母公司位于中国或澳门的实体（无论身处何地）提供新的先进芯片须经许可，且推定拒绝；现有设备群可"在另行通知前"继续运行。
  • Scrutiny: Megaspeed's Malaysian and Indonesian sites that "appeared to be remotely serving Chinese clients" are under Singapore and US (DOJ) investigation.审视：Megaspeed 位于马来西亚与印尼、"似乎在远程服务中国客户"的站点，正受新加坡与美国（司法部）调查。
  • Pending: Remote Access Security Act (H.R. 2683, passed House 12 Jan 2026, in Senate Banking) "could prohibit the provision of cloud services to any company with an ultimate parent headquartered in China" (Nvidia 10-Q).待定：《远程访问安全法》（H.R. 2683，众议院于 2026 年 1 月 12 日通过，现于参议院银行委员会）"可能禁止向任何最终母公司总部位于中国的公司提供云服务"（英伟达 10-Q）。
  • Beijing's side: the Oct 2026 draft AI-technology export controls could reach model weights or training know-how flowing back into China (draft; comments close 15 Nov 2026).北京一侧：2026 年 10 月的 AI 技术出口管制草案可能触及回流中国的模型权重或训练技术诀窍（草案；征求意见截至 2026 年 11 月 15 日）。
Sources来源7

  • BIS press release · 13 May 2025 official官方

D18 If I build a data centre in Malaysia, Singapore or the UAE, can Chinese-owned companies rent its compute, and what headquarters rules apply?如果我在马来西亚、新加坡或阿联酋建数据中心，中资公司能租用其算力吗？适用什么总部规则？ expert专家

If your data center is not Chinese-owned and you bought the chips lawfully, Chinese-owned customers can rent compute today, except Entity List, military-intelligence and sanctioned parties, and except where your own licence or local approval forbids it (the UAE's does in practice). The headquarters rule bites the owner, not the tenant: if your data center's ultimate parent is in China or Macau, new chips need a US licence that is presumed denied.

若你的数据中心非中资、且你合法购入芯片，中资客户今日可以租用算力——实体清单、军事情报与受制裁主体除外，你自身许可或本地批准所禁止的情形除外（阿联酋的批准在实践中即禁止）。总部规则咬住的是所有者而非租户：若你数据中心的最终母公司在中国或澳门，新芯片需要一张被推定拒绝的美国许可证。

  • Ownership test: "headquartered in, or with an ultimate parent headquartered in" China or Macau (§744.23(a)(3) as confirmed by BIS, 31 May 2026). A Chinese minority investor does not make you China-headquartered; a Chinese ultimate parent does. If the Affiliates Rule returns on 10 Nov 2026, any company 50%+ owned by an Entity List party is treated as listed, which adds a second ownership screen on both your investors and your customers.所有权判定："总部位于、或最终母公司总部位于"中国或澳门（§744.23(a)(3)，经 BIS 于 2026 年 5 月 31 日确认）。中国的少数股东不会使你成为总部在中国；中国的最终母公司则会。若关联方规则于 2026 年 11 月 10 日恢复，任何由实体清单主体持股 50% 以上的公司均被视为列名，这会在你的投资者与客户两端增加第二道所有权筛查。
  • By country: Singapore, no local licence to buy; Malaysia, MITI permit to move chips out, none to import and use; UAE, chips license-free only for G42, Core42 and the eight US firms, otherwise a BIS licence, and the partnership's KYC reserves compute for US hyperscalers and approved providers, so Chinese tenants are in practice excluded.分国家：新加坡，购买无需本地许可；马来西亚，转运芯片出去需 MITI 许可，进口自用不需要；阿联酋，仅 G42、Core42 与八家美国公司可免许可获取芯片，否则需 BIS 许可，且伙伴关系的 KYC 将算力预留给美国云厂商与获批提供商，故中资租户在实践中被排除。
  • Tenant screening: Entity List and 50% affiliates (if the rule returns), military end users, §744.8 parties; the May 2025 guidance on training Chinese models for restricted ends; red flags for diversion of model weights. H200-route licences also require filed KYC against unauthorised remote access and lists of China/Macau remote users.租户筛查：实体清单与 50% 关联方（若规则恢复）、军事最终用户、§744.8 主体；2025 年 5 月关于为受限目的训练中国模型的指引；模型权重转移的危险信号。H200 路线许可证还要求就防止未经授权的远程访问提交 KYC，并列出中国/澳门的远程用户。
  • Risk: this route draws the most scrutiny (Megaspeed), and RASA would close it for China-HQ customers. Price the exit.风险：这条路线最受审视（Megaspeed），而 RASA 将对总部在中国的客户关闭它。为退出定价。
  • Our reading, not a BIS determination; get a classification or advisory opinion before signing tenants.此为我们的解读，而非 BIS 的裁定；在签订租户前，先取得归类或咨询意见。
Sources来源7
  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026 official官方
  • 90 FR 47201 · Affiliates Rule关联方规则 official官方
  • 90 FR 50857 · suspension暂停 official官方
  • 91 FR 43034 official官方
  • Commerce · 15 May 2025 official官方
  • MITI Directive 1/2025 official官方
  • Project research files本站研究文件
D19 If a UAE data center owned by a Gulf entity but with a Chinese minority investor leases compute to a Chinese AI lab, which party needs a BIS license, and under which rule: 744.23, the Diffusion successor rule, or none?如果一家阿联酋数据中心由海湾实体持有、但有一名中国少数股东，向一家中国 AI 实验室出租算力，哪一方需要 BIS 许可证？依据哪条规则：744.23、扩散后继规则，还是无需？ expert专家

For the chips: the exporter or reexporter needs a licence under §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) and §744.23 unless the data center is a Supplement No. 8 party (G42, Core42, the US hyperscalers), and the Chinese minority stake does not change that because the HQ test looks at the ultimate parent. For the lease: no EAR licence is required for remote access today (there is no Diffusion successor), but the lease can breach the licensee's conditions and the UAE's KYC commitment, and §744 end-use bans apply if the lab is listed or serves military-intelligence ends.

就芯片而言：出口商或再出口商依 §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) 与 §744.23 需要许可证，除非该数据中心是附录 8 主体（G42、Core42、美国云厂商），且中国少数股权不改变这一点，因为总部判定看的是最终母公司。就租赁而言：今日远程访问无需 EAR 许可证（不存在扩散后继规则），但租赁可能违反被许可方的条件与阿联酋的 KYC 承诺，且若实验室被列名或服务于军事情报用途，则 §744 最终用途禁令适用。

  • Chip export leg: §742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) worldwide licence requirement, enforced to D:1/D:4/D:5 destinations and to entities headquartered in or with an ultimate parent in D:5/Macau (BIS 13 May 2025 and 31 May 2026 guidance, restated in 91 FR 43034). The UAE is A:5 since 10 Jul 2026, so a Gulf-owned data center needs a licence only if not listed in Supplement No. 8; the applicant is the exporter/reexporter (Nvidia, OEM or distributor), with the data center as consignee/end user. A Chinese minority investor is irrelevant to §744.23(a)(3) unless it is the ultimate parent; it would matter under the Affiliates Rule (if revived 10 Nov 2026) only if the investor is an Entity List party holding ≥50%.芯片出口环节：§742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A) 的全球许可要求，适用于 D:1/D:4/D:5 目的地，以及总部位于或最终母公司位于 D:5/澳门的实体（BIS 2025 年 5 月 13 日与 2026 年 5 月 31 日指引，并在 91 FR 43034 中重申）。阿联酋自 2026 年 7 月 10 日起为 A:5，故海湾所有的数据中心仅在未列入附录 8 时才需许可证；申请人是出口商/再出口商（英伟达、OEM 或分销商），数据中心为收货人/最终用户。中国少数股东与 §744.23(a)(3) 无关，除非其为最终母公司；仅当该投资者是持股 ≥50% 的实体清单主体时，才在关联方规则（若于 2026 年 11 月 10 日恢复）下有意义。
  • Lease leg: remote access is not an export of the item under current BIS practice; the Diffusion Rule's IaaS provisions are unenforced and the replacement draft was withdrawn (Mar 2026), so there is no successor rule. Constraints that do apply: §744.11/744.6-type end-use and end-user prohibitions (Entity List lab, military-intelligence end use, §744.8 parties); the BIS May 2025 warning on training Chinese models for restricted ends; the licence conditions on the data center (reporting, KYC, declared uses) and, for H200-route licences, the filed KYC and remote-user lists; the May 2025 partnership's KYC commitment reserving compute for US hyperscalers and approved providers. Breach of a licence condition exposes the licensee (the data center and the exporter), not the Chinese lab.租赁环节：按现行 BIS 实践，远程访问不构成该物项的出口；扩散规则的 IaaS 条款未执行，替代草案已撤回（2026 年 3 月），故无后继规则。确实适用的约束：§744.11/744.6 式的最终用途与最终用户禁令（实体清单实验室、军事情报最终用途、§744.8 主体）；BIS 2025 年 5 月关于为受限目的训练中国模型的警告；数据中心的许可条件（报告、KYC、申报用途），以及就 H200 路线许可证而言已提交的 KYC 与远程用户清单；2025 年 5 月伙伴关系的 KYC 承诺，将算力预留给美国云厂商与获批提供商。违反许可条件使被许可方（数据中心与出口商）承担风险，而非中国实验室。
  • If the lab is the data center's ultimate parent or the facility is China-HQ: §744.23 applies to the chips with presumption of denial (91 FR 1684), and existing kit may be used "until further notice."若实验室是数据中心的最终母公司，或该设施总部在中国：§744.23 适用于芯片并推定拒绝（91 FR 1684），现有设备可"在另行通知前"使用。
  • Pending: RASA (H.R. 2683) would bring remote access by China-HQ parents under the EAR; the FY2027 NDAA is the likely vehicle.待定：RASA（H.R. 2683）将把总部在中国的母公司的远程访问纳入 EAR；2027 财年《国防授权法》是可能的载体。

Our reading of the texts; no BIS advisory opinion on this fact pattern was found.此为我们对文本的解读；未发现 BIS 就此事实情形出具的咨询意见。

E

Market: demand, supply, the gap市场：需求、供给与缺口

Chinese demand and who buys; price inside China vs list; chips reaching China lawfully per year; the training-to-inference shift; Nvidia's China revenue and share by quarter; the gap that legal supply leaves unmet.

中国的需求与买家；境内价格 vs 标价；每年合法进入中国的芯片量；由训练转向推理；英伟达各季度的中国营收与份额；合法供给留下的缺口。

E1 How big is Chinese demand for AI compute, who buys, and how much does Nvidia still meet?中国对 AI 算力的需求有多大、谁在买、英伟达还能满足多少？ basic基础

About 4 million AI accelerators shipped into China in 2025. Big internet firms (ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent, Baidu) buy most of them. Nvidia's lawful share is collapsing: from about two thirds of the market in 2024 to a projected single digit in 2026 by one estimate.

2025 年约有 400 万块 AI 加速卡进入中国。大型互联网公司（字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯、百度）买走了其中大部分。英伟达的合法份额正在崩塌：据一项估计，从 2024 年约占市场三分之二，降至 2026 年预计的个位数。

  • 2025 volume: ~4M accelerators, of which 2.36M Nvidia + AMD and ~1.65M (41%) Chinese-made (IDC via Tom's Hardware). H1 2026: ~2.0M shipped; Nvidia 678k (34%), Huawei 572k (28%) [A, original Reuters not read].2025 年出货量：约 400 万块加速卡，其中英伟达与 AMD 为 236 万块，中国自产约 165 万块（41%）（IDC，经 Tom's Hardware 转载）。2026 年上半年：约 200 万块出货；英伟达 67.8 万块（34%），华为 57.2 万块（28%）[A，未读路透社原文]。
  • Money: 2025 accelerator-card market RMB 378.4bn; internet sector 53%, non-internet 47% (CIC consultancy via DoNews) [A].资金：2025 年加速卡市场规模为 3784 亿元人民币；互联网行业占 53%，非互联网占 47%（CIC 咨询，经 DoNews 转载）[A]。
  • Buyers: ByteDance 2026 capex plan raised to RMB 200bn (about RMB 85bn for AI chips), unnamed sources only, it files nothing. Alibaba, Tencent, Baidu combined RMB 131.9bn (US$19.4bn) of capex in the June 2026 quarter, +114% YoY. Not all of that is chips: Alibaba says part is higher component prices. Telcos: China Mobile H1 2026 capex RMB 61.0bn, 31.6% on compute/AI. AI labs (DeepSeek, Zhipu, MiniMax) mostly rent cloud capacity; DeepSeek reportedly may get limited H200 permission. Universities, military and state labs show up in 100+ grey-market H200 tenders (qualitative).买家：字节跳动 2026 年资本开支计划上调至 2000 亿元人民币（其中约 850 亿元用于 AI 芯片），仅有未具名消息来源，公司不作任何披露。阿里巴巴、腾讯、百度在 2026 年 6 月当季合计资本开支 1319 亿元人民币（194 亿美元），同比增长 114%。其中并非全部用于芯片：阿里巴巴称部分源于零部件价格上涨。电信运营商：中国移动 2026 年上半年资本开支 610 亿元人民币，其中 31.6% 用于算力/AI。AI 实验室（DeepSeek、智谱、MiniMax）多以租用云端算力为主；据报 DeepSeek 可能获得有限的 H200 许可。高校、军方和国家实验室出现在 100 多个灰色市场 H200 招标中（定性判断）。
  • Orders vs legal supply: Chinese firms ordered >2M H200 (~$27,000 each) in Jan 2026 against Nvidia inventory of 700k; ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent ordered >3M RTX PRO 5500 (~$11,400 each, >$35bn); total China orders >6M, and Nvidia says buyers may get 5-10% of what they asked for. Double-ordering is likely, so headline orders overstate need.订单与合法供给对比：2026 年 1 月中国企业订购了逾 200 万块 H200（每块约 27,000 美元），而英伟达库存仅 70 万块；字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯订购了逾 300 万块 RTX PRO 5500（每块约 11,400 美元，合计逾 350 亿美元）；中国订单总量逾 600 万块，而英伟达表示买家最终可能只拿到所申请数量的 5–10%。重复下单的可能性很大，因此表面订单夸大了实际需求。
  • Nvidia's share (units vs value differ): Bernstein: 66% (2024), 40% (2025), ~8% (2026E) of China's AI chip market. IDC's 34% H1-2026 unit share counts lower-end parts, so the two are not like for like. TrendForce projects domestic chips at nearly 90% of the "high-end" market in 2026. Definitions differ; treat the range as the finding.英伟达份额（按数量与按金额口径不同）：伯恩斯坦（Bernstein）占中国 AI 芯片市场 66%（2024 年）、40%（2025 年）、约 8%（2026 年预期）。IDC 给出的 2026 年上半年 34% 为数量份额，且计入了低端产品，两者口径不一致。TrendForce 预计 2026 年国产芯片将占"高端"市场近 90%。各家定义不同；应将此区间本身视作结论。
  • Need vs lawful, rough: Bloomberg Intelligence 2026 demand ~4.2M vs supply ~2.6M (all domestic), gap ~1.6M. Morgan Stanley says expansion is limited more by high-end compute availability than by demand.需求与合法供给的粗略对比：Bloomberg Intelligence 预计 2026 年需求约 420 万块，供给约 260 万块（全部为国产），缺口约 160 万块。摩根士丹利认为，扩张受限更多是因为高端算力供给不足，而非需求不足。

Honesty marks: ByteDance figures are unnamed-source. Capex is not all chips. The 4.2M/2.6M gap was never traced to the original Bloomberg note. IDC and Bernstein shares are not comparable.诚实标注：字节跳动的数字来自未具名消息来源。资本开支并非全部用于芯片。420 万/260 万的缺口始终未能追溯到 Bloomberg 的原始报告。IDC 与伯恩斯坦的份额不可直接比较。

E2 What does a top Nvidia GPU cost inside China versus the official price elsewhere, and how wide is the gap?一块顶级英伟达 GPU 在中国境内的价格，与别处的官方价格相比如何，差距有多大？ detail细节

Lawful licensed H200s are quoted near the normal price (~$27,000 in order reports). The gap is in the grey market, where prices have doubled in six months for Blackwell servers and workstation cards.

合法持证的 H200 报价接近正常价格（订单报道中约为 27,000 美元）。差距出现在灰色市场：Blackwell 服务器与工作站显卡的价格在六个月内翻了一倍。

  • H200: ~$27,000 in Chinese orders (Reuters, Jan 2026). Global list reference in our chip file: ~$32k. So no lawful premium shows in press, but the US-side license adds a 25% cut to the US Treasury and the 25% Section 232 tariff on chips passing through the US for inspection (cost lands somewhere in the chain; Nvidia says it cannot pass the tariff on).H200：中国订单中约 27,000 美元（路透社，2026 年 1 月）。我们芯片资料中的全球标价参考：约 32,000 美元。因此媒体中看不到合法溢价，但美国一侧的许可证附带向美国财政部上缴 25% 的收入分成，以及芯片经美国过境查验时征收的 25% 的第 232 条关税（成本落在供应链的某个环节；英伟达称无法转嫁该关税）。
  • H20 (2025): $10,000-12,000. RTX Pro 6000D: ~$7,000 (RMB 50,000) in 2025, weak demand, now discontinued with market price doubled. RTX PRO 5500: ~$11,400, samples Dec 2026, deliveries Q1 2027.H20（2025 年）：10,000–12,000 美元。RTX Pro 6000D：2025 年约 7,000 美元（50,000 元人民币），需求疲软，现已停产，市场价格翻倍。RTX PRO 5500：约 11,400 美元，2026 年 12 月送样，2027 年第一季度交付。
  • Grey market (banned parts): DGX B300 8-GPU server RMB 4M to >8M in six months (US retail ~$400k, which is about RMB 2.8M at ~7.1; my arithmetic, so roughly 1.4x then 2.9x US retail). RTX 6000 Pro RMB 50k to up to 130k. RTX 5090: $1,999 list vs ~$3,500 grey in Sep 2025 (about 1.75x).灰色市场（禁运产品）：DGX B300 八卡服务器价格在六个月内从 400 万元人民币涨至逾 800 万元（美国零售价约 40 万美元，按约 7.1 汇率折合约 280 万元；为本站自行测算，故约为美国零售价的 1.4 倍、继而 2.9 倍）。RTX 6000 Pro 从 5 万元涨至最高 13 万元。RTX 5090：标价 1,999 美元，而 2025 年 9 月灰色市场约 3,500 美元（约 1.75 倍）。
  • Domestic chips are also rising: Ascend 950PR ~RMB 60k to >80k in 2026; 910C board ~RMB 90k to >110k; 950DT >RMB 250k. Cause: global HBM shortage.国产芯片价格同样在上涨：Ascend 950PR 从 2026 年约 6 万元人民币涨至逾 8 万元；910C 板卡从约 9 万元涨至逾 11 万元；950DT 逾 25 万元。原因：全球 HBM 短缺。

Honesty marks: No source gives a like-for-like "China price vs list" for the H100/H200 grey market in 2026. The ~$32k list price is a reference figure, not an Nvidia price sheet.诚实标注：没有任何来源给出 2026 年 H100/H200 灰色市场"中国价格对标价"的同口径比较。约 32,000 美元的标价是参考数字，并非英伟达的价目表。

E3 How many AI chips reach China each year, lawfully versus smuggled?每年有多少 AI 芯片进入中国，合法渠道与走私各占多少？ detail细节

Lawful flow into mainland China is now tiny for top chips (about 20,000 H200s delivered by August 2026). Smuggling estimates range widely: median ~140,000 chips in 2024 (CNAS), and 290,000 to 1.6 million H100-equivalents cumulative through 2025 (Epoch AI). Nobody has a solid per-route figure.

就顶级芯片而言，流入中国内地的合法数量如今极少（到 2026 年 8 月约交付 20,000 块 H200）。走私估算差异极大：2024 年中位数约 14 万块芯片（CNAS），以及到 2025 年累计 29 万至 160 万块 H100 等效芯片（Epoch AI）。没有人掌握可靠的分路线数据。

  • Lawful, 2025: 2.36M Nvidia + AMD accelerators shipped into China (IDC), but that is mostly lower-end parts and pre-ban stock, not controlled top chips.合法渠道，2025 年：236 万块英伟达与 AMD 加速卡运入中国（IDC），但其中大多为低端产品和禁令前库存，而非受管制的顶级芯片。
  • Lawful, 2026: US licensed ~10 Chinese buyers for up to 75,000 H200 each (May 2026); Beijing approved >400k in Jan on conditions; only ~20,000 delivered by Aug 2026 (~2.5% of the 400k+), with most of each US allowance steered to Hong Kong. Nvidia holds ~500k H200 built for China.合法渠道，2026 年：美国于 2026 年 5 月批准约 10 家中国买家，每家最多 75,000 块 H200；北京方面于 1 月有条件批准逾 40 万块；到 2026 年 8 月仅交付约 20,000 块（约为 40 万块的 2.5%），且每家的美国配额大多被导向香港。英伟达持有约 50 万块为中国制造的 H200。
  • Smuggling, 2024: CNAS June 2025 probabilistic model, 10,000 to several hundred thousand, median ~140,000 [web, snippet only]. Epoch AI: 290,000 to 1.6M H100-equivalents of compute smuggled through end 2025 [web, snippet only]. These extrapolate from known cases; they are not seizure counts.走私，2024 年：CNAS 2025 年 6 月的概率模型，1 万至数十万块，中位数约 14 万块 [web，仅摘要]。Epoch AI：到 2025 年底累计走私 29 万至 160 万块 H100 等效算力 [web，仅摘要]。这些是从已知案例外推得出，并非查获数量。
  • Routes seen in prosecutions: Malaysia, Thailand, Singapore, Hong Kong. Hong Kong handled more than half of China's US$239bn chip imports in Jan-May 2026. Remote cloud is a separate, legal-but-scrutinised channel (see H section). Taiwan and Japan routes: no sourced estimate in our files.起诉案件中出现的路线：马来西亚、泰国、新加坡、香港。2026 年 1 至 5 月，香港经手了中国 2,390 亿美元芯片进口中的一半以上。远程云访问是另一条合法但受严格审视的渠道（见 H 部分）。台湾和日本路线：我们的资料中没有可引用的估算。
  • Case anchors for scale: ~US$2.5bn of Super Micro servers (SDNY, unsealed 2026-03-19); US$300M+ servers via Malaysia/Singapore (C.D. Cal., 2026-09-29); ~US$390M in the Singapore server-fraud case.案件规模锚点：约 25 亿美元的美超微（Super Micro）服务器（纽约南区法院，2026 年 3 月 19 日解封）；经马来西亚/新加坡的逾 3 亿美元服务器（加州中区法院，2026 年 9 月 29 日）；新加坡服务器欺诈案中约 3.9 亿美元。
  • Specific "chips via Singapore and Malaysia, by year" estimate: not found. Treat as an open gap.关于"经新加坡和马来西亚、按年份的芯片流量"的具体估算：未找到。视作一处未解的空白。

Honesty marks: Smuggling numbers are modelled estimates with very wide ranges. Epoch and CNAS were read from search snippets only; check the originals before quoting. The IDC totals mix chip classes.诚实标注：走私数字是区间极宽的模型估算。Epoch 与 CNAS 仅从搜索摘要读取；引用前请核对原文。IDC 的总量混合了不同芯片类别。

E4 Is Chinese demand shifting from training to inference, and does that change which chips are worth trading?中国需求是否正从训练转向推理，这是否改变了哪些芯片值得交易？ expert专家

Probably yes at the margin, but no hard shift data was found. Beijing's own steering points the same way: Nvidia only for frontier training, Chinese chips for inference. Lawful trading of banned chips is not an option either way.

在边际上很可能是，但未找到确凿的转移数据。北京方面的引导也指向同一方向：英伟达仅用于前沿训练，国产芯片用于推理。无论如何，合法交易被禁芯片都不是一个选项。

  • Policy signal (unconfirmed): H200 use reportedly limited to model training, inference steered to Huawei (GPUSmith [A]); July 2026 report that labs were told to migrate onto Huawei (AI Weekly [A]). Market desk's read: Beijing admits Nvidia "where domestic chips cannot yet serve (frontier training)".政策信号（未经证实）：据报 H200 的使用被限定于模型训练，推理则被引导至华为（GPUSmith [A]）；2026 年 7 月有报道称各实验室被要求迁移至华为（AI Weekly [A]）。市场组的解读：北京只在"国产芯片尚无法胜任之处（前沿训练）"认可英伟达。
  • Technical logic: inference is limited by memory size and speed more than raw compute. The H20 (weak compute, 96 GB at 4 TB/s) was the clearest inference part. Huawei's 950PR targets "prefill" and recommendation workloads; the 950DT targets training and "decode".技术逻辑：推理更多受制于显存容量和速度，而非原始算力。H20（算力弱，96 GB、4 TB/s）是最明确的推理用芯片。华为 950PR 面向"预填充"（prefill）和推荐类工作负载；950DT 面向训练和"解码"（decode）。
  • Demand-side numbers: no reliable training/inference split of Chinese demand found. Nvidia's RTX PRO 5500 (uncontrolled in the US, gaming-class memory) is pitched at serving and small-lab work, and drew >3M ordered cards, which suggests inference/fine-tune demand is large.需求侧数据：未找到中国需求在训练/推理间的可靠拆分。英伟达 RTX PRO 5500（在美国不受管制、采用游戏级显存）定位于推理服务和小型实验室工作，获得逾 300 万块订单，表明推理/微调需求很大。
  • Trading implication: for a lawful trader, chip class matters less than the licence: top Nvidia parts need a US licence held by the maker, not a distributor. Parts under the TPP 4,800 line (RTX PRO 5500 class) are the active lawful lane; see H section.交易含义：对合法交易商而言，芯片类别不如许可证重要：顶级英伟达产品需要由制造商（而非分销商）持有的美国许可证。低于总处理性能（TPP）4,800 线的产品（RTX PRO 5500 一类）才是当前的合法通道；见 H 部分。

Honesty marks: Unsettled. Sources are aggregators; no Chinese government text. No measured training/inference share.诚实标注：尚无定论。来源均为二手聚合站点；没有中国官方文本。没有实测的训练/推理份额。

Sources来源4
  • GPUSmith · 22 Jul 2026 secondary二手
  • AI Weekly · 24 Jul 2026 secondary二手
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • TrendForce · 21 Oct 2026
E5 Nvidia's China revenue by quarter, share of revenue in fiscal 2026, and how much of the fall is the H20 ban versus Beijing?英伟达按季度的中国营收、2026 财年中国营收占比，以及营收下滑中有多少源于 H20 禁令、多少源于北京方面？ detail细节

China (including Hong Kong) was about $19.7bn, 9.1% of fiscal 2026 revenue, down from $25.0bn and 19.2% in fiscal 2025. Hopper sales to China went from $4.6bn in one quarter (Q1 FY26) to zero (Q1 FY27). A full quarter-by-quarter table could not be built from what we found.

中国（含香港）约为 197 亿美元，占 2026 财年营收的 9.1%，低于 2025 财年的 250 亿美元和 19.2%。对华 Hopper 销售从某一季度（2026 财年第一季度）的 46 亿美元降至零（2027 财年第一季度）。根据我们掌握的资料，无法构建完整的逐季度表格。

  • Annual: FY2024 ~20.2% of revenue; FY2025 $25.0bn (19.2%); FY2026 $19.7bn (9.1%), -21% YoY. Source is a secondary analysis of the 10-K [web]; the market desk repeats $19.7bn vs $25.0bn. Note the "China" line is by customer billing location and includes non-accelerator products.年度：2024 财年约占营收 20.2%；2025 财年 250 亿美元（19.2%）；2026 财年 197 亿美元（9.1%），同比下降 21%。来源为对 10-K 的二手分析 [web]；市场组沿用 197 亿美元对比 250 亿美元之说。注意："中国"这一项按客户开票地统计，且包含非加速卡产品。
  • Quarters found: Q1 FY26 (Feb-Apr 2025): H20 sales ~$4.6bn; $4.5bn H20 inventory/purchase-commitment charge booked to gross margin (GAAP 60.5% vs 71.3% ex-charge); ~$2.5bn more H20 revenue could not ship. Q2 FY26 guidance assumed ~$8bn H20 revenue lost. Q1 FY27 (to 26 Apr 2026): no Hopper shipped to China, vs $4.6bn a year earlier; total revenue $81.6bn, data center $75.2bn. Q2 FY27 (to 26 Jul 2026): first H200 shipments, <1% of ~$89bn data-center revenue; a ~$400M H200 inventory charge is reported by one low-quality source only (unverified). Guidance assumes no China data-center compute revenue.已找到的季度数据：2026 财年第一季度（2025 年 2 至 4 月）：H20 销售约 46 亿美元；计入毛利的 H20 库存/采购承诺减值 45 亿美元（GAAP 毛利率 60.5%，剔除该减值为 71.3%）；另有约 25 亿美元 H20 营收无法发货。2026 财年第二季度指引假设损失约 80 亿美元 H20 营收。2027 财年第一季度（截至 2026 年 4 月 26 日）：对华无 Hopper 发货，上年同期为 46 亿美元；总营收 816 亿美元，数据中心 752 亿美元。2027 财年第二季度（截至 2026 年 7 月 26 日）：首批 H200 发货，占约 890 亿美元数据中心营收的不足 1%；约 4 亿美元 H200 库存减值仅由一家低质量来源报道（未核实）。指引假设无中国数据中心算力营收。
  • Attribution, H20 ban vs Beijing: the April 2025 US licence requirement caused the first drop (the $4.5bn charge and ~$8bn Q2 loss). Beijing's discouragement (CAC summons 31 Jul 2025, H20 de facto blocked ~Aug 2025) then kept the relicensed H20 from selling: the 10-Q says Nvidia "was unable to sell our H20 inventory" because of PRC restrictions, and that H200 sales "were restricted by the PRC government". No source splits the gap between the two causes; any split would be an estimate.归因，H20 禁令还是北京方面：2025 年 4 月美国的许可要求造成了第一次下滑（45 亿美元减值和第二季度约 80 亿美元损失）。随后北京方面的劝阻（2025 年 7 月 31 日国家网信办（CAC）约谈，H20 在约 2025 年 8 月被事实上封堵）使重新获证的 H20 无法销售：10-Q 称英伟达因中国的限制"无法售出 H20 库存"，且 H200 销售"受到中国政府限制"。没有任何来源能把下滑在两种原因之间作出拆分；任何拆分都将是估算。
  • Gap: FY2024 to latest quarter per-quarter table not assembled (primary 10-Q/10-K quarter tables were not opened).空白：2024 财年至最新季度的逐季表格未能编制（未打开主要的 10-Q/10-K 季度表）。

Honesty marks: Percentages and the Q1 FY26 figures come from secondary summaries of Nvidia filings, not the filings themselves. Verify against the 10-K/10-Q tables before publishing. The $400M H200 charge is unverified.诚实标注：各项百分比和 2026 财年第一季度数据来自对英伟达文件的二手摘要，而非文件本身。发布前请对照 10-K/10-Q 表格核实。4 亿美元 H200 减值未经核实。

E6 How big is the gap that legal supply leaves unmet?合法供给未能满足的缺口有多大？ detail细节

Roughly 1.6 million AI chips short in 2026 on one estimate, worth about US$18-25bn at Chinese chip prices (our rough arithmetic). Order books are far bigger than that, but inflated by double-ordering.

据一项估计，2026 年约短缺 160 万块 AI 芯片，按中国芯片价格计算约值 180 至 250 亿美元（本站粗略测算）。订单规模远大于此，但被重复下单所夸大。

  • Domestic demand ~4.2M vs domestic supply ~2.6M in 2026 (Bloomberg Intelligence): gap ~1.6M, mainly HBM.2026 年国内需求约 420 万块，对国内供给约 260 万块（Bloomberg Intelligence）：缺口约 160 万块，主要是 HBM。
  • Supply forecasts: JPM 2.0M, Morgan Stanley 2.4M (2026); 3.0M / 4.8M (2027); JPM 5M (2028). JPM sees ~14M total demand by 2030 (40% CAGR) and a seller's market for domestic GPUs through ~2028.供给预测：摩根大通 200 万块、摩根士丹利 240 万块（2026 年）；300 万块/480 万块（2027 年）；摩根大通 500 万块（2028 年）。摩根大通预计到 2030 年总需求约 1,400 万块（年复合增长率 40%），国产 GPU 将维持卖方市场至约 2028 年。
  • Sizing (market desk's arithmetic, not a source figure): 1.6M × RMB 80-110k = RMB 130-175bn, about US$18-25bn. Units mix chip classes; prices are indicative quotes.规模测算（市场组自算，非来源数字）：160 万块 × 每块 8 万至 11 万元人民币 = 1,300 至 1,750 亿元人民币，约合 180 至 250 亿美元。单位混合了不同芯片类别；价格为指示性报价。
  • Nvidia-class orders that cannot be filled: >2M H200 ordered vs ~20k delivered; >6M RTX PRO 5500 requested vs 5-10% fill and a ~3M global cap in 2027.无法满足的英伟达级订单：订购逾 200 万块 H200，而仅交付约 2 万块；申请逾 600 万块 RTX PRO 5500，而履约率为 5–10%，且 2027 年全球产能上限约 300 万块。
  • Price signals: Ascend 950PR +33% in 2026; grey B300 servers doubled.价格信号：Ascend 950PR 在 2026 年上涨 33%；灰色市场 B300 服务器价格翻倍。
  • Why it persists: HBM (not logic) is the binding limit, with foundry yield and HBM3/3e qualification constraints through ~2028; H200 licensed in the US but gated by Beijing; Blackwell banned; RTX PRO 5500 not delivering until Q1 2027.为何持续：HBM（而非逻辑芯片）是约束性瓶颈，叠加代工良率和 HBM3/3e 认证的限制，持续至约 2028 年；H200 在美国已获许可，但受北京方面管控；Blackwell 被禁；RTX PRO 5500 要到 2027 年第一季度才交付。

Honesty marks: The 4.2M/2.6M figure is the most cited but second-hand. True shortage is below headline orders.诚实标注：420 万/260 万这一数字被引用最多，但属二手。真实短缺低于表面订单。

F

Chinese-made alternatives国产替代

Huawei Ascend and Cambricon / Hygon / Moore Threads / Biren / MetaX: shipping volume and how they compare to the H100/H200; SMIC process and capacity; HBM supply (CXMT); the CANN vs CUDA software gap; US reach over Ascend.

华为昇腾与寒武纪／海光／摩尔线程／壁仞／沐曦：出货量及与 H100/H200 的对比；中芯国际工艺与产能；HBM 供给（长鑫）；CANN 与 CUDA 的软件差距；美国对昇腾的管辖触及。

F1 Can Huawei, Cambricon or Moore Threads chips replace Nvidia, or are buyers still desperate for US parts?华为、寒武纪或摩尔线程的芯片能取代英伟达吗，还是买家仍迫切需要美国产品？ basic基础

Not yet. Chinese chips already supply roughly two fifths of units and are favoured by Beijing, but they trail on single-chip speed, memory, software and supply. Buyers still queue for Nvidia: orders for H200 and RTX PRO 5500 are in the millions.

尚不能。国产芯片已供应约五分之二的数量，并受北京方面青睐，但在单芯片速度、显存、软件和供给方面仍落后。买家仍在排队购买英伟达：H200 和 RTX PRO 5500 的订单量以百万计。

  • Ascend 910C: ~752-800 dense FP16 TFLOPS (H100: 989), 128 GB HBM at 3.2 TB/s (H100 80 GB at 3.35 TB/s; H200 141 GB at 4.8 TB/s). About 60% of an H100 on inference in DeepSeek testing, reported second-hand.Ascend 910C：约 752–800 密集 FP16 TFLOPS（H100：989），128 GB HBM、3.2 TB/s（H100 为 80 GB、3.35 TB/s；H200 为 141 GB、4.8 TB/s）。在 DeepSeek 的测试中，推理约为 H100 的 60%，为二手转述。
  • Against what China can lawfully buy: 910C beats the H20 on compute (752 vs 148 FP16) and memory size (128 vs 96 GB), loses slightly on bandwidth (3.2 vs 4.0 TB/s).对比中国可合法购买的产品：910C 在算力（752 对 148 FP16）和显存容量（128 对 96 GB）上胜过 H20，在带宽（3.2 对 4.0 TB/s）上略逊。
  • Best Chinese chips are "closer to the hardware Nvidia released about five years ago than to its current frontier." Strength is system scale (CloudMatrix 384), not single chips.最好的国产芯片"更接近英伟达约五年前发布的硬件，而非其当前前沿"。其长处在于系统规模（CloudMatrix 384），而非单芯片。
  • Demand evidence: Huawei says it cannot meet domestic demand yet (Sep 2026); Ascend prices rose 20-50% in 2026.需求证据：华为称目前仍无法满足国内需求（2026 年 9 月）；Ascend 价格在 2026 年上涨 20–50%。

Honesty marks: Huawei publishes no 910C datasheet; FP16 figure is 752 or ~800 and memory is sometimes reported as 96 GB. The 60% figure is second-hand.诚实标注：华为未公布 910C 数据表；FP16 数字为 752 或约 800，显存有时被报为 96 GB。60% 这一数字为二手转述。

F2 Which Chinese AI chips ship in volume, what do Cambricon, Hygon, Moore Threads, MetaX, Biren and T-Head offer, which are production-ready, and who is listed and what did they earn?哪些国产 AI 芯片已量产出货，寒武纪、海光、摩尔线程、沐曦、壁仞和平头哥各提供什么，哪些已可量产，谁已上市、各自收益如何？ basic基础

Huawei is by far the largest (~812,000 shipped in 2025), then Alibaba T-Head, Cambricon and Baidu Kunlunxin. Moore Threads, MetaX and Biren are smaller and mostly loss-making.

华为遥遥领先（2025 年出货约 812,000 块），其次是阿里巴巴平头哥、寒武纪和百度昆仑芯。摩尔线程、沐曦和壁仞规模较小，且大多亏损。

  • Huawei is ByteDance's largest domestic supplier, then Cambricon, then Iluvatar CoreX. Cambricon and Huawei are the only two vendors on the government-approved AI hardware procurement list.华为是字节跳动最大的国内供应商，其次是寒武纪，再次是天数智芯（Iluvatar CoreX）。寒武纪和华为是政府批准的 AI 硬件采购清单上仅有的两家厂商。

F3 How big is the performance gap between Ascend and H100/H200 on training and inference, what are the Ascend roadmap specs and units shipped, and how do they compare per dollar?Ascend 与 H100/H200 在训练和推理上的性能差距有多大，Ascend 的路线图规格和出货量如何，按每美元算力又如何比较？ detail细节

One 910C is roughly an H100 on paper for FP16 compute but behind on memory bandwidth and real-world efficiency. The 2026 generation (950) adds FP8/FP4 but is only about half an H100's FP8 rate per chip. Huawei compensates by wiring up many chips. On price per unit of compute the 910C looks cheaper than the H200 on paper; real cost depends on software and power.

单块 910C 在纸面 FP16 算力上大致相当于 H100，但在显存带宽和实际效率上落后。2026 年一代（950）增加了 FP8/FP4，但每芯片的 FP8 速率仅约为 H100 的一半。华为通过把许多芯片互联来弥补。按单位算力价格计，910C 在纸面上比 H200 便宜；真实成本取决于软件和功耗。

  • Specs (reported, not Huawei datasheets for 910C): 910C 752-800 FP16 TFLOPS, 128 GB, 3.2 TB/s, 784 GB/s link, no FP8. 950PR (Q1 2026): FP8 1 PFLOPS, FP4 2 PFLOPS, 128 GB at 1.6 TB/s, 2 TB/s link. 950DT (Q4 2026): same compute, 144 GB at 4 TB/s. Huawei does not say whether the 950 figures are dense. Epoch AI (Sep 2026): Nvidia B300 is about 7x Ascend 950 in low-precision compute.规格（为转述，非华为 910C 数据表）：910C 为 752–800 FP16 TFLOPS，128 GB，3.2 TB/s，784 GB/s 互联，无 FP8。950PR（2026 年第一季度）：FP8 1 PFLOPS，FP4 2 PFLOPS，128 GB、1.6 TB/s，2 TB/s 互联。950DT（2026 年第四季度）：算力相同，144 GB、4 TB/s。华为未说明 950 的数字是否为密集算力。Epoch AI（2026 年 9 月）：在低精度算力上，英伟达 B300 约为 Ascend 950 的 7 倍。
  • Shipped: ~812,000 Ascend in 2025; 572,000 in H1 2026.出货：2025 年约 812,000 块 Ascend；2026 年上半年 572,000 块。
  • Inference: 910C ~60% of an H100 (DeepSeek testing, second-hand).推理：910C 约为 H100 的 60%（DeepSeek 测试，二手转述）。
  • Rough per-dollar (my arithmetic, assumed RMB 7.1/US$): 910C ~752 TFLOPS / ~US$15.5k (RMB 110k) = ~0.049 TFLOPS per dollar; H200 989 / US$27k = ~0.037; H20 148 / US$11k = ~0.013. On paper the 910C is cheaper per FP16 compute than the H200 and far cheaper than the H20. This ignores memory bandwidth, interconnect, software and 2.3x worse power per FLOP at system level (see F8).每美元粗算（本站测算，假设 7.1 元人民币/美元）：910C 约 752 TFLOPS / 约 15,500 美元（11 万元人民币）= 约每美元 0.049 TFLOPS；H200 为 989 / 27,000 美元 = 约 0.037；H20 为 148 / 11,000 美元 = 约 0.013。纸面上 910C 每单位 FP16 算力比 H200 便宜，远比 H20 便宜。此测算忽略了显存带宽、互联、软件，以及系统层面每 FLOP 功耗差 2.3 倍（见 F8）。
  • Training vs inference cost-competitiveness: no sourced benchmark or total-cost study found for 910C clusters vs Nvidia. Treat as unsettled; DeepSeek's V4 delay while rewriting for Ascend (single secondary report) shows a real software cost.训练与推理的成本竞争力：未找到针对 910C 集群对比英伟达的可引用基准测试或总成本研究。视作尚无定论；DeepSeek 的 V4 因改写以适配 Ascend 而延期（单一二手报道），显示出真实的软件成本。

Honesty marks: Per-dollar numbers are my back-of-envelope using quoted prices and spec-sheet FP16, not benchmarks. Roadmap specs are vendor claims.诚实标注：每美元数字是本站用报价和规格表 FP16 做的粗略估算，并非基准测试。路线图规格为厂商声明。

F4 How many Ascend and other domestic AI chips can China produce per year, and what limits it?中国每年能生产多少 Ascend 及其他国产 AI 芯片，制约因素是什么？ detail细节

Forecasts put domestic supply at 2.0-2.6 million AI chips in 2026. The limit is HBM memory, not the logic chip: SMIC could make more chips than it can fit memory to.

预测认为 2026 年国产供给为 200 万至 260 万块 AI 芯片。制约因素是 HBM 显存，而非逻辑芯片：中芯国际能生产的芯片数量超过其可配套的显存数量。

  • 2026 domestic supply forecasts: JPM 2.0M, Morgan Stanley 2.4M, Bloomberg Intelligence ~2.6M. 2025 actual: ~1.65M.2026 年国产供给预测：摩根大通 200 万块，摩根士丹利 240 万块，Bloomberg Intelligence 约 260 万块。2025 年实际：约 165 万块。
  • Huawei: plans ~600k 910C and ~750k 950PR in 2026 (Reuters, Mar 2026, via Aquanode; unconfirmed).华为：计划 2026 年生产约 600k 910C 和约 750k 950PR（路透社，2026 年 3 月，经 Aquanode 转载；未经证实）。
  • SMIC could make >805k Ascend dies a year (SemiAnalysis via Inside China's Machine) but lacks HBM.中芯国际每年可生产逾 805k 块 Ascend 裸片（SemiAnalysis，经 Inside China's Machine 转载），但缺乏 HBM。
  • Limits in order: HBM supply, then foundry yield and leading-edge capacity, then HBM3/3e qualification (JPM: through ~2028).制约因素依次为：HBM 供给，其次是代工良率和先进制程产能，再次是 HBM3/3e 认证（摩根大通：持续至约 2028 年）。

Honesty marks: All forecasts are broker or analyst estimates; Huawei does not publish production.诚实标注：所有预测均为券商或分析师估算；华为不公布产量。

Sources来源5
  • Hilo/JPM
  • Hilo/MS
  • cnyes/BI
