F5 What is SMIC's most advanced usable process, how much capacity and what yields? Which fab makes each Chinese chip?中芯国际最先进的可用制程是什么，产能和良率如何？每款国产芯片由哪家晶圆厂制造？ detail细节

SMIC's usable leading-edge process is a 7nm-class node (N+2), with reported yield around 20%. Total SMIC capacity is about 1.1 million 8-inch-equivalent wafers a month, ~95% used. Reliable 5nm-class figures were not found.

中芯国际可用的先进制程是 7nm 级节点（N+2），据报良率约 20%。中芯国际总产能约为每月 110 万片 8 英寸当量晶圆，利用率约 95%。未找到可靠的 5nm 级数据。

  • Yield: ~20% for 7nm-class (Tom's Hardware, 2026-04-30). Capacity: ~1.1M 8-inch-equivalent wafers/month, ~95% utilised (The Substrate). These are all-process totals, not AI-only.良率：7nm 级约 20%（Tom's Hardware，2026 年 4 月 30 日）。产能：约每月 110 万片 8 英寸当量晶圆，利用率约 95%（The Substrate）。这些是全制程总量，并非仅 AI。
  • AI-specific 7nm wafer count: not found. Indirect: SMIC could make >805k Ascend dies a year if memory were available (SemiAnalysis).AI 专用 7nm 晶圆数量：未找到。间接数据：若有显存，中芯国际每年可生产逾 805k 块 Ascend 裸片（SemiAnalysis）。
  • Which fab: Ascend 910C/950 are made by SMIC (7nm-class). Early Ascend 910B dies were found to be TSMC-made through Sophgo (see F10). A chip-by-chip fab table for Cambricon, Hygon, Moore Threads, MetaX, Biren was not built; none of our files confirm their fabs, and some historically used TSMC before it was cut off.由哪家晶圆厂制造：Ascend 910C/950 由中芯国际制造（7nm 级）。早期 Ascend 910B 裸片被发现是经由 Sophgo 的台积电（TSMC）代工（见 F10）。没有为寒武纪、海光、摩尔线程、沐曦、壁仞编制逐芯片的晶圆厂对照表；我们的资料均未确认它们的晶圆厂，其中一些在被台积电切断前历史上曾使用台积电。
  • 5nm-class SMIC capacity and yield: no reliable source found. Huawei "cannot use TSMC's newest process", per the chip file.5nm 级中芯国际产能与良率：未找到可靠来源。据芯片资料，华为"无法使用台积电最新制程"。

Honesty marks: The 20% yield is a press figure, not SMIC-reported. SMIC does not disclose node-level yield.诚实标注：20% 良率是媒体数字，并非中芯国际公布。中芯国际不披露节点级良率。

F6 How much HBM can Chinese makers get (CXMT, stockpile), under what rules, and what is HBM's link to the export rules?中国厂商能获得多少 HBM（CXMT、库存），受何种规则约束，HBM 与出口规则又有何关联？ detail细节

The US controls high-bandwidth memory (December 2024), so China's main supply was a pre-controls stockpile that likely ran out in late 2025. CXMT is the home-grown replacement, but its output is small and not yet good enough, so HBM is the tightest limit on Chinese AI chips.

美国管制高带宽内存（2024 年 12 月），因此中国的主要供应来自管制前的库存，该库存很可能在 2025 年底耗尽。CXMT 是本土替代者，但其产量小、品质尚不够好，因此 HBM 是对国产 AI 芯片最紧的约束。

  • Rule: HBM stacks with memory bandwidth density above 2 GB/s/mm² are controlled under ECCN 3A090.c (89 FR 96790, 2 Dec 2024, published 5 Dec 2024).规则：内存带宽密度高于 2 GB/s/mm² 的 HBM 堆栈受出口管制分类编码（ECCN）3A090.c 管制（89 FR 96790，2024 年 12 月 2 日，2024 年 12 月 5 日公布）。
  • Stockpile: ~13M stacks bought before the Dec 2024 controls (enough for ~1.6M Ascend 910C), likely exhausted by late 2025 (SemiAnalysis via The Substrate). Which suppliers (Samsung, SK Hynix) filled it: our files do not break this out; the interaction with Samsung and SK Hynix supply is not documented in our files beyond the stockpile estimate.库存：在 2024 年 12 月管制前购入约 1,300 万堆栈（足够约 160 万块 Ascend 910C），很可能在 2025 年底耗尽（SemiAnalysis，经 The Substrate 转载）。由哪些供应商（三星、SK 海力士）供货：我们的资料未作拆分；除库存估算外，与三星和 SK 海力士供应之间的关联在我们的资料中没有记录。
  • CXMT: 2026 HBM output estimated at ~2M stacks (enough for 250-300k 910C equivalents), optimistic case ~7M; usable-for-AI HBM ~5,000 wafers/month (Bloomberg Intelligence via cnyes).CXMT：2026 年 HBM 产量估计约 200 万堆栈（足够 25 万至 30 万块 910C 等效），乐观情形约 700 万；可用于 AI 的 HBM 约每月 5,000 片晶圆（Bloomberg Intelligence，经 cnyes 转载）。
  • Effect: global HBM shortage pushed domestic chip prices up 20-50% in 2026; HBM is why the 1.6M-chip gap exists.影响：全球 HBM 短缺使 2026 年国产芯片价格上涨 20–50%；HBM 正是 160 万块芯片缺口存在的原因。
  • "Under what rules" for CXMT itself: US controls bind US-origin exports to China; CXMT's own sales inside China are not governed by the HBM rule. Rules on equipment sold to CXMT: not researched.就 CXMT 本身"受何种规则"而言：美国管制约束对华的美国原产出口；CXMT 在中国境内自身的销售不受 HBM 规则约束。向 CXMT 出售设备的规则：未作研究。

Honesty marks: Stockpile and CXMT numbers are analyst estimates with a wide range (2M vs 7M).诚实标注：库存和 CXMT 数字为分析师估算，区间很宽（200 万对 700 万）。

F7 How big is the software gap between CUDA and Huawei's CANN, what is the migration cost for PyTorch code, and how fast is it closing?CUDA 与华为 CANN 之间的软件差距有多大，PyTorch 代码的迁移成本如何，差距正以多快速度缩小？ expert专家

Large, but narrowing. CUDA has had nearly two decades and millions of developers. Huawei open-sourced CANN in August 2025 and runs PyTorch through an adapter, but analysts expect years before it matches.

差距很大，但正在缩小。CUDA 已有近二十年历史和数以百万计的开发者。华为于 2025 年 8 月将 CANN 开源，并通过适配层运行 PyTorch，但分析师预计尚需数年才能追平。

  • Gaps reported in operator coverage, automatic parallelisation, kernel fusion and distributed communication scheduling. A vendor-side claim that "CANN Next" reaches over 95% CUDA compatibility is unverified.据报在算子覆盖、自动并行、核融合和分布式通信调度方面存在差距。厂商一方声称"CANN Next"达到 95% 以上 CUDA 兼容，此说法未经核实。
  • PyTorch: runs through the torch_npu adapter, which is not native, so support lags each PyTorch release.PyTorch：通过 torch_npu 适配层运行，并非原生，因此对每个 PyTorch 版本的支持都有滞后。
  • Migration cost: no sourced engineering-hours figure. Qualitative example: DeepSeek V4 reportedly slipped while core code was rewritten for Ascend via CANN (single secondary source). Community size on CANN/MindSpore is far smaller than CUDA's.迁移成本：没有可引用的工程工时数字。定性例子：据报 DeepSeek V4 在将核心代码经 CANN 改写以适配 Ascend 期间延期（单一二手来源）。CANN/MindSpore 的社区规模远小于 CUDA。
  • Policy interplay: Beijing pushes labs to Huawei; CUDA pull remains strong, which is part of why RTX PRO 5500 (CUDA, at RMB 85-90k, same as an Ascend 950PR) draws orders.政策相互作用：北京方面推动各实验室转向华为；CUDA 的黏性依然很强，这也是 RTX PRO 5500（CUDA 生态，售价 8.5 万至 9 万元人民币，与 Ascend 950PR 相同）获得订单的部分原因。
  • Tom's Hardware: Huawei did not specify which CANN parts it released, so some analysis is speculation.Tom's Hardware：华为未说明其开源了 CANN 的哪些部分，因此部分分析属于推测。

Honesty marks: This is the weakest-sourced answer. No benchmarks, no migration-cost study. Do not quote the 95% figure.诚实标注：这是来源最薄弱的回答。没有基准测试，没有迁移成本研究。请勿引用 95% 这一数字。

F8 What are the realistic interconnect and scale-out limits of Ascend CloudMatrix clusters versus NVL72 racks?Ascend CloudMatrix 集群相较 NVL72 机架，在互联和横向扩展上的现实上限是什么？ expert专家

Huawei wins on raw system size and loses on power. A CloudMatrix 384 has more total compute and memory than an Nvidia GB200 NVL72 but uses about four times the electricity.

华为在原始系统规模上胜出，在功耗上落败。一套 CloudMatrix 384 的总算力和总显存都超过英伟达 GB200 NVL72，但耗电约为其四倍。

  • CloudMatrix 384 (384 × 910C, optical links): ~300 dense BF16 PFLOPS vs 180 for GB200 NVL72; 3.6x the memory; 2.1x the bandwidth.CloudMatrix 384（384 × 910C，光互联）：约 300 密集 BF16 PFLOPS，对比 GB200 NVL72 的 180；显存为其 3.6 倍；带宽为其 2.1 倍。
  • Power: ~559 kW vs ~145 kW, about 2.3x worse power per FLOP (SemiAnalysis). China has cheaper power than chips, so the trade works there.功耗：约 559 kW 对约 145 kW，每 FLOP 功耗约差 2.3 倍（SemiAnalysis）。中国的电力比芯片便宜，因此这种取舍在当地行得通。
  • 950 generation: 2 TB/s link, faster than Hopper-era NVLink (900 GB/s). Alibaba's Panjiu AL128 is a similar rack-scale effort.950 一代：2 TB/s 互联，快于 Hopper 时代的 NVLink（900 GB/s）。阿里巴巴的磐久（Panjiu）AL128 是类似的机架级尝试。
  • Limits not sourced: cluster-level training efficiency at tens of thousands of chips, failure rates, optical-link reliability.未有来源的上限：数万块芯片规模下的集群级训练效率、故障率、光互联可靠性。

Honesty marks: Paper system numbers, not measured training runs.诚实标注：为纸面系统数字，并非实测训练运行。

F9 Would trading Chinese-made AI chips or servers out of China expose me to US rules? Are Ascend chips covered by the Foreign Direct Product Rule?把中国制造的 AI 芯片或服务器运出中国进行交易，会让我受美国规则约束吗？Ascend 芯片本身是否受外国直接产品规则管辖？ expert专家

Yes, there is risk. In May 2025 BIS said that using certain Huawei Ascend chips anywhere in the world may violate US export rules, because they are made with US technology. It is guidance, not a published rule, and its exact reach is unsettled.

是的，存在风险。2025 年 5 月，美国商务部工业与安全局（BIS）表示，在全球任何地方使用某些华为 Ascend 芯片都可能违反美国出口规则，因为这些芯片使用了美国技术制造。这是指导意见，而非已公布的规则，其确切适用范围尚无定论。

  • BIS press release, 13 May 2025: using Huawei Ascend and other PRC advanced chips "anywhere in the world" may break the EAR (General Prohibition 10). The same package warned against letting US chips train or run Chinese AI models, listed diversion red flags, and announced licence requirements for Malaysia and Thailand. No Malaysia/Thailand rule has appeared in the Federal Register (checked to 27 Oct 2026).BIS 新闻稿，2025 年 5 月 13 日：在"全球任何地方"使用华为 Ascend 及其他中国先进芯片都可能违反《出口管理条例》（EAR）（一般禁令第 10 条）。同一揽子措施还警告不得让美国芯片用于训练或运行中国 AI 模型，列出了转运红旗信号，并宣布对马来西亚和泰国的许可要求。《联邦公报》中并未出现针对马来西亚/泰国的规则（核查至 2026 年 10 月 27 日）。
  • For Chinese buyers inside China, Ascend raises no US export issue.对中国境内的中国买家而言，Ascend 不涉及美国出口问题。
  • For a Singapore/UAE trader or user: guidance only; no penalty case found based on the Ascend guidance. Violations of the EAR carry up to 20 years per count criminally (ECRA, seen in charged cases); civil exposure can reach twice the transaction value (the basis of the reported TSMC figure).对新加坡/阿联酋的交易商或用户而言：仅为指导意见；未发现基于该 Ascend 指导意见的处罚案例。违反 EAR 在刑事上每项罪名最高可判 20 年（ECRA，见于已起诉案件）；民事敞口可达交易额的两倍（即所报道的台积电数字的依据）。
  • Practical read: reselling or hosting Ascend kit outside China is a legal grey zone to be cleared with counsel; the guidance is also not a licence prohibition by itself. Anything with US-origin parts or software needs a chain-of-title check.实务解读：在中国境外转售或托管 Ascend 设备属于须经律师厘清的法律灰色地带；该指导意见本身也并非许可证禁令。任何含美国原产部件或软件的物项都需要做产权链核查。

Honesty marks: Guidance, not a rule; unsettled whether it is enforceable as written. No enforcement under it found. Not legal advice.诚实标注：为指导意见而非规则；能否按字面执行尚无定论。未发现据此的执法。不构成法律意见。

Sources来源3
  • BIS press release · 13 May 2025 official官方
  • computelaw.blog

F10 What did BIS allege about TSMC dies in Huawei Ascend, what is TSMC's penalty exposure, and how does the foundry due-diligence rule affect Chinese designers and Huawei fabbing at TSMC?BIS 就华为 Ascend 中的台积电裸片提出了什么指控，台积电面临多大处罚敞口，代工尽职调查规则又如何影响中国设计厂商以及华为在台积电代工？ expert专家

In 2025 teardown work found a TSMC-made die from Sophgo inside Huawei's Ascend 910B. Reuters reported TSMC could face a fine of $1bn or more. No public penalty has been confirmed. A January 2025 "foundry rule" adds checks on who may order advanced chips.

2025 年的拆解工作在华为 Ascend 910B 内部发现了一块来自 Sophgo 的台积电制造裸片。路透社报道称台积电可能面临 10 亿美元或更高的罚款。尚无已确认的公开处罚。2025 年 1 月的"代工厂规则"增加了对谁可订购先进芯片的核查。

  • Allegation (Reuters, April 2025): a TSMC chip made for design firm Sophgo matched a die in the Ascend 910B, found by TechInsights; TSMC suspended shipments to Sophgo. Reuters sources: the figure comes from a fine of up to twice the value of violating transactions; Commerce would typically start with a letter giving 30 days to respond. As of the reports, no public action; a 10-figure export fine would be rare. Precedent: Seagate paid $300M (2023, hard drives to Huawei).指控（路透社，2025 年 4 月）：一块为设计公司 Sophgo 制造的台积电芯片与 Ascend 910B 中的一块裸片相匹配，由 TechInsights 发现；台积电随即暂停对 Sophgo 的供货。路透社消息来源：该数字源自最高可达违规交易额两倍的罚款；美国商务部通常会先发函、给予 30 天答复期。截至报道时，尚无公开行动；十位数美元的出口罚款将属罕见。先例：希捷（Seagate）支付 3 亿美元（2023 年，向华为出售硬盘）。
  • Our files: Sophgo's Entity List addition rests on secondary sources, so is not claimed; no confirmed BIS fine on TSMC.我们的资料：Sophgo 被列入实体清单一事基于二手来源，故不作认定；没有已确认的 BIS 对台积电罚款。
  • Foundry rule (90 FR 5298, 16 Jan 2025): due-diligence duties for foundries and chip packagers making advanced chips, with an "approved IC designer" status; approved-designer scheme deadline 31 Dec 2026. Effect on Chinese designers: in practice they must be vetted or pass through approved routes to have advanced chips fabbed at TSMC. Whether Huawei designs can still be fabbed there: Huawei is long on the Entity List, so TSMC cannot lawfully make for it; the Sophgo case is the example of that being bypassed. Rule text details (transistor-count threshold etc.) are not in our files; read the Federal Register notice.代工厂规则（90 FR 5298，2025 年 1 月 16 日）：对制造先进芯片的代工厂和芯片封装商设定尽职调查义务，并设"经批准的 IC 设计方"资格；经批准设计方方案的截止日期为 2026 年 12 月 31 日。对中国设计厂商的影响：实际上它们必须经过审核或走经批准的渠道，才能在台积电代工先进芯片。华为的设计能否仍在那里代工：华为早已列入实体清单，因此台积电不能合法为其制造；Sophgo 案正是绕开这一限制的例子。规则文本细节（晶体管数量阈值等）不在我们的资料中；请阅读《联邦公报》通告。

Honesty marks: "BIS alleged" is an overstatement: the claim is Reuters unnamed-source reporting. No outcome found. Rule details beyond the headline are a gap.诚实标注："BIS 指控"的说法言过其实：该主张来自路透社未具名消息来源的报道。未发现结果。标题之外的规则细节是一处空白。

G

Enforcement and its outcomes执法及其结果

The main US cases since 2022: charges, dollar amounts, sentences, Entity List additions; Singapore / Malaysia / Hong Kong cases and which law they used; how far liability reaches up a reseller chain; the 50%-ownership Affiliates Rule.

2022 年以来的主要美国案件：指控、涉案金额、量刑、实体清单新增；新加坡／马来西亚／香港案件及其所依法律；责任能向转售链上游追溯多远；50% 持股的关联方规则。

G1 Has anyone actually been caught smuggling AI chips to China, and what happened to them?真的有人因向中国走私 AI 芯片被抓吗？他们的下场如何？ basic基础

Yes. Dozens of people have been arrested or charged in the US, Singapore and Taiwan since 2025. So far the outcomes are arrests, guilty pleas and seized assets. I found no prison sentence yet.

有。自 2025 年以来，已有数十人在美国、新加坡和台湾被捕或被起诉。目前的结果是逮捕、认罪和资产查封。尚未发现任何监禁判决。

  • Guilty pleas: Alan Hao Hsu and his company Hao Global (Texas, "Operation Gatekeeper", $160M+ of H100/H200, plea 10 Oct 2025). Willy Sun (Super Micro case) reportedly pleaded guilty.认罪：Alan Hao Hsu 及其公司 Hao Global（得克萨斯州，"看门人行动"（Operation Gatekeeper），涉及 $160M+ 的 H100/H200，2025 年 10 月 10 日认罪）。Willy Sun（美超微案）据报已认罪。 press, date unverified媒体，日期未核实
  • Charged, not yet tried: ALX Solutions (Aug 2025), four people in Nov 2025 (Florida/Alabama/California), Super Micro co-founder Wally Liaw and two others (unsealed 19 Mar 2026, about $2.5B of servers; trial reportedly 2 Nov 2026), Greg Lui of Earthmade (indicted 29 Sep 2026, $300M+).已起诉、尚未开庭：ALX Solutions（2025 年 8 月）、2025 年 11 月的四人（佛罗里达／阿拉巴马／加利福尼亚）、美超微联合创始人 Wally Liaw 等三人（2026 年 3 月 19 日解封，涉及约 $2.5B 的服务器；据报 2026 年 11 月 2 日开庭）、Earthmade 的 Greg Lui（2026 年 9 月 29 日被起诉，$300M+）。 press媒体
  • Outside the US: Singapore fraud case (Feb 2025, expanded Jul 2026); Taiwan indictment of a senior Nvidia manager and 8 others (about Aug 2026).美国之外：新加坡欺诈案（2025 年 2 月，2026 年 7 月扩大）；台湾对一名英伟达高级经理及另外 8 人的起诉（约 2026 年 8 月）。
  • Money moves first: $50M+ seized in Gatekeeper, S$38M house seized in Singapore, forfeiture sought on 50 H200s, Super Micro stock down about 33% (company not charged).资金先行：看门人行动中查封 $50M+，新加坡查封一栋价值 S$38M 的住宅，对 50 块 H200 提起没收，美超微股价下跌约 33%（公司未被起诉）。
  • Gap: Hsu's sentencing was set for 18 Feb 2026. I found no report of the result.缺口：Hsu 的量刑原定于 2026 年 2 月 18 日。未发现关于结果的报道。 unknown未知

G2 What fines, prison terms or Entity List placements came out of the main cases, and how long did they take?主要案件最终带来了哪些罚款、监禁或实体清单列名？耗时多久？ detail细节

No prison term and no fine from an AI-chip diversion case so far. Maximums are large (20 years and $1M per count), but the real hits so far are seizures, frozen accounts and share-price falls.

迄今没有任何 AI 芯片转流案判处监禁或罚款。法定上限很高（每项指控最高 20 年监禁、$1M 罚款），但目前真正的打击是资产查封、账户冻结和股价下跌。

  • Statutory ceilings: ECRA criminal, willful: $1,000,000 and 20 years per violation. Smuggling count: 10 years. Money laundering: 20 years. Civil ECRA: the greater of $374,474 per violation or 2x the transaction value.法定上限：《出口管制改革法》（ECRA）刑事、故意违规：每项违规 $1,000,000 罚款、20 年监禁。走私罪：10 年。洗钱：20 年。ECRA 民事：取每项违规 $374,474 或交易额两倍中的较高者。
  • Corporate ceiling for Hao Global: twice the gross gain plus probation.Hao Global 的公司层面上限：非法所得的两倍，外加缓刑。
  • Speed: Singapore charges (Feb 2025) still had no verdict after about 20 months. Gatekeeper: plea Oct 2025, announced Dec 2025, sentencing set for Feb 2026, result not found.速度：新加坡的指控（2025 年 2 月）约 20 个月后仍无判决。看门人行动：2025 年 10 月认罪，2025 年 12 月公布，量刑定于 2026 年 2 月，结果未查到。
  • Entity List: the March 2025 batch hit Chinese AI/supercomputing buyers (Inspur subsidiaries and others), not diverters. The cases desk found no diverter listed.实体清单：2025 年 3 月那一批针对的是中国 AI／超算买家（浪潮子公司等），而非转流者。案例台未发现有转流者被列名。
  • No BIS administrative fine against a company for AI-chip exports was found.未发现 BIS 因 AI 芯片出口对任何公司处以行政罚款。

G3 Were any Singapore, Malaysia or Hong Kong traders charged, and what exactly did they do wrong?新加坡、马来西亚或香港的贸易商有被起诉的吗？他们究竟做错了什么？ detail细节

Singapore yes, Malaysia and Hong Kong no (none found). Singapore charged people with fraud, not export-control violations: they lied to Dell and Super Micro about who the servers were for.

新加坡有，马来西亚和香港没有（未发现）。新加坡以欺诈罪而非出口管制违规起诉相关人员：他们就服务器的最终用户向戴尔和美超微撒谎。

  • Singapore: Aaron Woon, Alan Wei (Aperia Group CEO), Li Ming and Jenny Lim, charged Feb 2025. Four companies were added in Jul 2026. Alleged: false end-user statements on servers carrying Nvidia chips, about US$390M, then routed to Malaysia. Charges: fraud, money laundering, and Computer Misuse Act (Li). Wei pleaded not guilty to 11 charges (6 Jul 2026). A S$38M bungalow was seized. No verdict.新加坡：Aaron Woon、Alan Wei（Aperia Group CEO）、Li Ming 和 Jenny Lim，于 2025 年 2 月被起诉。2026 年 7 月又追加四家公司。指控内容：就搭载英伟达芯片的服务器作出虚假最终用户声明，涉及约 US$390M，随后转运至马来西亚。罪名：欺诈、洗钱，以及《滥用电脑法》（针对 Li）。Wei 对 11 项指控表示不认罪（2026 年 7 月 6 日）。一栋价值 S$38M 的别墅被查封。尚无判决。
  • Why fraud: the Singapore Strategic Goods (Control) Act did not need to be used; Singapore says it will not enforce foreign regimes, but fraud on a company is a Singapore crime.为何以欺诈定罪：无需动用新加坡《战略物资（管制）法》；新加坡表示不会执行外国的管制制度，但对公司实施欺诈本身是新加坡的犯罪。
  • Megaspeed (Singapore; Malaysian unit Speedmatrix): reported under US and Singapore investigation for possible remote service of Chinese clients. No charge, fine or listing found; the firm denies wrongdoing.Megaspeed（新加坡；马来西亚子公司 Speedmatrix）：据报因可能为中国客户提供远程服务而受美国和新加坡调查。未发现起诉、罚款或列名；该公司否认有不当行为。 press媒体
  • Malaysia, Hong Kong, Japan, Thailand: no prosecutions found. Malaysia and Thailand appear as transit points in US indictments, the people charged were in the US.马来西亚、香港、日本、泰国：未发现起诉。马来西亚和泰国在美国起诉书中作为中转点出现，被起诉者身处美国。
  • Taiwan (not asked, but same pattern): forgery and breach of trust, not an export statute.台湾（问题未涉及，但模式相同）：以伪造文书和背信罪论处，而非出口管制法。

G4 When a reseller three steps down the chain diverts chips, how far up does liability reach? What did the cases say about the original seller?当链条下游第三级的转售商转流芯片时，责任能上溯到多高？这些案件对最初的卖方有何说法？ expert专家

The law can reach up the chain, but in every case I found, prosecutors charged the people who handled or lied about the shipment, not the chipmaker or the big server brands. Those brands were treated as deceived parties or left uncharged.

法律可以上溯整条链条，但在我找到的每一个案件中，检方起诉的都是经手货物或就货物撒谎的人，而非芯片制造商或大型服务器品牌。这些品牌被视为受骗方，或未被起诉。

  • Three legal levels. Civil ECRA liability is strict: intent is not needed for the person who exports or reexports without a license. Knowledge ("knowing or reason to know") is needed for the "dealing" offense in 764.2(e). Criminal liability needs willfulness.三个法律层级。ECRA 民事责任是严格责任：对于无许可证即出口或再出口的人，不需要主观故意。764.2(e) 的"经手"罪名则需要"明知或有理由知道"。刑事责任需要故意（willfulness）。
  • So an upstream seller is exposed when it knew, or ignored red flags. BIS says ignoring a red flag can be evidence of "reason to know".因此，上游卖方在明知或无视危险信号时即面临风险。BIS 表示，无视危险信号可作为"有理由知道"的证据。
  • In practice: Dell and Super Micro were the deceived parties in the Singapore case. In the US Super Micro case the co-founder and two others were charged, but Super Micro itself was not (its stock fell about 33%). Nvidia was not charged anywhere; in Taiwan one of its managers was indicted personally.实践中：在新加坡案中，戴尔和美超微是受骗方。在美国的美超微案中，联合创始人等三人被起诉，但美超微公司本身未被起诉（其股价下跌约 33%）。英伟达在任何地方都未被起诉；在台湾，其一名经理被个人起诉。
  • No court has yet ruled on how far "reason to know" reaches up a chain in a chip case. The cases are not yet tried.尚无法院就芯片案件中"有理由知道"能上溯至链条多高作出裁决。这些案件尚未开庭。 unsettled未定
Sources来源5
  • BIS, counter-diversion industry guidance · 13 May 2025 primary原始文献
  • Arnold & Porter · May 2025 law firm律所
  • BIS, EAR Part 764
  • CNBC · 19 Mar 2026 press媒体
  • Notebookcheck, Taiwan Nvidia manager press媒体
G5 What does a voluntary self-disclosure to BIS look like, and how much does it reduce penalties in real cases?向 BIS 作自愿披露是怎样的流程？在实际案件中它能减轻多少处罚？ expert专家

It is a written report to BIS's enforcement office before BIS finds the violation. It cuts the penalty a lot on paper, and BIS now treats a deliberate failure to disclose as a reason to charge more. I found no AI-chip case where a disclosure and its discount were public.

它是在 BIS 发现违规之前，向其执法办公室提交的书面报告。纸面上它能大幅减轻处罚；而 BIS 如今将故意不披露视为加重处罚的理由。我未发现任何 AI 芯片案件公开过自愿披露及其折减幅度。

  • Mechanism: the process sits in EAR 764.5 (my knowledge, not re-read in this pass).机制：该流程规定于 EAR 764.5（凭作者既有知识，本轮未重新查阅）。
  • Policy shift: an April 2023 BIS memo made deliberate non-disclosure of significant violations an aggravating factor; Seagate's $300M penalty (Apr 2023) came a day later. Law-firm view: non-disclosure sized the Seagate penalty up. That is the firms' reading, not a BIS statement.政策转变：2023 年 4 月的一份 BIS 备忘录将故意不披露重大违规列为加重情节；一天之后便是 Seagate 的 $300M 处罚（2023 年 4 月）。律所观点：不披露放大了 Seagate 的处罚金额。这是律所的解读，而非 BIS 的声明。
  • Real-case evidence: Cadence got only partial cooperation credit because it did not proactively disclose key communications or make China-based staff available.真实案例证据：Cadence 仅获得部分配合减免，因为它未主动披露关键通信，也未安排在华员工接受问询。
  • Size of discount: from memory, the BIS penalty guidelines set the base for a non-egregious voluntary disclosure at half the transaction value (capped per violation), against the standard schedule otherwise. Verify against Supplement 1 to Part 766 before relying on it.折减幅度：凭记忆，BIS 的处罚指南将非严重情形下自愿披露的基准定为交易额的一半（设每项违规上限），否则适用标准处罚表。在依赖此说法前，请对照《第 766 部分附录 1》核实。 unverified未核实
Sources来源3
  • BIS, Seagate $300M · Apr 2023
  • Paul Weiss, Seagate & VSD policy · 2023 law firm律所
  • Data Center Dynamics, Cadence plea · Jul 2025 press媒体
G6 What were the sentences and fines in the major chip smuggling cases?各大芯片走私案的判决和罚款是什么？ detail细节

None yet, by what I could find. Every AI-chip case is at plea or pre-trial stage. The big fines so far are in chip-equipment and software cases, not GPU smuggling.

据我所查，尚无。每一起 AI 芯片案都处于认罪或审前阶段。迄今的大额罚款出现在芯片设备和软件案件中，而非 GPU 走私案。

G7 What penalties has BIS imposed on toolmakers or EDA firms, and what was the legal basis?BIS 对设备制造商或 EDA 公司处以过哪些处罚？法律依据是什么？ expert专家

Three big ones: Seagate $300M (2023, hard drives to Huawei), Cadence about $140M (2025, chip-design software to a Chinese military university), Applied Materials $252.5M (Feb 2026, chip equipment). All rest on ECRA and the EAR.

三起大案：Seagate $300M（2023 年，向华为出售硬盘），Cadence 约 $140M（2025 年，向一所中国军事院校出售芯片设计软件），应用材料（Applied Materials）$252.5M（2026 年 2 月，芯片设备）。三者均依据 ECRA 和 EAR。

  • Seagate: about 7.4M foreign-made hard drives (about $1.1B) sent to Huawei. The legal hook was the foreign direct product rule on Huawei. Admitted conduct, multi-year audit, five-year suspended denial order.Seagate：约 740 万块境外制造的硬盘（约 $1.1B）售予华为。法律抓手是针对华为的外国直接产品规则。承认行为、多年审计、五年暂缓执行的拒绝令。
  • Cadence: pleaded guilty to one conspiracy count; paid more than $140M across DOJ and BIS (about $118M criminal; BIS civil over $95M; the sum is partly overlapping, so "about $140M total" is the headline). It sold design tools at least 56 times to NUDT's alias between 2015 and 2020 (sources vary), after NUDT was on the Entity List in Feb 2015. Plea needed court approval; three years' probation expected.Cadence：就一项共谋罪认罪；在 DOJ 和 BIS 两头合计支付逾 $140M（刑事约 $118M；BIS 民事逾 $95M；两者部分重叠，故以"合计约 $140M"为口径）。它在 2015 至 2020 年间至少 56 次（各来源数字不一）向 NUDT 的化名出售设计工具，而 NUDT 早在 2015 年 2 月已列入实体清单。认罪尚需法院批准；预计三年缓刑。 press媒体
  • Applied Materials: $252.5M, twice the transaction value (12 Feb 2026), for unlicensed exports of semiconductor equipment (BIS press release; details not read in full).应用材料：$252.5M，为交易额的两倍（2026 年 2 月 12 日），因无许可证出口半导体设备（BIS 新闻稿；细节未完整阅读）。
  • Tool/EDA cases show BIS's top tool: a civil penalty of 2x the deal value.设备／EDA 案件显示出 BIS 的最重手段：按交易额两倍计的民事处罚。
Sources来源3
  • BIS, Seagate
  • Data Center Dynamics, Cadence press媒体 · Verdict, Cadence press媒体
  • BIS, Applied Materials · 12 Feb 2026
G8 Have any companies, not only individuals, been charged in chip smuggling cases?芯片走私案中，除个人外，是否有公司被起诉？ detail细节

Yes, small ones. Hao Global, ALX Solutions, Earthmade and Janford Realtor in the US, and four companies in Singapore. No large chipmaker or server brand has been charged.

有，都是小公司。美国有 Hao Global、ALX Solutions、Earthmade 和 Janford Realtor，新加坡有四家公司。没有任何大型芯片制造商或服务器品牌被起诉。

Hao Global pleaded guilty (fine cap 2x gross gain). ALX Solutions and Earthmade Computer are charged. Janford Realtor is a named front company. Singapore added four companies in Jul 2026. Super Micro, Dell and Nvidia: not charged. Cadence (EDA, G7) is the large-company example, but it is a software case.

Hao Global 已认罪（罚款上限为非法所得两倍）。ALX Solutions 和 Earthmade Computer 被起诉。Janford Realtor 是被点名的空壳公司。新加坡于 2026 年 7 月追加四家公司。美超微、戴尔和英伟达：未被起诉。Cadence（EDA，见 G7）是大公司的例子，但那是一起软件案件。

G9 What are the main US enforcement actions on AI-chip diversion since 2023 and their outcomes?2023 年以来美国在 AI 芯片转流上的主要执法行动及其结果是什么？ basic基础

See G1 and G6. In one line: Gatekeeper (plea), Super Micro co-founder case (trial reportedly 2 Nov 2026), Earthmade (indicted), Nov 2025 four-person case (indicted), ALX (charged), and Entity List additions aimed at Chinese buyers.

见 G1 和 G6。一句话：看门人行动（已认罪）、美超微联合创始人案（据报 2026 年 11 月 2 日开庭）、Earthmade（已起诉）、2025 年 11 月的四人案（已起诉）、ALX（被起诉），以及针对中国买家的实体清单新增。

Before 2025 the record is thin: BIS equipment and software penalties (G7) were the large actions. The first public GPU-diversion arrests were in Aug 2025. Entity List: Dec 2024 added 140 entities; Mar 2025 added Inspur subsidiaries and others.

2025 年之前记录很少：BIS 对设备和软件的处罚（见 G7）是当时的大动作。首批公开的 GPU 转流逮捕发生在 2025 年 8 月。实体清单：2024 年 12 月新增 140 家实体；2025 年 3 月新增浪潮子公司等。

G10 The 2025 DOJ cases: exact charges, dollar amounts, sentences?2025 年 DOJ 的那些案件：具体罪名、金额、判决各是什么？ detail细节

Charges were conspiracy and violations of ECRA, smuggling, and money laundering. Amounts: $160M (Gatekeeper), "21+ exports" (ALX), unspecified in Nov 2025 case. Sentences: none found.

罪名为共谋、违反 ECRA、走私和洗钱。金额：$160M（看门人行动）、"21 次以上出口"（ALX）、2025 年 11 月案件未具体说明。判决：未发现。

  • Gatekeeper: Hsu pleaded to smuggling and unlawful export; $160M+ of H100/H200; $50M+ in PRC wires; $50M+ GPUs and cash seized. Yuan: conspiracy to violate ECRA. Gong: conspiracy to smuggle.看门人行动：Hsu 就走私和非法出口认罪；涉及 $160M+ 的 H100/H200；$50M+ 来自中国的电汇；查封 $50M+ 的 GPU 和现金。Yuan：共谋违反 ECRA。Gong：共谋走私。
  • ALX: ECRA violations, 21+ unlicensed exports of H100 and RTX 4090 via Singapore/Malaysia, Oct 2022 to Jul 2025.ALX：违反 ECRA，经新加坡／马来西亚进行 21 次以上无许可证出口 H100 和 RTX 4090，时间为 2022 年 10 月至 2025 年 7 月。
  • Nov 2025: Ho, Raymond, Li, Chen and front company Janford Realtor: ECRA conspiracy, smuggling, money laundering; A100/H100/H200 via Malaysia/Thailand; forfeiture sought on 50 H200s.2025 年 11 月：Ho、Raymond、Li、Chen 及空壳公司 Janford Realtor：共谋违反 ECRA、走私、洗钱；经马来西亚／泰国运送 A100/H100/H200；对 50 块 H200 提起没收。
  • Your question names "Seagate-style civil penalties": Seagate was 2023 and is a BIS civil case (G7), not a 2025 DOJ chip case.问题中提到的"Seagate 式民事处罚"：Seagate 案发生在 2023 年，属于 BIS 的民事案件（见 G7），并非 2025 年 DOJ 的芯片案件。
Sources来源3
  • Cases desk, rows 1–5 (project research files)案例台，第 1–5 行（本站研究文件）
  • DOJ · 8 Dec 2025
  • DOJ · 20 Nov 2025
G11 What evidence did DOJ use to prove knowledge? What should a distributor's compliance file look like?DOJ 用哪些证据来证明"明知"？分销商的合规档案应当是什么样子？ expert专家

The public documents I have allege false end-user statements, front companies, and wire payments from China. I did not find chat-log or freight-document detail in the sources. A good file answers the red flags BIS lists.

我掌握的公开文件指控存在虚假最终用户声明、空壳公司以及来自中国的电汇付款。我未在来源中找到聊天记录或货运单据的细节。一份好的档案应当回应 BIS 列出的各项危险信号。

  • Alleged in public sources: false statements to Dell and Super Micro about end users (Singapore); front company Janford Realtor; $50M+ PRC wires (Gatekeeper); buying for a non-China front company while a Chinese parent used the chips (Earthmade). The evidence itself (chats, freight papers) is not in the sources.公开来源中的指控：就最终用户向戴尔和美超微作虚假陈述（新加坡）；空壳公司 Janford Realtor；$50M+ 来自中国的电汇（看门人行动）；以非中国的空壳公司名义采购，而由中国母公司使用这些芯片（Earthmade）。证据本身（聊天记录、货运单据）不在来源之中。 (unknown; court filings not read)（未知；未查阅法院文件）
  • BIS red flags (13 May 2025): a customer that never bought advanced chips before, sudden order spikes, residential addresses, data-center infrastructure that does not fit the order, little or no online presence, reluctance to answer questions.BIS 的危险信号（2025 年 5 月 13 日）：此前从未购买过先进芯片的客户、订单量突然激增、住宅地址、与订单不匹配的数据中心基础设施、几乎没有网络踪迹、不愿回答问题。
  • Due diligence BIS suggests: check incorporation date, ownership structure, stated end use; check links to D:5 countries; for cloud providers, confirm users are not China-headquartered.BIS 建议的尽职调查：核查注册成立日期、股权结构、声称的最终用途；核查与 D:5 组国家的关联；对云服务商，确认其用户的总部不在中国。
  • A file that answers wilful blindness: KYC record per customer, ownership chart to the ultimate parent, signed end-use statement, site visit or proof of data-center capacity, screening against Entity List, written reasons for each cleared red flag, and a record of orders refused.一份能回应"故意视而不见"的档案：每位客户的 KYC 记录、追溯至最终母公司的股权图、已签署的最终用途声明、实地走访或数据中心产能证明、对照实体清单的筛查、对每个已排除的危险信号的书面说明，以及已拒绝订单的记录。 (inference from the guidance)（基于该指南的推断）

G12 Which companies were added to the Entity List for AI-chip reasons in 2024–2026?2024 至 2026 年间，哪些公司因 AI 芯片原因被列入实体清单？ detail细节

Confirmed from BIS: 140 additions in Dec 2024, and the March 2025 batch (six Inspur subsidiaries plus 11 China and one Taiwan entities). Later additions rest on secondary sources, so I list none. Whether any are your clients' customers is not something I can know.

经 BIS 确认：2024 年 12 月新增 140 家，以及 2025 年 3 月那一批（六家浪潮子公司，加上 11 家中国和一家台湾实体）。更晚的新增仅见于二手来源，故不予列出。它们是否是贵方客户的客户，我无从得知。

Dec 2024 additions came with the HBM control and 24 tool types. March 2025 listings were for AI and supercomputer development for Chinese end users; no diversion finding. A later listing named Sophgo only in secondary sources unverified未核实. Practical step: screen every counterparty against the Consolidated Screening List, and (if the Affiliates Rule returns, G14) against anything 50% owned by a listed party.

2024 年 12 月的新增随 HBM 管制和 24 类设备一同出台。2025 年 3 月的列名针对的是面向中国最终用户的 AI 与超算研发；无转流认定。更晚的一次列名点到 Sophgo，但仅见于二手来源 unverified未核实。实务做法：对照综合筛查清单（Consolidated Screening List）筛查每一个交易对手，并（若关联方规则恢复，见 G14）对任何被列名方持股 50% 的实体进行筛查。

G13 Has any non-US company been penalised under 764.2(e) for causing or aiding a violation without ever touching US soil? What was the theory?有没有非美国公司在从未踏足美国的情况下，因依 764.2(e)"促成或协助"违规而受罚？其法律理论是什么？ expert专家

I found none in AI-chip cases. In older BIS cases, foreign firms were charged under 764.2(e) for dealing in US-origin items. The reach rests on the item being "subject to the EAR", not on where the company sits.

在 AI 芯片案件中我未找到。在更早的 BIS 案件中，外国公司曾因处理美国原产物项而依 764.2(e) 被起诉。其管辖触及的依据是物项"受 EAR 管辖"，而非公司所在地。

  • 764.2(e) bars handling (ordering, transporting, financing, forwarding) an item subject to the EAR with knowledge a violation has occurred or is about to occur.764.2(e) 禁止在明知违规已发生或即将发生的情况下，处理（订购、运输、融资、转运）受 EAR 管辖的物项。
  • Old examples: Streit Group FZE and Streit Middle East (UAE) and Avnet Asia, charged under 764.2(e); Elettronica Aster (Italy) settled with a 764.2(e) count. All involved US-origin goods.旧例：Streit Group FZE 与 Streit Middle East（阿联酋）及 Avnet Asia，依 764.2(e) 被起诉；Elettronica Aster（意大利）就一项 764.2(e) 指控达成和解。均涉及美国原产货物。
  • For chips: a Taiwan-made chip made with US tools can be subject to the EAR through the foreign direct product rule (see section A of this site). That is the likely theory for a Singapore reseller. Not tested in a published chip case.就芯片而言：用美国工具制造的台湾产芯片，可通过外国直接产品规则受 EAR 管辖（见本站 A 部分）。这可能正是适用于新加坡转售商的法律理论。尚未在任何公开的芯片案件中得到检验。 unsettled未定
  • 764.2(b) (causing or aiding) is used separately.764.2(b)（促成或协助）则另行适用。
Sources来源2

G14 Is the 50% Affiliates Rule in force, paused or repealed, and until when?50% 关联方规则目前是生效、暂停还是已废止？到什么时候？ expert专家

Paused, and the pause ends on 9 Nov 2026 unless BIS acts. The rule returns on 10 Nov 2026 on paper. A two-month extension to 10 Jan 2027 has been announced by the Treasury Secretary, but I found no Federal Register notice putting it into law.

已暂停，且除非 BIS 另有动作，暂停将于 2026 年 11 月 9 日结束。纸面上该规则将于 2026 年 11 月 10 日恢复。财政部长已宣布延期两个月至 2027 年 1 月 10 日，但我未找到将其写入法律的《联邦公报》通知。

  • 29 Sep 2025: rule issued (90 FR 47201): Entity List limits extend to companies 50% or more owned by listed parties.2025 年 9 月 29 日：规则发布（90 FR 47201）：实体清单的限制扩大至被列名方持股 50% 或以上的公司。
  • 10 Nov 2025: one-year suspension (90 FR 50857) after the Trump-Xi deal. Runs to 9 Nov 2026.2025 年 11 月 10 日：特朗普—习近平达成协议后，暂停一年（90 FR 50857）。持续至 2026 年 11 月 9 日。
  • 23 Sep 2026: Bessent said the suspension would be extended through 10 Jan 2027. A National Law Review piece of 2 Oct 2026 says the same. Our legal desk found no FR extension as of 27 Oct. Check the Federal Register before 10 Nov.2026 年 9 月 23 日：贝森特（Bessent）表示暂停将延长至 2027 年 1 月 10 日。《National Law Review》2026 年 10 月 2 日的一篇文章亦如此说。截至 10 月 27 日，我方法律台未发现《联邦公报》的延期通知。请在 11 月 10 日前查阅《联邦公报》。
  • Caveat: BIS can end the pause early; it depends on the US-China framework agreement staying in good standing.注意：BIS 可提前结束暂停；这取决于美中框架协议能否维持良好状态。
  • Not repealed.并未废止。

G15 Does the Affiliates Rule reach my overseas subsidiary? How is ownership counted for a joint venture? What is Nvidia's and Chinese suppliers' exposure if it returns?关联方规则会触及我的海外子公司吗？合资企业的持股如何计算？若规则恢复，英伟达和中国供应商的风险敞口是什么？ expert专家

It reaches your subsidiary only if listed parties own 50% or more of it, or if your subsidiary sells to such a company. Ownership of listed parties is added together, so a joint venture split among several listed owners can cross the line.

只有当被列名方持有你的子公司 50% 或以上股权，或你的子公司向此类公司销售时，规则才会触及它。被列名方的持股是合并计算的，因此由多个被列名方分持的合资企业可能越过这条线。

  • Test is ownership, not control.判定标准是所有权，而非控制权。 (From the rule's published summaries; I did not re-read the FR text in this pass. Check 90 FR 47201 for the counting rules.)（依据该规则已公布的摘要；本轮我未重新查阅《联邦公报》原文。计数规则请查 90 FR 47201。）
  • If it returns: any item subject to the EAR going to a 50%-owned affiliate of a listed entity needs a license, like the parent. Your screening has to go one step further down ownership charts (this is the added step in the legal desk's lawful routes, route B).若规则恢复：任何受 EAR 管辖的物项，运往被列名实体持股 50% 的关联企业，都像运往母公司一样需要许可证。你的筛查必须沿股权图再往下一层（这正是法律台"合法路径"路线 B 中新增的步骤）。
  • Exposure for Nvidia and for Chinese suppliers: not quantified in any source I read. The rule hits Chinese groups with many subsidiaries hardest.英伟达和中国供应商的风险敞口：我读到的任何来源都未量化。该规则对拥有众多子公司的中国集团打击最重。 unknown未知
Sources来源2
  • FR 2025-19001
  • Legal desk, lawful routes — route B (project research files)法律台，合法路径——路线 B（本站研究文件）
G16 If a licensed H20 export is lawful but the end user later offers compute to a sanctioned entity, does 744.11 / 744.6 liability reach the exporter, the distributor, or only the end user?如果一笔持证 H20 出口本身合法，但最终用户此后向受制裁实体提供算力，744.11 / 744.6 的责任会触及出口商、分销商，还是只触及最终用户？ expert专家

Mainly the end user, who acts. The exporter and distributor are reached only if they knew, or if the license had a condition the end user broke. This is my reading of the structure, not a ruling.

主要是实施该行为的最终用户。只有当出口商和分销商明知，或许可证附有被最终用户违反的条件时，责任才会触及他们。这是我对法条结构的解读，而非裁决。

  • Entity List and military end-user rules (744.11 and the military rules) restrict exports, reexports and transfers to a listed party. Offering compute to such a party is not itself a transfer of the chip.实体清单和军事最终用户规则（744.11 及军事相关规则）限制向被列名方出口、再出口和转让。向此类方提供算力本身并不构成芯片的转让。
  • The H200 conditions in the January 2026 rule bar military and Entity List users, including through remote access. Breaking a license condition can bring the license holder into the case. H20 license terms are not public.2026 年 1 月规则中的 H200 条件禁止军事和实体清单用户使用，包括通过远程访问。违反许可证条件可使持证人被牵入案中。H20 的许可证条款未公开。
  • 764.2(e) can reach an exporter or distributor who learns of a planned violation and carries on.764.2(e) 可触及那些已获悉拟议违规却仍继续行事的出口商或分销商。
  • 744.6 covers US persons' own support activities, so it reaches only US-person staff.744.6 针对美国人自身的支持活动，故仅触及美籍人员。
  • No published case on this scenario.此情形尚无公开案例。 unknown未知
Sources来源3
  • 91 FR 1684 · 15 Jan 2026
  • Legal desk, lawful routes — routes A and C (project research files)法律台，合法路径——路线 A 与 C（本站研究文件）
  • BIS, EAR Part 764
G17 What happens to a company that buys banned chips through a middleman?通过中间商购买被禁芯片的公司会有什么下场？ basic基础

The buyer, the middleman and anyone who knew can all be charged. Companies have lost the chips, had accounts frozen and faced multi-year criminal exposure. Claiming ignorance fails when red flags were present.

买家、中间商以及任何知情者都可能被起诉。有的公司已失去芯片、账户被冻结，并面临数年的刑事风险。在存在危险信号时，声称不知情是站不住脚的。

Seizures (G1), civil penalties up to 2x deal value, denial of export privileges, criminal ceilings of 20 years. A Chinese buyer can also end up on the Entity List. For the buyer in China the chips are also exposed to a presumption of denial on any later reexport.

资产查封（见 G1）、最高为交易额两倍的民事处罚、吊销出口权、最高 20 年的刑事上限。中国买家还可能被列入实体清单。对身处中国的买家而言，这些芯片在日后任何再出口时还将面临推定拒绝。

Sources来源2
  • Legal desk, rules in force — penalties table (project research files)法律台，现行规则——处罚一览（本站研究文件）
  • See G1 and G2 sources参见 G1 与 G2 的来源
H

Lawful routes to serve the demand合法供应路径

What an Asian distributor can sell without a license and the mandatory screening; cloud rental from outside China and where "export of capability" begins; training on licensed chips in a third country; the BIS license application; the 15% revenue-share; the compliance program expected.

亚洲分销商无需许可可售什么及强制尽调步骤；境外云算力租赁与"能力出口"的起点；在第三国以获许可芯片训练；BIS 许可申请；15% 收入分成；对方期待的合规体系。

H1 Is there any fully legal way for my company to make money from Chinese demand for AI compute?我的公司有没有完全合法的途径，从中国对 AI 算力的需求中赚钱？ basic基础

Yes, but the easy routes are thin and the profitable ones are mostly closed. A distributor can lawfully sell below-threshold parts and act as a named consignee or US-licensed distributor under a chipmaker's H200 license; it cannot lawfully move controlled chips into China on its own.

可以，但容易的途径很窄，赚钱的途径大多已关闭。分销商可以合法销售低于门槛的部件，并作为芯片厂商 H200 许可证下的指定收货人或美国持证分销商；但它不能自行将受管制芯片运入中国。

Five routes exist on paper. (A) US-licensed H200-class exports — but in practice only Nvidia/AMD can be the applicant, because the license conditions need the maker's own US-sales and foundry data; a third party joins only as the named Chinese end user or a US distributor under the maker's license. (B) Products below every US threshold (RTX PRO 5500, 4090D, 5090D, RTX PRO 6000D) need no US license to China — the open lane for a trader, but supply is rationed to 5–10% of orders and Beijing can block any part. (C) H20/MI308 under licenses already granted. (D) Chinese domestic chips. (E) Cloud/remote compute from a non-China-owned data center outside China. Routes into mainland China are also gated by Beijing. Reexport of controlled chips from a third country into China, and reselling controlled chips already inside China, are both presumption of denial — not lawful.

纸面上存在五条途径。(A) 美国持证的 H200 级出口——但实践中只有英伟达／AMD 能作为申请人，因为许可条件需要厂商自己的美国销售与代工数据；第三方只能作为指定的中国最终用户，或在厂商许可证下作为美国分销商加入。(B) 低于所有美国门槛的产品（RTX PRO 5500、4090D、5090D、RTX PRO 6000D）出口至中国无需美国许可证——这是贸易商的开放通道，但供货被配给到订单的 5–10%，且北京可封锁任何部件。(C) 已获发许可证下的 H20/MI308。(D) 中国国产芯片。(E) 来自中国境外、非中国所有的数据中心的云／远程算力。进入中国内地的途径同样受北京把关。将受管制芯片从第三国再出口至中国，以及转售已在中国境内的受管制芯片，两者都属推定拒绝——不合法。

Sources来源3
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • 91 FR 1684 · effective生效 · 15 Jan 2026
  • TrendForce · 5–10% fill供货 5–10% · 21 Oct 2026
H2 What lawful routes exist for a Chinese firm to get Nvidia compute — overseas subsidiaries or licensed cloud?中国公司获取英伟达算力有哪些合法途径——海外子公司，还是持证云服务？ detail细节

A China firm's own site in China can receive US-licensed H200-class chips; its overseas subsidiary has a harder path, not an easier one. Licensed cloud from a non-China-owned operator is lawful today but is the most-scrutinized route.

中国公司在中国本土的站点可以接收美国持证的 H200 级芯片；其海外子公司的路径更难，而非更易。向非中国所有的运营商租用持证云服务在今天是合法的，但属受审查最严的途径。

The "headquartered in / ultimate parent in China or Macau" test follows the company wherever it sits (§742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A), confirmed by the 31 May 2026 BIS guidance). So a Chinese firm's foreign subsidiary needs a US license in every country, with a presumption of denial even for H200-class — a worse position than the same firm's China site, which can use the case-by-case H200 route. Setting up a non-China front company and then letting the Chinese parent use the chips is a false-end-user violation (the Earthmade case, >$300M). The remaining lawful path for a Chinese user is renting compute from a non-China-owned operator abroad (route E), which current BIS practice does not treat as an export of the chips — but see H.3.

「总部位于／最终母公司位于中国或澳门」的测试无论公司身处何地都跟随该公司（§742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A)，经 2026 年 5 月 31 日 BIS 指引确认）。因此，中国公司的境外子公司在每个国家都需要美国许可证，即便是 H200 级也推定拒绝——比该公司的中国站点处境更差，后者可走逐案审查的 H200 途径。设立非中国的幌子公司、再让中国母公司使用这些芯片，属虚假最终用户违规（Earthmade 案，>$300M）。中国用户余下的合法路径，是向境外非中国所有的运营商租用算力（途径 E）；当前 BIS 实践不将其视为芯片的出口——但参见 H.3。

Sources来源4
  • BIS guidance · 31 May 2026
  • 91 FR 1684
  • Earthmade indictment · Oct 2026
  • Project research files本站研究文件
H3 What can an Asian distributor lawfully sell to a Chinese customer today without a license, and which end-use/end-user screening steps are mandatory?亚洲分销商今天在无许可证的情况下，可以合法向中国客户出售什么？哪些最终用途／最终用户审查步骤是强制性的？ basic基础

Parts that fall below every US threshold and are not named in an is-informed letter — currently RTX PRO 5500, RTX 4090D, RTX 5090D / D V2, RTX PRO 6000D. Mandatory screening: no Entity List party, no military/military-intelligence end use, and no knowledge of diversion.

低于所有美国门槛、且未被告知函点名的部件——当前为 RTX PRO 5500、RTX 4090D、RTX 5090D / D V2、RTX PRO 6000D。强制筛查：相关方不得在实体清单上、不得用于军事／军事情报最终用途、且不得知悉存在转移。

A chip that is not 3A090/4A090 and not covered by an is-informed letter needs no license to China for an ordinary commercial buyer. But four end-use/end-user checks still bind regardless of the chip: (1) no Entity List party — and, if the Affiliates (50%) Rule returns on 10 Nov 2026, no company 50%+ owned by one; (2) no military or military-intelligence end use; (3) no "knowledge or reason to know" of diversion (the red-flag duty); (4) get the maker's ECCN in writing before relying on "uncontrolled," because self-classification is the only basis otherwise. Controlled chips (H100/H200/B200 and up) cannot be sold this way. The US can add any product to an is-informed letter overnight — it did exactly that to the H20 in April 2025.

对于普通商业买家，非 3A090/4A090、且未被告知函涵盖的芯片，出口至中国无需许可证。但无论何种芯片，四项最终用途／最终用户核查仍具约束力：(1) 无实体清单相关方——且若关联方规则（50% 规则）于 2026 年 11 月 10 日回归，则不得为被列实体 50% 以上持股的公司；(2) 无军事或军事情报最终用途；(3) 不得"知悉或有理由知悉"存在转移（红旗义务）；(4) 在依赖"不受管制"之前，取得厂商书面出具的出口管制分类编码（ECCN），否则自我分类是唯一依据。受管制芯片（H100/H200/B200 及以上）不能以此方式销售。美国可在一夜之间将任何产品列入告知函——2025 年 4 月对 H20 正是如此。

  • 90 FR 47201 · Affiliates Rule关联方规则（50% 规则）
  • Project research files本站研究文件

H4 Can I sell below-threshold or China-specific models to Chinese buyers, and what paperwork do I need?我可以向中国买家出售低于门槛或中国专供型号吗？需要哪些文件？ detail细节

Below-threshold parts, yes, with screening and a written ECCN. China-specific models (H20/H200) need a BIS license that in practice only the chipmaker holds — a distributor works under the maker's license or as the named consignee.

低于门槛的部件可以，但须经筛查并取得书面 ECCN。中国专供型号（H20/H200）需要 BIS 许可证，而实践中只有芯片厂商持有——分销商在厂商许可证下运作，或作为指定收货人。

Paperwork for a below-threshold sale: the maker's written classification (ECCN), end-user and end-use screening records (Entity List / Consolidated Screening List checks, a signed end-user statement, red-flag review), and an AES export filing. For H200-class (route A) the shipment also needs the maker's BIS license, independent US-lab testing of every shipment before export, and payment of the 25% Section 232 tariff when the chip enters the US to be tested. H20/MI308 ship only under the individual licenses BIS granted the makers from Aug 2025; the license terms are not public. Reality: Beijing capped H200 approvals (ByteDance and Tencent got ~10k each by Aug 2026 against 75k licensed), and below-threshold supply is rationed to 5–10% of orders.

低于门槛销售所需文件：厂商书面分类（ECCN）、最终用户与最终用途筛查记录（实体清单／综合筛查清单核查、已签署的最终用户声明、红旗审查），以及 AES 出口申报。对 H200 级（途径 A），该批货还需厂商的 BIS 许可证、出口前对每一批货的独立美国实验室测试，以及芯片进入美国受测时缴纳的 25% 第 232 条关税。H20/MI308 仅在 BIS 自 2025 年 8 月起向厂商发放的单独许可证下出货；许可证条款未公开。现实：北京对 H200 审批设限（字节跳动和腾讯到 2026 年 8 月各获约 1 万片，而获证额度为 7.5 万片），低于门槛的供货则被配给到订单的 5–10%。

H5 Can Chinese companies legally rent GPU cloud capacity outside China, and does it matter if they train big models on it?中国公司可以在境外合法租用 GPU 云算力吗？在其上训练大模型是否会有影响？ detail细节

Yes, today — renting compute is "generally not treated as an export of the chips themselves" under current BIS practice. Training large models is allowed too, with the hard limits being who the user is (no Entity List, military-intelligence or WMD work), not how big the model is.

今天可以——在当前 BIS 实践下，租用算力"一般不被视为芯片本身的出口"。训练大模型也被允许，硬性限制在于用户是谁（不得为实体清单、军事情报或大规模杀伤性武器相关方），而非模型有多大。

Conditions on the operator: the chips were obtained lawfully (a non-China-owned operator in Singapore, Japan, or Malaysia-with-a-permit needs no US license for most chips; in the UAE only listed parties — G42, Core42, 8 US hyperscalers — get license-free chips); no service to Entity List parties; no knowing support of military-intelligence or WMD end uses, including AI training for such parties headquartered in D:5/Macau; and any license the operator holds may itself forbid China-HQ users — the H200-route licenses explicitly police remote users. This is the highest-scrutiny route: Megaspeed (data centers in Malaysia/Indonesia that "appeared to be remotely serving Chinese clients") is under US and Singapore investigation, with DOJ taking over from Commerce.

对运营商的条件：芯片须合法获得（新加坡、日本，或持许可证的马来西亚的非中国所有运营商，对多数芯片无需美国许可证；在阿联酋只有被列名方——G42、Core42、8 家美国云厂商——可获免许可证芯片）；不得向实体清单相关方提供服务；不得明知而支持军事情报或大规模杀伤性武器最终用途，包括为总部位于 D:5／澳门的此类相关方进行 AI 训练；运营商持有的任何许可证本身也可能禁止总部位于中国的用户——H200 途径的许可证明确监管远程用户。这是审查最严的途径：Megaspeed（在马来西亚／印尼的数据中心，"似乎在远程服务中国客户"）正受美国与新加坡调查，司法部已从商务部接手。

Sources来源5

  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • 91 FR 43034 · UAE阿联酋
  • Asia Times · 4 Jun 2026
  • CNBC · 10 Oct 2025
H6 Where exactly is the line between lawful cloud services to a Chinese customer and an illegal "export" of capability?向中国客户提供合法云服务，与非法"出口"算力能力之间，界线究竟在哪里？ expert专家

The line is the chip, not the computation: moving or giving someone control of a controlled chip is an export; letting them run jobs on your lawfully-held chip is not, under current practice. That is a real gap in the EAR, and Congress is trying to close it with the Remote Access Security Act (RASA).

界线在于芯片，而非计算：转移受管制芯片或将其控制权交予他人，属出口；而让对方在你合法持有的芯片上运行任务，在当前实践下则不属。这是《出口管理条例》（EAR）中一个真实的缺口，国会正试图以《远程访问安全法》（RASA）将其填补。

The EAR controls the item (the chip) and certain software/technology; IaaS/remote access to compute is not itself a controlled export under current BIS practice, so a Chinese company renting on a non-China-owned cloud abroad is lawful today. The Jan 2025 AI Diffusion rule contained remote-access provisions, but that rule is not enforced (BIS set it aside May 2025; GAO held on 12 May 2026 that the non-enforcement is itself a CRA "rule"), so those provisions do not bind. The crossings that do turn a cloud deal into a violation: serving an Entity List party, knowingly supporting military-intelligence/WMD end uses, transferring controlled model weights to barred users, or breaching a license condition that forbids China-HQ users. RASA (H.R. 2683) passed the House 369–22 on 12 Jan 2026 and would bring remote/cloud access under control — Nvidia says it "could prohibit the provision of cloud services to any company with an ultimate parent headquartered in China." If it (or an NDAA version) passes, route E closes for China-HQ customers.

《出口管理条例》（EAR）管制物项（芯片）及某些软件／技术；在当前 BIS 实践下，对算力的 IaaS／远程访问本身不属受管制出口，因此中国公司在境外非中国所有的云上租用算力，在今天是合法的。2025 年 1 月的 AI 扩散规则包含远程访问条款，但该规则未被执行（BIS 于 2025 年 5 月将其搁置；GAO 于 2026 年 5 月 12 日裁定，这一不执行本身即构成《国会审查法》（CRA）意义上的"规则"），因此这些条款不具约束力。确实会使云交易变成违规的越界行为包括：为实体清单相关方提供服务、明知而支持军事情报／大规模杀伤性武器最终用途、向被禁用户转移受管制的模型权重，或违反禁止总部位于中国用户的许可条件。RASA（H.R. 2683）于 2026 年 1 月 12 日以 369–22 在众议院通过，将把远程／云访问纳入管制——英伟达称其"可能禁止向任何最终母公司总部位于中国的公司提供云服务"。若该法案（或其 NDAA 版本）通过，途径 E 对总部位于中国的客户关闭。

Sources来源4
  • computelaw.blog
  • GAO B-337935 · 12 May 2026
  • H.R. 2683 · passed House众议院通过 · 12 Jan 2026
  • NVDA 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026
H7 Can a Chinese customer lawfully train models on licensed chips in a third country (UAE, Singapore) if the chips never enter China, and what contract terms does BIS expect?如果芯片从不进入中国，中国客户可以在第三国（阿联酋、新加坡）用持证芯片合法训练模型吗？BIS 期望哪些合同条款？ expert专家

Training abroad can be lawful if the chips were obtained lawfully and the Chinese user is only a remote customer of a non-China-owned operator — but a China-HQ firm cannot lawfully buy or control the chips itself. Bringing the weights home is generally fine today (model weights are not broadly export-controlled now that the Diffusion rule is unenforced); the exposure is on the chips and who ran them.

若芯片系合法获得，且中国用户仅是非中国所有运营商的远程客户，境外训练可以合法——但总部位于中国的公司不能自行合法购买或控制这些芯片。将权重带回国内在今天一般没有问题（既然扩散规则未被执行，模型权重现已不受广泛出口管制）；风险在于芯片以及谁运行了它们。

Two very different situations. (1) Renting on someone else's non-China-owned cluster: lawful under route E, subject to the H.3 conditions. (2) A China-HQ firm standing up its own cluster abroad: it needs a US license in every country, presumption of denial even for H200-class — so "my own banned-chip cluster in a third country" is the unlawful version unless licensed. On weights: the Diffusion rule's model-weight control (ECCN 4E091) is not enforced, so moving trained weights into China is not itself a general EAR violation today — but if the training used chips obtained or operated in violation (e.g., a diverted H100 cluster, or service to a barred party), the underlying chip/service violation stands. Contract terms BIS expects on a licensed remote-access arrangement (from the route-A conditions): KYC procedures to prevent unauthorized remote access filed with BIS; a list of IaaS remote end users in/HQ'd in China, Macau and other named countries; no transfer of model weights to undisclosed users; no remote access for Entity List/§744.8 parties; and a description of physical site security. Audit/verification is done through the mandatory independent US-lab testing of every shipment, not a named on-site audit right.

两种截然不同的情形。(1) 在他人非中国所有的集群上租用：在途径 E 下合法，须符合 H.3 的条件。(2) 总部位于中国的公司在境外自建集群：它在每个国家都需要美国许可证，即便 H200 级也推定拒绝——因此"我自己在第三国的禁售芯片集群"是不合法的版本，除非获证。关于权重：扩散规则的模型权重管制（ECCN 4E091）未被执行，因此今天将训练好的权重移入中国，本身并不构成一般性的 EAR 违规——但若训练所用芯片系违规获得或运行（例如被转移的 H100 集群，或向被禁方提供服务），则底层的芯片／服务违规依然成立。BIS 对持证远程访问安排所期望的合同条款（源自途径 A 的条件）：向 BIS 报备的、防止未经授权远程访问的 KYC 程序；位于／总部位于中国、澳门及其他被列名国家的 IaaS 远程最终用户名单；不得向未披露用户转移模型权重；不得为实体清单／§744.8 相关方提供远程访问；以及对站点物理安全的说明。审计／核查通过对每一批货强制性的独立美国实验室测试进行，而非某项指定的现场审计权。

Sources来源4

  • 91 FR 1684
  • GAO B-337935 · weights control dormant权重管制休眠
  • 90 FR 4544 · 4E091 weights control, unenforced Diffusion rule4E091 权重管制，属未执行的扩散规则
H8 What does applying for a US export licence involve — cost, time, chance of approval?申请美国出口许可证涉及什么——成本、时间、获批几率？ detail细节

Filing is free via BIS's SNAP-R portal, but the practical cost is the compliance work plus the 25% import tariff on route-A chips. Timing is weeks-to-months; the odds depend entirely on the chip and the end user — presumption of denial for anything above the H200 line, case-by-case below it, and in practice the chipmaker, not a trader, is the applicant.

通过 BIS 的 SNAP-R 门户提交是免费的，但实际成本是合规工作加上途径 A 芯片 25% 的进口关税。时长为数周至数月；几率完全取决于芯片与最终用户——高于 H200 线的一律推定拒绝，低于则逐案审查；而实践中申请人是芯片厂商，而非贸易商。

There is no government filing fee for a BIS license (SNAP-R submission). A route-A (H200-class) application must certify all nine conditions: full US shipment numbers and specs (TPP, DRAM/interconnect bandwidth, peak power); sufficient US supply with no diversion of foundry capacity from US customers; total TPP to China/Macau ≤ 50% of the same product's TPP shipped to US end users; no military/MI/WMD or Entity List/§744.8 parties (incl. via remote access); the consignee's KYC procedures filed with BIS; a list of IaaS remote end users in named countries; (for IaaS consignees) no undisclosed weight transfer; site physical-security description; and independent US-lab testing of every shipment. Because conditions 1–3 need the maker's own data, the realistic applicant is Nvidia/AMD; Nvidia got its H200 license and AMD its MI325 license in Feb 2026. Odds: above the H200 line (Blackwell B200/B300, B30A) = presumption of denial; H200-class from the US to China/Macau = case-by-case; reexports from third countries and China-HQ entities abroad = presumption of denial. On timing, BIS's own fast-track comparator is License Exception NAC's 25-calendar-day notification clock; full license reviews typically run longer (weeks to a few months) and these are not public per-case.

BIS 许可证（SNAP-R 提交）没有政府申请费。途径 A（H200 级）申请须证明全部九项条件：完整的美国出货数量与规格（总处理性能（TPP）、DRAM／互连带宽、峰值功率）；美国供应充足且未将代工产能从美国客户处转移；向中国／澳门的总 TPP ≤ 同一产品向美国最终用户出货 TPP 的 50%；无军事／军事情报／大规模杀伤性武器或实体清单／§744.8 相关方（含通过远程访问）；向 BIS 报备收货人的 KYC 程序；位于被列名国家的 IaaS 远程最终用户名单；（对 IaaS 收货人）无未披露的权重转移；站点物理安全说明；以及对每一批货的独立美国实验室测试。由于第 1–3 项条件需要厂商自己的数据，现实中的申请人是英伟达／AMD；英伟达于 2026 年 2 月获得其 H200 许可证，AMD 获得其 MI325 许可证。几率：高于 H200 线（Blackwell B200/B300、B30A）＝推定拒绝；自美国向中国／澳门的 H200 级＝逐案审查；来自第三国的再出口及境外总部位于中国的实体＝推定拒绝。时间方面，BIS 自身的快速通道参照是许可例外 NAC 的 25 个日历日通知时钟；完整许可审查通常更久（数周至数月），且这些个案不公开。

Marked: exact BIS processing times and approval rates for these specific chips are not public.标注：这些具体芯片的 BIS 审批时长与批准率并未公开。

H9 Which License Exceptions (ACA, NAC, LPP) exist for advanced computing items, what are their notification requirements, and do they cover Macau and Hong Kong?先进计算物项有哪些许可例外（ACA、NAC、LPP）？它们的通知要求是什么？是否涵盖澳门和香港？ detail细节

NAC and ACA are codified and in force (15 CFR 740.8); LPP was created by the unenforced AI Diffusion rule and is effectively dormant. For China/Hong Kong/Macau, only NAC can apply — and it excludes data-center AI GPUs, so it does not help an H100/H200-class shipment.

NAC 与 ACA 已成文并生效（15 CFR 740.8）；LPP 由未被执行的 AI 扩散规则设立，实际上处于休眠。对中国／香港／澳门，只有 NAC 可能适用——而它排除数据中心 AI GPU，因此无助于 H100/H200 级的出货。

NAC (Notified Advanced Computing) authorizes export/reexport of 3A090/4A090 and related .z items — except 3A090.c (HBM) and except items designed/marketed for data centers meeting 3A090.a — to Macau, Country Group D:5, and D:5/Macau-HQ'd entities anywhere. It requires a 25-calendar-day prior notification in SNAP-R; if BIS does not object within 25 days, STELA issues a NAC confirmation number; BIS can convert it into a license requirement. Because it carves out data-center .a chips, NAC covers lower/consumer parts (e.g., RTX 4090-class) into China, not AI training accelerators. ACA (Advanced Computing Authorized) covers the same ECCNs to/within any destination worldwide EXCEPT Macau, D:5, and D:5/Macau-HQ'd entities — so by design ACA does not cover China, Hong Kong or Macau. LPP (Low Processing Performance) was added by the Jan 2025 AI Diffusion rule; since BIS set that rule aside in May 2025 (GAO: non-enforcement suspends the rule's license-exception eligibility), LPP is not an available route today. Hong Kong and Macau both sit in Country Group D:5, so: NAC yes (for eligible non-data-center, non-.c items), ACA no, LPP dormant. The 31 May 2026 BIS guidance confirms the exceptions listed in §740.2(a)(9)(ii) remain available where applicable.

NAC（通知先进计算，Notified Advanced Computing）授权 3A090/4A090 及相关 .z 物项的出口／再出口——但不含 3A090.c（HBM），也不含为满足 3A090.a 的数据中心设计／营销的物项——至澳门、国家组 D:5，以及任何地方总部位于 D:5／澳门的实体。它要求在 SNAP-R 中进行 25 个日历日的事先通知；若 BIS 在 25 天内不反对，STELA 会发出 NAC 确认号；BIS 可将其转为许可要求。由于它排除数据中心 .a 芯片，NAC 涵盖出口至中国的较低端／消费级部件（如 RTX 4090 级），而非 AI 训练加速器。ACA（授权先进计算，Advanced Computing Authorized）涵盖相同的 ECCN，适用于全球除澳门、D:5 及总部位于 D:5／澳门的实体之外的任何目的地——因此按设计 ACA 不涵盖中国、香港或澳门。LPP（低处理性能，Low Processing Performance）由 2025 年 1 月的 AI 扩散规则新增；由于 BIS 于 2025 年 5 月将该规则搁置（GAO：不执行即暂停该规则的许可例外适用资格），LPP 在今天不是可用途径。香港和澳门都位于国家组 D:5，因此：NAC 可用（对符合条件的非数据中心、非 .c 物项），ACA 不可用，LPP 休眠。2026 年 5 月 31 日的 BIS 指引确认，§740.2(a)(9)(ii) 列出的例外在适用情形下仍然可用。

Checked live via Exa MCP, 31 Oct 2026 (Tavily over usage limit).经 Exa MCP 实时核查，2026 年 10 月 31 日（Tavily 超出用量限额）。

  • GAO B-337935 · Diffusion exceptions suspended扩散规则例外已暂停 · 12 May 2026

H10 The 15% revenue share Nvidia/AMD pay to the US on China sales — legal basis, is it an export tax (constitutionally barred) or a license condition, and does it bind the distributor downstream?英伟达／AMD 就中国销售向美国缴纳的 15% 收入分成——法律依据何在？它是出口税（宪法禁止）还是许可条件？是否对下游分销商具有约束力？ expert专家

It was an informal license condition agreed in Aug 2025, never published as a rule, and its legality is contested — critics argue a charge on exports looks like an export tax, which the Constitution's Export Clause bars. It appears to have been overtaken by the 25% import tariff, and it does not flow down to distributors as a legal duty.

它是 2025 年 8 月商定的非正式许可条件，从未作为规则发布，其合法性存在争议——批评者认为对出口征费形似出口税，而宪法的《出口条款》予以禁止。它似乎已被 25% 的进口关税取代，且不会作为法律义务向下传导至分销商。

The 15% share on H20/MI308 China sales was agreed between the administration and Nvidia/AMD in Aug 2025 as an informal condition attached to the H20 license grants; it was never published as a Federal Register rule. The constitutional problem: Article I, §9 (the Export Clause) bars the federal government from taxing exports, so a percentage-of-export-revenue payment is legally shaky if characterized as a tax rather than a negotiated license condition — this is why it was structured as a condition, and why it is contested. In practice it looks superseded by the 25% Section 232 import tariff on the route-A H200 program (a tariff on import for testing sits on firmer constitutional ground than a levy on export), and neither Nvidia's nor AMD's latest 10-Q mentions a 15% payment. It is a chipmaker-government arrangement; it does not bind a downstream distributor as a matter of law, though a distributor could see it reflected in a maker's reseller pricing or terms.

H20/MI308 中国销售的 15% 分成，于 2025 年 8 月由政府与英伟达／AMD 商定，作为 H20 许可发放所附的非正式条件；它从未作为《联邦公报》规则发布。宪法问题：宪法第一条第 9 款（《出口条款》）禁止联邦政府对出口征税，因此若将按出口收入比例的缴付定性为税而非经协商的许可条件，则在法律上站不住脚——这正是它被构造为条件的原因，也是其受争议的原因。实践中它似乎已被途径 A 的 H200 计划 25% 第 232 条进口关税取代（对进口受测征收关税，比对出口征费在宪法上更站得住脚），而且英伟达和 AMD 最新的 10-Q 均未提及 15% 的缴付。这是芯片厂商与政府之间的安排；就法律而言，它不约束下游分销商，尽管分销商可能在厂商的经销定价或条款中看到它的体现。

Marked: legality unsettled; no court ruling; the "export tax" characterization is a legal argument, not a holding.标注：合法性未定；无法院裁决；"出口税"的定性是一项法律论点，而非判决。

Sources来源4
  • builtin.com · 15% share15% 分成
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • Proclamation 11002 (91 FR 2443)
  • NVDA 10-Q · 26 Aug 2026
H11 What compliance programme would a bank, insurer or Nvidia distributor expect me to have before they work with me?在与我合作之前，银行、保险公司或英伟达分销商会期望我具备怎样的合规方案？ expert专家

A written export-compliance program built to defeat a "willful blindness" theory: documented KYC on every buyer, Entity List / Consolidated Screening List checks, red-flag resolution, written ECCNs, end-user/end-use statements, record retention, and a voluntary-self-disclosure process if something goes wrong.

一套书面的出口合规方案，用以瓦解"故意视而不见"的指控理论：对每个买家的书面 KYC、实体清单／综合筛查清单核查、红旗处置、书面 ECCN、最终用户／最终用途声明、记录留存，以及出现问题时的自愿披露（VSD）流程。

The bar is set by what BIS/DOJ used to prove knowledge in the 2025–2026 cases — KYC red flags, freight documents, and chat logs. A compliance file that survives scrutiny should contain: (1) documented customer due diligence (beneficial-ownership, screening against the Entity List and, if the Affiliates Rule returns, ≥50%-owned affiliates); (2) end-use and end-user statements, with resolution notes for any red flag (e.g., a buyer with no data-center footprint ordering data-center GPUs, routing through Singapore/Malaysia, mismatched addresses); (3) written ECCN classifications from the maker, not self-asserted; (4) transaction records and AES filings retained (5 years); (5) a documented escalation/hold process and a voluntary self-disclosure (VSD) procedure under §764.5 — VSDs are expressly invited in BIS guidance and materially reduce penalties. Why it matters commercially: Nvidia and its authorized distributors cut off traders who fail KYC or show diversion signals, and banks/insurers price export-control risk off exactly these controls. The cases show the downside — individuals charged at up to 20 years per ECRA count, early asset seizures, and 2× transaction-value civil penalties (Applied Materials $252.5M, 12 Feb 2026).

标准由 BIS／司法部在 2025–2026 年案件中用以证明"知情"的证据所确定——KYC 红旗、货运单据和聊天记录。一份经得起审查的合规档案应包含：(1) 有书面记录的客户尽职调查（受益所有权、对实体清单的筛查，以及若关联方规则回归，对 ≥50% 持股关联企业的筛查）；(2) 最终用途与最终用户声明，并对任何红旗附处置说明（例如：无数据中心业务的买家却订购数据中心 GPU、经由新加坡／马来西亚中转、地址不一致）；(3) 厂商出具的书面 ECCN 分类，而非自我声称；(4) 交易记录与 AES 申报留存（5 年）；(5) 有书面记录的升级／暂停流程，以及依 §764.5 的自愿披露（VSD）程序——BIS 指引明确欢迎 VSD，并可实质性减轻处罚。商业上为何重要：英伟达及其授权分销商会切断未通过 KYC 或显示转移信号的贸易商，而银行／保险公司正是依据这些控制来为出口管制风险定价。案件显示了代价——个人按《出口管制改革法》（ECRA）每项指控最高 20 年监禁、早期资产扣押，以及交易额两倍的民事处罚（Applied Materials，2.525 亿美元，2026 年 2 月 12 日）。

Sources来源5

I

Nvidia's position英伟达的处境

China revenue now vs before; public statements and write-downs (the H20 charge); the revenue-share; how Nvidia and its distributors screen resellers and what gets a trader cut off; how China revenue is treated in guidance.

当前 vs 以往的中国营收；公开表态与减值（H20 计提）；收入分成；英伟达及其分销商如何筛查转售商、什么会令交易商被切断；中国营收在业绩指引中的处理。

I1 How much of Nvidia's revenue comes from China now, versus before the controls?英伟达现在来自中国的收入占比是多少？与管制前相比如何？ basic基础

China (including Hong Kong) was about 7% of Nvidia's revenue in the first half of fiscal 2027, against 15% a year earlier and about 19% in fiscal 2025. In the latest quarter it was $7.9B of $96.2B, about 8%.

在 2027 财年上半年，中国（含香港）约占英伟达收入的 7%，而一年前为 15%，2025 财年约为 19%。最近一个季度为 $96.2B 中的 $7.9B，约 8%。

I2 What is Nvidia's China data-center revenue per quarter, and how much of the fall is the April 2025 H20 ban versus Beijing's later discouragement?英伟达每季度来自中国的数据中心收入是多少？其中下滑有多少源于 2025 年 4 月的 H20 禁令，又有多少源于北京后来的劝阻？ detail细节

Nvidia does not report China data-center revenue as a line. The total China line fell from $9.7B in Q1 FY26 to about $4.0B in Q2 FY26 after the ban. The split between US ban and Beijing's pressure is not given by Nvidia.

英伟达并未将中国数据中心收入单列。禁令之后，中国总收入这一项从 FY26 第一季度的 $9.7B 降至 FY26 第二季度的约 $4.0B。美国禁令与北京施压各占多少，英伟达没有给出。

  • US side: April 2025 license requirement, $4.5B charge, $2.5B of H20 not shipped in Q1 FY26, and about $8B of H20 revenue lost from the Q2 FY26 outlook.美国一侧：2025 年 4 月的许可证要求、$4.5B 计提、FY26 第一季度未能发货的 $2.5B H20，以及 FY26 第二季度展望中损失的约 $8B H20 收入。
  • Beijing side: from 31 Jul 2025 CAC summoned Nvidia over H20 "backdoor" worries. Later H200 licenses were granted by the US, but Nvidia says the PRC government restricted those sales and it could not sell all it had licenses for. After a $0.4B H200 charge in H1 FY27, Nvidia has shipped "a fraction" of the allowed volume.北京一侧：自 2025 年 7 月 31 日起，国家网信办（CAC）就 H20 的"后门"担忧约谈英伟达。此后美国批准了 H200 的许可，但英伟达称中国政府限制了这些销售，它无法售出其持证范围内的全部产品。在 FY27 上半年计提 $0.4B H200 减值后，英伟达仅发出获准量的"一小部分"。
  • A clean split cannot be computed from public numbers.依据公开数据无法算出清晰的拆分。 unknown未知

I3 What has Nvidia said publicly about China sales, write-downs and the revenue share?关于中国销售、减值以及收入分成，英伟达公开说过什么？ detail细节

It said the US license rule forced a $4.5B write-down, that H200 sales are mostly blocked by Beijing, and that it forecasts no China data-center revenue. On the 15% revenue share it has said nothing in its 10-Q.

它表示美国的许可证规则迫使其计提 $4.5B 减值，H200 的销售大多被北京阻断，并且它预测不会有来自中国的数据中心收入。至于 15% 收入分成，它在 10-Q 中只字未提。

  • Apr 2025: told by US government a license is needed for H20. May 2025: $4.5B charge.2025 年 4 月：被美国政府告知 H20 需要许可证。2025 年 5 月：计提 $4.5B。
  • 10-Q (26 Aug 2026): US government granted licenses for "small amounts of H200 products to specific China-based customers", but "such sales were restricted by the PRC government, and we have been unable to sell all the products for which we have licenses".10-Q（2026 年 8 月 26 日）：美国政府批准了向"特定中国客户出售少量 H200 产品"的许可，但"此类销售受到中国政府限制，我们无法售出持证范围内的全部产品"。
  • The 10-Q mentions the 25% import tariff on licensed H200s, which Nvidia "has been unable to pass along", and no 15% payment. I searched the filing for it and found none.10-Q 提到对持证 H200 征收 25% 进口关税，而英伟达"一直无法转嫁"这笔关税；文中没有 15% 付款的内容。我在该申报文件中搜索过，未找到。
  • Regulators in China published a preliminary finding on 15 Sep 2025 that Nvidia broke its Mellanox approval terms (SAMR). Penalties are possible.中国监管机构于 2025 年 9 月 15 日公布初步认定，称英伟达违反了其 Mellanox 收购的批准条件（市场监管总局，SAMR）。可能面临处罚。

I4 How large was the H20 inventory write-down, and how much did it hit gross margin?H20 的存货减值有多大？对毛利率的冲击有多少？ detail细节

$4.5B in Q1 fiscal 2026. Without it the quarter's non-GAAP gross margin would have been 71.3%.

2026 财年第一季度计提 $4.5B。若无此项，该季度的非 GAAP 毛利率本应为 71.3%。

The 10-Q says total inventory provisions in H1 FY26 were $6.3B including the $4.5B H20 charge, and cut gross margin by 5.9 points over the half-year (1.1 points in Q2 FY26). Those numbers include all provisions, not only H20. The $4.5B alone was the main part. A separate H200 charge of $0.4B was booked in H1 FY27.

10-Q 称，FY26 上半年的存货计提合计 $6.3B，其中包含 $4.5B 的 H20 计提，并使半年毛利率下降 5.9 个百分点（FY26 第二季度为 1.1 个百分点）。这些数字涵盖全部计提，不仅是 H20。单单 $4.5B 便是主要部分。FY27 上半年另计提了 $0.4B 的 H200 减值。

I5 How much did the government's revenue-share arrangement cost or bring in for Nvidia?政府的收入分成安排给英伟达带来了多少成本或收入？ detail细节

Nothing public. The 15% deal of Aug 2025 reportedly never produced payments, and was replaced in Jan 2026 by a 25% import tariff on licensed H200s.

I6 How does Nvidia treat China revenue in its guidance?英伟达在其业绩指引中如何处理中国收入？ expert专家

It leaves China data-center compute revenue out entirely. The CFO said so in May and August 2026.

它完全不计入中国数据中心计算收入。首席财务官在 2026 年 5 月和 8 月都这样表示。

Q3 FY27 guidance is about $108B, plus or minus 2%, and gross margin about 74%. The CFO said it "assumes no Data Center compute revenue from China". Same exclusion in the May 2026 outlook ($91B). Not excluded: other China revenue (non-compute), which is where the $7.9B comes from. Upside is not modeled.

FY27 第三季度指引约为 $108B，上下浮动 2%，毛利率约 74%。首席财务官称其"假设没有来自中国的数据中心计算收入"。2026 年 5 月的展望（$91B）采用相同的剔除口径。未被剔除的是中国的其他收入（非计算类），这正是 $7.9B 的来源。上行空间未纳入模型。

I7 How do Nvidia and its distributors screen resellers, and what gets a trader cut off?英伟达及其分销商如何筛查转售商？什么情况会让一个贸易商被切断供货？ expert专家

Nvidia does not publish its screening rules or any cut-off case. What is public: it says it cannot control products after sale and relies on customers' compliance programs, and it helps authorities on diversion.

英伟达不公开其筛查规则，也不公开任何切断供货的案例。公开的内容是：它称售出后无法控制产品，依赖客户的合规计划，并在转流问题上协助当局。

  • 10-Q: "as we do not have physical control of our products after sale, we must also rely on the compliance programs of our customers and partners", and it "will continue to provide assistance to authorities regarding attempted diversion".10-Q："由于我们在售出后对产品没有实际控制，我们还必须依赖客户与合作伙伴的合规计划"，并"将继续就企图转流的行为向当局提供协助"。
  • No public example of Nvidia cutting off a distributor found.未发现英伟达切断某分销商的公开实例。 unknown未知
  • Nvidia's contract flow-down terms are not public.英伟达合同中的层层传递条款并不公开。 unknown未知
  • Expect, as the baseline, the BIS red flags in G11.作为基准，可参照 G11 中 BIS 列出的危险信号。

I8 What is Nvidia's current legal position in China: can it sell anything, what does it sell, what has it written off?英伟达目前在中国的法律处境如何：它能卖东西吗？卖什么？又减记了多少？ basic基础

It can sell under US license: H200-class chips shipped from the US, and parts below the license line. In practice it sells little data-center product because Beijing restricts buying. Written off: $4.5B (H20) and $0.4B (H200).

它可凭美国许可证销售：自美国发货的 H200 级芯片，以及低于许可线的产品。实际上，由于北京限制采购，它几乎卖不出数据中心产品。已减记：$4.5B（H20）和 $0.4B（H200）。

  • US side: H200-class (TPP under 21,000, DRAM bandwidth under 6,500 GB/s) case-by-case since 15 Jan 2026, 25% tariff, nine conditions. H20 needs a license. Blackwell: presumption of denial.美国一侧：H200 级（总处理性能 TPP 低于 21,000，DRAM 带宽低于 6,500 GB/s）自 2026 年 1 月 15 日起逐案审查，25% 关税，九项条件。H20 需要许可证。Blackwell：推定拒绝。
  • Beijing side: unpublished approvals steer buyers to domestic chips (see section C).北京一侧：未公开的审批将买家引向国产芯片（见 C 部分）。
  • Sales: Hopper to China under 1% of data-center revenue in Q2 FY27. Licensed buyers: about ten Chinese firms by May 2026 (Reuters, unnamed sources).销售：FY27 第二季度，对华 Hopper 产品不足数据中心收入的 1%。持证买家：截至 2026 年 5 月约十家中国企业（路透社，匿名消息源）。
  • Pending: Chinese antitrust finding on Mellanox.待决事项：中国对 Mellanox 的反垄断认定。

I9 What did Nvidia disclose in its 10-K and 10-Qs about China revenue share, the $4.5B charge and the $8B lost quarter, and what risk-factor language changed?英伟达在其 10-K 和 10-Q 中就中国收入分成、$4.5B 计提和损失 $8B 的季度披露了什么？风险因素的措辞有何变化？ detail细节

The 10-Q confirms the $4.5B and the license letters. The $8B was a forward estimate in the May 2025 press release, not a 10-Q figure. Newer language admits that Chinese restrictions, not just US ones, block sales.

10-Q 确认了 $4.5B 和许可告知函。$8B 是 2025 年 5 月新闻稿中的前瞻性估算，而非 10-Q 的数字。较新的措辞承认，阻碍销售的不只是美国的限制，还有中国的限制。

  • $8B: guidance for Q2 FY26 "reflects a loss in H20 revenue of approximately $8.0 billion" (May 2025 release).$8B：FY26 第二季度指引"反映了约 $8.0 billion 的 H20 收入损失"（2025 年 5 月新闻稿）。
  • Newer risk language: licenses exist but "such sales were restricted by the PRC government"; excess-inventory charge on H200; US bills (cloud access, location verification) could hurt; China's antitrust finding.较新的风险措辞：许可证已存在，但"此类销售受到中国政府限制"；H200 的超额存货计提；美国的多项立法（云访问、位置核验）可能造成损害；中国的反垄断认定。
  • I did not compare the 10-K risk factors across years line by line; the full-year FY26 10-K was not read.我没有逐行比较各年度 10-K 的风险因素；未阅读 FY26 全年的 10-K。 gap缺口

I10 Are the 15% payment and the UAE/Saudi licence conditions public documents, and could a distributor be bound by them as flow-down terms?15% 付款以及阿联酋／沙特的许可条件是公开文件吗？分销商会否作为层层传递条款受其约束？ expert专家

No. The 15% deal was never published, and the UAE/Saudi licence terms are not public either. Whether Nvidia's reseller agreements pass them down is not public.

不是。15% 协议从未公布，阿联酋／沙特的许可条款也不公开。英伟达的转售协议是否将其层层传递，同样不公开。

The 15% is described as an informal condition, now replaced by the tariff. What is public: the H200 conditions in the January 2026 rule (nine conditions, including the maker certifying US supply is sufficient and a US lab test), which apply to the licensed shipment. For a third-party distributor, the legal desk reads that only Nvidia or AMD can apply in practice, so a distributor joins under the maker's license. Flow-down contract terms unknown未知.

这 15% 被描述为一项非正式条件，如今已被关税取代。公开的内容是：2026 年 1 月规则中的 H200 条件（九项条件，包括制造商须证明美国本土供应充足，以及一项美国实验室测试），这些条件适用于持证的那批货物。对第三方分销商而言，法律台的解读是：实际上只有英伟达或 AMD 能提出申请，因此分销商是在制造商的许可证下加入的。层层传递的合同条款 unknown未知。

I11 What has Nvidia built into its chips (location verification, tracking) to help enforce the rules?英伟达在芯片中内置了什么（位置核验、追踪）来协助执行规则？ detail细节

An optional software service that estimates which country a chip is in. It is not mandatory, not a kill switch, and not law.

一项可选的软件服务，用于估算芯片位于哪个国家。它不是强制的，不是"关闭开关"，也不是法律。

Reuters (10 Dec 2025) reported the feature uses GPU attestation data and network delay signals, and customers choose to install it. Nvidia says it is observational only and denied any backdoor. Chinese regulators asked Nvidia for clarification. It limits use against smugglers because those users would not opt in. Mandates are only bills (Chip Security Act, section J).

路透社（2025 年 12 月 10 日）报道称，该功能使用 GPU 认证数据和网络延迟信号，由客户自行选择安装。英伟达称它仅为观测性质，并否认存在任何后门。中国监管机构已要求英伟达作出说明。它在打击走私方面作用有限，因为那些用户不会选择启用。强制要求目前仅停留在法案阶段（《芯片安全法》，见 J 部分）。

I12 Is Nvidia still allowed to sell to Chinese customers at all? Can a US chip company legally sell to Chinese customers?英伟达到底还能不能向中国客户销售？美国芯片公司能合法地向中国客户销售吗？ basic基础

Yes, under US license for H200-class and with no license for parts below the line. No for Blackwell. Lawful on the US side, and limited in practice by Beijing.

可以——H200 级需凭美国许可证，低于该线的产品无需许可证。Blackwell 不可以。在美国一侧合法，实践中则受北京限制。

见 I8。每款芯片的细节见本站 B 部分。

J

What comes next接下来会怎样

Is the window closing or reopening; pending bills (GAIN AI Act, Chip Security Act, location verification) and where they stand; the Oct 2025 Trump–Xi deal, the one-year pause, 10 Nov 2026 and 10 Jan 2027; who gains and loses if a deal brings relief; signals to watch.

窗口是在关闭还是重启；待审法案（GAIN AI 法案、芯片安全法、位置核验）及其进展；2025 年 10 月的特朗普–习会、一年暂停期、2026 年 11 月 10 日与 2027 年 1 月 10 日；若达成交易谁得谁失；应关注的信号。

J1 Is this a window about to close, or a market that will open up again?这是一扇即将关闭的窗口，还是一个会重新打开的市场？ basic基础

Both at once — the US has loosened (H200-class now sells to China case-by-case) while keeping Blackwell and reexports banned, and Congress is pushing to tighten. It is a managed, throttled window, not an open market, and it can swing on political dates. Plan for Nvidia being available in small, gated amounts, with a domestic fallback.

两者同时存在——美国既放松了（H200 级现已对中国逐案销售），又保持对 Blackwell 和再出口的禁令，同时国会在推动收紧。这是一个受管理、受节流的窗口，而非开放市场，并可能随政治日期而摆动。请按英伟达只能以少量、受闸口控制的数量供应来规划，并备有国产后备方案。

Direction of travel in 2026: the administration moved H200-class from presumption-of-denial to case-by-case (15 Jan 2026) and expanded UAE access, which is loosening; but it withdrew the broader Diffusion-replacement rule (Mar 2026), reaffirmed the China-HQ license requirement worldwide (31 May 2026), and faces a Congress with three tightening bills in play. Net read from the research: policy is a throttle, mirrored by Beijing's own throttle (case-by-case approvals, ~10k-unit mainland caps). The biggest near-term swing factor is the US–China truce (extended to 10 Jan 2027) and whether the Affiliates Rule returns on 10 Nov 2026. For a 3-year roadmap: assume Nvidia-class supply stays small and conditional (China approved only ~20k H200 of hundreds of thousands ordered; RTX PRO 5500 deliveries not before Q1 2027), and that the structural gap (~1.6M units in 2026, HBM-bound) keeps domestic chips as the volume backbone — so plan dual-track, not Nvidia-only.

2026 年的走向：政府将 H200 级从推定拒绝改为逐案审查（2026 年 1 月 15 日），并扩大阿联酋的准入，这是放松；但它撤回了更广泛的扩散规则替代草案（2026 年 3 月），在全球范围重申了"中国总部"许可要求（2026 年 5 月 31 日），并面对一个有三项收紧法案在推进的国会。研究的总体判读：政策是一个节流阀，并由北京自己的节流阀相映（逐案审批、内地约 1 万片的上限）。近期最大的摆动因素是美中休战（已延至 2027 年 1 月 10 日），以及关联方规则是否于 2026 年 11 月 10 日回归。对三年路线图：假设英伟达级供应保持少量且有条件（中国在数十万片订单中仅批准约 2 万片 H200；RTX PRO 5500 的交付不早于 2027 年第一季度），且结构性缺口（2026 年约 160 万片，受 HBM 制约）使国产芯片保持为出货主力——因此请做双轨规划，而非只靠英伟达。

Sources来源2
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • Asia Times · 29 Sep 2026
J2 What pending bills or rules could change the picture in 2027?哪些待决法案或规则可能在 2027 年改变格局？ detail细节

Four bills matter — RASA (cloud/remote access), the Chip Security Act (location verification), AI OVERWATCH (congressional review of licenses), and GAIN AI (US customers first). None is law yet; the likely vehicle is the FY2027 NDAA. BIS has no published location-verification rule; the pressure is coming from Congress.

有四项法案值得关注——RASA（云／远程访问）、《芯片安全法》（位置验证）、AI OVERWATCH（对许可证的国会审查），以及 GAIN AI（美国客户优先）。目前都尚未成为法律；可能的载体是 2027 财年 NDAA。BIS 没有已发布的位置验证规则；压力来自国会。

Status as of 31 Oct 2026:

截至 2026 年 10 月 31 日的状态：

  • RASA (H.R. 2683 / S. 3519) — passed House 369–22 on 12 Jan 2026; sits in Senate Banking. Would control remote/cloud access; closes route E for China-HQ customers.RASA（H.R. 2683 / S. 3519）——2026 年 1 月 12 日以 369–22 在众议院通过；现处参议院银行委员会。将管制远程／云访问；对总部位于中国的客户关闭途径 E。
  • Chip Security Act (H.R. 3447) — House Foreign Affairs 42–0 on 26 Mar 2026. Would require location-verification features in chips within 1 year.《芯片安全法》（H.R. 3447）——2026 年 3 月 26 日众议院外交事务委员会 42–0 通过。将要求芯片在 1 年内具备位置验证功能。
  • AI OVERWATCH Act — House Foreign Affairs 42–2 on 21 Jan 2026; Senate version 4 May 2026. Congressional review of chip licenses to adversaries.AI OVERWATCH 法案——2026 年 1 月 21 日众议院外交事务委员会 42–2 通过；参议院版本 2026 年 5 月 4 日。对向对手国发放芯片许可证进行国会审查。
  • GAIN AI Act — passed the Senate inside its NDAA in Oct 2025 but is not in the enacted law; would require US customers be served before exports.GAIN AI 法案——2025 年 10 月随 NDAA 在参议院通过，但未进入已颁布的法律；将要求在出口前先满足美国客户。
  • FY2027 NDAA (H.R. 8800 / S. 4784) — Senate cloture failed 14 Jul 2026; the Senate manager's amendment reportedly carries OVERWATCH and the Chip Security Act. This is the realistic vehicle for all of them.2027 财年 NDAA（H.R. 8800 / S. 4784）——参议院结束辩论动议（cloture）于 2026 年 7 月 14 日未获通过；据报参议院主管修正案载有 OVERWATCH 和《芯片安全法》。这是上述所有法案的现实载体。
  • On BIS itself: no published chip-location-verification or subsystem rule; the draft Diffusion-replacement rule was withdrawn from OIRA on 13 Mar 2026 with no replacement published. So near-term rule-change risk is Congress-driven, not an imminent BIS regulation.就 BIS 本身而言：没有已发布的芯片位置验证或子系统规则；扩散规则替代草案已于 2026 年 3 月 13 日从 OIRA 撤回，未发布替代规则。因此近期的规则变动风险由国会驱动，而非迫在眉睫的 BIS 规章。

J3 If Congress passes a location-verification / "chip tracking" mandate, who bears the compliance duty — chipmaker, OEM, reseller or data-center operator — and does it apply retroactively to installed fleets?如果国会通过位置验证／"芯片追踪"强制要求，谁承担合规义务——芯片厂商、OEM、分销商还是数据中心运营商？它是否追溯适用于已安装的设备群？ expert专家

As drafted, the Chip Security Act would put the design/verification duty on the chipmaker (features built into the chip within a year), with downstream reporting falling on whoever exports or operates. Retroactive reach to already-installed fleets is not specified in the bill and would be the hard-fought detail.

按草案，《芯片安全法》会将设计／验证义务置于芯片厂商（功能须在一年内内置于芯片），下游报告义务则落在出口方或运营方。对已安装设备群的追溯适用在法案中未作规定，将是激烈争夺的细节。

The Chip Security Act (H.R. 3447) frames the obligation as location-verification features in chips within 1 year — a design mandate that lands first on the chipmaker (Nvidia/AMD) and the OEM that integrates them, with export/report duties on the exporter and operator. The bill text as summarized does not resolve whether it reaches chips already deployed in the field; that question — prospective-only vs. retrofit of installed fleets — is exactly the kind of provision that gets negotiated in NDAA conference, and there is no enacted answer yet. Note the BIS side already grandfathers installed kit for a different purpose: "bona fide operators of data centers … are not required to cease the ongoing use, storage, disposal, or servicing" of chips they already hold, "until further notice" (31 May 2026) — a precedent for treating installed fleets gently, but not a ruling on a future tracking mandate.

《芯片安全法》（H.R. 3447）将义务界定为芯片在 1 年内具备位置验证功能——这一设计强制要求首先落在芯片厂商（英伟达／AMD）和集成它们的 OEM 身上，出口／报告义务则落在出口方和运营方。该法案摘要文本并未厘清它是否触及已在现场部署的芯片；这个问题——仅面向未来，还是对已安装设备群进行加装——正是会在 NDAA 协商会议上谈判的那类条款，目前尚无已颁布的答案。注意 BIS 一方出于另一目的已对存量设备作豁免："数据中心的善意运营方……无需停止"对其已持有芯片"持续的使用、存储、处置或维护"，"直至另行通知"（2026 年 5 月 31 日）——这为宽待已安装设备群提供了先例，但并非对未来追踪强制要求的裁定。

Marked: allocation of duty and retroactivity are not settled; bill not enacted.标注：义务分配与追溯适用尚未确定；法案未颁布。

Sources来源3
  • Project research files本站研究文件
