J4 What did the Oct 2025 Trump–Xi Busan deal commit on chips and rare earths, how long does the pause run, and what happens to the Affiliates Rule and port fees when it ends?2025 年 10 月特朗普—习近平釜山会晤在芯片与稀土上做了哪些承诺？暂停期有多长？到期时关联方规则和港口费会怎样？ detail细节

The truce paused the tit-for-tat — the US suspended the Affiliates (50%) Rule and China suspended its Oct 2025 rare-earth/counter-measures — and was extended to run to 10 Jan 2027. The Affiliates Rule's suspension expires on paper on 9–10 Nov 2026, and as of 31 Oct 2026 no Federal Register notice extending it has appeared.

休战暂停了针锋相对——美国暂停了关联方规则（50% 规则），中国暂停了其 2025 年 10 月的稀土／反制措施——并已延长至 2027 年 1 月 10 日。关联方规则的暂停在纸面上于 2026 年 11 月 9–10 日到期，而截至 2026 年 10 月 31 日，尚未出现延长它的《联邦公报》通知。

The Affiliates/50% Rule (90 FR 47201, 29 Sep 2025) would extend Entity List restrictions to any company 50%+ owned by a listed party. It was suspended by a one-year notice (90 FR 50857, 12 Nov 2025) running to 9 Nov 2026. On 23 Sep 2026 Treasury announced the US–China truce is extended to 10 Jan 2027, and China's own Oct 2025 suspensions run to the same 9–10 Nov 2026 date — but no FR notice extending the Affiliates suspension was in BIS's list as of 27 Oct 2026. If it snaps back: the "no Entity List party" screen every distributor runs widens overnight to cover every ≥50%-owned affiliate — a big increase in diligence burden and in exposure for anyone touching Chinese tech supply chains, and a fresh headwind for Nvidia/AMD China sales and for Chinese suppliers linked to listed parents. This is the single most important date in the next 10 days (see J.7).

关联方／50% 规则（90 FR 47201，2025 年 9 月 29 日）会将实体清单限制扩大至被列名方 50% 以上持股的任何公司。它被一份为期一年的通知（90 FR 50857，2025 年 11 月 12 日）暂停，暂停至 2026 年 11 月 9 日。2026 年 9 月 23 日，财政部宣布美中休战延长至 2027 年 1 月 10 日，中国自身 2025 年 10 月的暂停也运行至同样的 2026 年 11 月 9–10 日——但截至 2026 年 10 月 27 日，BIS 的清单中没有延长关联方暂停的《联邦公报》通知。若它回弹："无实体清单相关方"筛查将在一夜之间扩大到涵盖每个 ≥50% 持股的关联企业——对任何涉及中国科技供应链者而言，尽职调查负担和风险敞口都大幅增加，并对英伟达／AMD 的中国销售以及与被列名母公司相关的中国供应商构成新的逆风。这是未来 10 天内最重要的单一日期（见 J.7）。

Marked: extension announced in press/Treasury but not yet in the Federal Register as of 31 Oct 2026; port-fee specifics not confirmed in the research files.标注：延长已由媒体／财政部宣布，但截至 2026 年 10 月 31 日尚未进入《联邦公报》；港口费的具体细节未在研究文件中得到确认。

J5 If a US-China trade deal includes chip relief, who gains and who loses among US chip firms and Chinese buyers?如果美中贸易协议包含芯片松绑，在美国芯片企业和中国买家之间，谁获益、谁受损？ detail细节

Winners: Nvidia and AMD (recover China revenue), and Chinese hyperscalers (ByteDance, Alibaba, Tencent) who get cheaper, better compute. Losers: China's domestic chipmakers (Huawei, Cambricon) who lose captive demand and pricing power, grey-market traders who lose their premium, and US China-hawks.

赢家：英伟达和 AMD（收复中国营收），以及获得更便宜、更好算力的中国云厂商（字节跳动、阿里巴巴、腾讯）。输家：失去俘获式需求与定价权的中国国产芯片厂商（华为、寒武纪）、失去溢价的灰色市场贸易商，以及美国的对华鹰派。

Relief that lets more H200/RTX-class chips flow would restore part of Nvidia's lost China business (China-HQ revenue fell to $19.7bn in FY2026 from $25.0bn, −21%) and fill some of the order book (>2M H200 and >3M RTX PRO 5500 ordered vs. tiny deliveries). Chinese cloud/internet buyers — the big spenders, RMB 131.9bn capex in a single quarter for the big three — gain cheaper frontier-training compute. The losers are the domestic champions Beijing has been protecting: Huawei Ascend and Cambricon enjoy a seller's market today (Ascend 950PR price +33% in 2026 on scarcity); real Nvidia relief undercuts their captive demand and the "buy domestic" mandate. Grey-market resellers lose the scarcity premium (B300 servers ran RMB 4M→8M+ in six months). And US hawks lose ground politically, which is why the tightening bills (J.2) are the counter-pressure.

让更多 H200/RTX 级芯片流通的松绑，将收复英伟达部分失去的中国业务（总部位于中国的营收在 2026 财年从 250.0 亿美元降至 197.0 亿美元，−21%），并填补部分订单（已订购 >200 万片 H200 和 >300 万片 RTX PRO 5500，而交付量极小）。中国云／互联网买家——大手笔支出者，三巨头单季资本开支达 1,319 亿元人民币——获得更便宜的前沿训练算力。输家是北京一直在保护的国产龙头：华为昇腾（Ascend）和寒武纪（Cambricon）今天享受卖方市场（昇腾 950PR 因稀缺在 2026 年涨价 +33%）；英伟达真正的松绑会削弱它们的俘获式需求与"买国产"的硬性要求。灰色市场转售商失去稀缺溢价（B300 服务器半年内从 400 万元人民币涨至 800 万元以上）。美国鹰派在政治上失势，这正是收紧法案（J.2）作为反向压力的原因。

Marked: directional analysis from market data, not a forecast with a cited probability.标注：基于市场数据的方向性分析，而非带有引用概率的预测。

Sources来源2
  • Project research files本站研究文件
  • New Market Pitch · Nvidia China revenue英伟达中国营收
J6 If I invest now and the rules tighten, can I be forced to unwind deals or write off stock, and how have past rule changes treated orders already in transit?如果我现在投资而规则收紧，我会被迫解除交易或减记库存吗？过去的规则变动如何处理已在途的订单？ expert专家

Yes — stranded inventory is a proven risk, not a hypothetical. Is-informed letters take effect immediately, so a chip legal today can be unsellable tomorrow; Nvidia itself took a $4.5bn H20 write-down and couldn't sell its H20 stock. The one protection is that BIS has let operators keep using chips they already hold.

会——滞留库存是已被证实的风险，而非假设。告知函立即生效，因此今天合法的芯片明天可能无法出售；英伟达自己就计提了 45 亿美元的 H20 减值，且无法出售其 H20 库存。唯一的保护是 BIS 允许运营方继续使用其已持有的芯片。

The pattern from 2025–2026: the April 2025 H20 is-informed letter imposed a license requirement effectively overnight, and Beijing then blocked the H20 from its side — leaving Nvidia unable to sell ~500k H200 built for China and taking a $4.5bn H20-related charge and an $8bn lost quarter. That is the stranded-stock scenario for a trader: a part you stocked becomes license-required (US side) or import-blocked (China side) with little notice. Rules usually carry an effective date with limited savings for goods already exported/in transit, but is-informed letters and sudden China customs holds give little runway. The protection that does exist: BIS's 31 May 2026 guidance says bona fide data-center operators need not stop using/servicing/disposing of chips they already have "until further notice" — i.e., installed fleets are grandfathered for use, even as new purchases get blocked. Practical takeaway: size positions for the possibility that a tranche becomes unsellable, and avoid speculative inventory of parts one is-informed letter away from control (the RTX PRO 5500's unconfirmed US classification is exactly such a part).

2025–2026 年的模式：2025 年 4 月的 H20 告知函实际上在一夜之间施加了许可要求，北京随后从其一侧封锁了 H20——使英伟达无法出售为中国制造的约 50 万片 H200，并计提了 45 亿美元的 H20 相关费用、损失了 80 亿美元的季度收入。这就是贸易商的滞留库存情景：你囤积的部件在几乎没有预警的情况下变为需许可证（美国一侧）或被禁进口（中国一侧）。规则通常设有生效日期，对已出口／在途货物只有有限的宽限，但告知函和中国海关的突然扣留几乎不留缓冲。确实存在的保护：BIS 2026 年 5 月 31 日的指引称，善意的数据中心运营方"直至另行通知"无需停止使用／维护／处置其已持有的芯片——即已安装设备群在使用上获豁免，即便新采购被封锁。实务要点：按某一批货可能变得无法出售来确定仓位规模，并避免对距离受管制只差一封告知函的部件进行投机性囤积（RTX PRO 5500 尚未确认的美国分类正是这样一种部件）。

Marked: EAR savings-clause specifics for in-transit goods not separately sourced in the research files; the H20 write-down is the concrete precedent.标注：在途货物的 EAR 保留条款具体细节未在研究文件中单独取证；H20 减值是具体的先例。

J7 Which signals should I watch (BIS notices, Nvidia earnings calls, Beijing guidance) to get early warning before I commit capital?在我投入资本之前，应关注哪些信号（BIS 通知、英伟达财报电话会、北京指引）以获得预警？ expert专家

Watch the Federal Register for Affiliates-Rule and Diffusion-replacement notices, the NDAA FY2027 conference for RASA/Chip Security/OVERWATCH, Nvidia/AMD 10-Qs and earnings calls for China-revenue and write-down language, and Beijing's customs/NDRC signals on H200 approvals and the "buy domestic" mandate.

关注《联邦公报》上关联方规则与扩散规则替代的通知、2027 财年 NDAA 协商会议中的 RASA／《芯片安全法》／OVERWATCH、英伟达／AMD 的 10-Q 与财报电话会中关于中国营收与减值的措辞，以及北京海关／发改委（NDRC）在 H200 审批和"买国产"要求上的信号。

The dated watch-list from the research:

研究给出的带日期观察清单：

  • 9–10 Nov 2026 — Affiliates (50%) Rule suspension ends on paper; China's Oct 2025 suspensions end the same day. Watch the Federal Register for an extension notice (none as of 31 Oct 2026). This is the nearest cliff.2026 年 11 月 9–10 日——关联方规则（50% 规则）暂停在纸面上结束；中国 2025 年 10 月的暂停同日结束。关注《联邦公报》上的延长通知（截至 2026 年 10 月 31 日尚无）。这是最近的悬崖。
  • 15 Nov 2026 — comment period closes on China's draft AI-technology export controls (MOFCOM/MOST Ann. 48).2026 年 11 月 15 日——中国 AI 技术出口管制草案的意见征询期结束（商务部（MOFCOM）／科技部第 48 号公告）。
  • 31 Dec 2026 — foundry rule's approved-IC-designer deadline.2026 年 12 月 31 日——代工厂规则的获批 IC 设计方截止日期。
  • 10 Jan 2027 — the announced US–China truce runs to this date.2027 年 1 月 10 日——已宣布的美中休战运行至此日。
  • 6 Apr 2027 — G42/Core42's UAE license-free status lapses.2027 年 4 月 6 日——G42／Core42 的阿联酋免许可证地位失效。
  • Ongoing: BIS's Federal Register page (new is-informed letters, a Diffusion-replacement rule, any location-verification rule); the NDAA FY2027 conference text; Nvidia and AMD 10-Qs/earnings calls (China-HQ revenue share, inventory charges, risk-factor language on RASA); and Beijing's customs/NDRC guidance (H200 approval caps, state-data-center domestic-chip mandate). The classification of the RTX PRO 5500 is the specific product signal for route B.持续关注：BIS 的《联邦公报》页面（新的告知函、扩散规则替代、任何位置验证规则）；2027 财年 NDAA 协商会议文本；英伟达和 AMD 的 10-Q／财报电话会（总部位于中国的营收占比、库存计提、关于 RASA 的风险因素措辞）；以及北京海关／发改委（NDRC）的指引（H200 审批上限、国有数据中心的国产芯片要求）。RTX PRO 5500 的分类是途径 B 的具体产品信号。
